SOURCE src/forest-ordered-work.ts bytes=4324 sha256=5e2db37f9e24d4fd705adefbef5da489697f639bdd11f3f28afd66a7e8c05de4
/** Retained canonical-order work indices for patch-level forest culling.
 * A skipped patch removes all its members from the frame loop; active patch
 * members are retained as a compact mask in original selection order, so
 * capacity and hysteresis decisions keep the same deterministic order. */
export class ForestOrderedWorkIndex {
  private groups=new Map<string,number[]>();
  private cells:readonly {id:string}[]=[];
  private keys:string[]=[];
  private skipped=new Set<string>();
  private indices:number[]=[];
  private rebuildCount=0;
  private incrementalUpdateCount=0;
  private lastNewlySkipped:string[]=[];
  private keyForCell:(id:string)=>string;

  constructor(keyForCell:(id:string)=>string){
    if(typeof keyForCell!=='function')throw new Error('Forest work index requires a patch-key function');
    this.keyForCell=keyForCell;
  }

  setSelection(cells:readonly {id:string}[]){
    if(!Array.isArray(cells)||cells.some(cell=>!cell||typeof cell.id!=='string'))throw new Error('Invalid forest work selection');
    const groups=new Map<string,number[]>(),keys:string[]=[];
    for(let index=0;index<cells.length;index++){
      const key=this.keyForCell(cells[index].id);if(typeof key!=='string'||!key)throw new Error('Invalid forest work patch key');
      keys.push(key);
      const members=groups.get(key);if(members)members.push(index);else groups.set(key,[index]);
    }
    this.cells=cells;this.keys=keys;this.groups=groups;this.skipped.clear();this.lastNewlySkipped=[];
    this.indices=Array.from({length:cells.length},(_,index)=>index);this.rebuildCount++;
  }

  /** Update only when census/habitat readiness changes which patches can be
   * skipped. Returned IDs are the just-skipped cells whose retention state
   * must be cleared exactly as the former per-cell far gate did. */
  updateSkipped(next:ReadonlySet<string>){
    let changed=next.size!==this.skipped.size;
    if(!changed)for(const key of next)if(!this.skipped.has(key)){changed=true;break;}
    if(!changed){this.lastNewlySkipped=[];return this.indices;}

    const newlySkipped:string[]=[];
    for(const key of next)if(!this.skipped.has(key)){
      const members=this.groups.get(key);if(members)for(const index of members)newlySkipped.push(this.cells[index].id);
    }
    this.skipped=new Set(next);this.lastNewlySkipped=newlySkipped;
    this.indices=this.rebuildActiveIndices();this.rebuildCount++;
    return this.indices;
  }

  /** Apply changed patch membership without rescanning the entire selection.
   * The maintained array remains in canonical cell order. */
  updateSkippedPatches(changes:ReadonlyMap<string,boolean>){
    const newlySkipped:string[]=[];let changed=false;
    for(const [key,skip] of changes){
      const previous=this.skipped.has(key);if(previous===skip)continue;
      const members=this.groups.get(key);if(!members)continue;
      changed=true;
      if(skip){
        this.skipped.add(key);
        for(const index of members){
          const position=this.lowerBound(this.indices,index);
          if(this.indices[position]===index)this.indices.splice(position,1);
          newlySkipped.push(this.cells[index].id);
        }
      }else{
        this.skipped.delete(key);
        for(const index of members){
          const position=this.lowerBound(this.indices,index);
          if(this.indices[position]!==index)this.indices.splice(position,0,index);
        }
      }
    }
    this.lastNewlySkipped=newlySkipped;
    if(changed)this.incrementalUpdateCount++;
    return this.indices;
  }

  newlySkippedIds(){return this.lastNewlySkipped;}
  currentIndices(){return this.indices;}
  diagnostics(){return {selectionCells:this.cells.length,groups:this.groups.size,skippedGroups:this.skipped.size,
    skippedCells:this.cells.length-this.indices.length,activeCells:this.indices.length,rebuilds:this.rebuildCount,incrementalUpdates:this.incrementalUpdateCount};}

  private lowerBound(values:readonly number[],target:number){
    let low=0,high=values.length;while(low<high){const middle=(low+high)>>>1;if(values[middle]<target)low=middle+1;else high=middle;}return low;
  }

  private rebuildActiveIndices(){
    const ordered:number[]=[];
    for(let index=0;index<this.keys.length;index++)if(!this.skipped.has(this.keys[index]))ordered.push(index);
    return ordered;
  }
}


SOURCE src/forest-patch-broadphase.ts bytes=10647 sha256=1c3db18c9e0b85c3500954be454d86d43b402e9fc54ae303feb3aaa8091a617d
import type {PopulationCell} from './planet-population.ts';

export const FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_VERSION='forest-patch-broadphase-1';
export const FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_DISTANCE_M=650;
/** Small distance guard for floating-point origin conversion at planet scale. */
export const FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_MARGIN_M=.1;

export type ForestPatchRoot={groundHeightM:number;water:number};
export type ForestPatchBounds={min:[number,number,number];max:[number,number,number];center:[number,number,number];radiusM:number};
type Member={id:string;direction:readonly [number,number,number];root:ForestPatchRoot|null};
type Group={key:string;members:Member[];byId:Map<string,Member>;currentCount:number;bounds:ForestPatchBounds|null;boundsDirty:boolean};
export type ForestPatchBroadphaseStats={groups:number;readyGroups:number;invalidatedGroups:number;skippedGroups:number;skippedCells:number};

/** Pure grouped broadphase for PlanetForest's existing canonical cell loop.
 * The caller still iterates its original `PopulationCell[]` in order and skips
 * cells only when their patch key is in the returned set. */
export class ForestPatchBroadphase {
  private radiusM:number;
  private keyForCell:(id:string)=>string;
  private groups=new Map<string,Group>();
  private byId=new Map<string,Group>();
  private dirtyPatchKeys=new Set<string>();
  private water=NaN;
  private lastStats:ForestPatchBroadphaseStats={groups:0,readyGroups:0,invalidatedGroups:0,skippedGroups:0,skippedCells:0};
  private lastVisitedGroups=0;private totalVisitedGroups=0;private fullScans=0;private incrementalScans=0;

  constructor(radiusM:number,keyForCell:(id:string)=>string){
    if(!Number.isFinite(radiusM)||radiusM<=0)throw new Error('Broadphase radius must be finite and positive');
    if(typeof keyForCell!=='function')throw new Error('Broadphase requires the canonical forest patch-key function');
    this.radiusM=radiusM;this.keyForCell=keyForCell;
  }

  /** Replace selection atomically. Resolve only habitat records already proven
   * current by the caller; null/missing residents keep the affected patch on
   * the unchanged per-cell fallback path. */
  setSelection(cells:readonly PopulationCell[],water:number,resolve:(cell:PopulationCell)=>ForestPatchRoot|null|undefined){
    if(!Number.isFinite(water))throw new Error('Broadphase water must be finite');
    if(!Array.isArray(cells)||typeof resolve!=='function')throw new Error('Invalid broadphase selection');
    const groups=new Map<string,Group>(),byId=new Map<string,Group>();
    for(const cell of cells){
      if(!cell||typeof cell.id!=='string'||!Array.isArray(cell.direction)||cell.direction.length!==3||
        !cell.direction.every(Number.isFinite)||Math.abs(Math.hypot(...cell.direction)-1)>1e-6)throw new Error('Invalid population cell in broadphase selection');
      if(byId.has(cell.id))throw new Error('Duplicate population cell in broadphase selection');
      const key=this.keyForCell(cell.id);if(typeof key!=='string'||!key)throw new Error('Invalid census patch key');
      let group=groups.get(key);
      if(!group){group={key,members:[],byId:new Map(),currentCount:0,bounds:null,boundsDirty:false};groups.set(key,group);}
      const resolved=resolve(cell)??null;
      if(resolved&&(!Number.isFinite(resolved.groundHeightM)||!Number.isFinite(resolved.water)))throw new Error('Invalid resolved forest root');
      const root=resolved&&resolved.water===water?{groundHeightM:resolved.groundHeightM,water:resolved.water}:null;
      const member:Member={id:cell.id,direction:cell.direction,root};
      group.members.push(member);group.byId.set(cell.id,member);byId.set(cell.id,group);
      if(root){group.currentCount++;this.extendBounds(group,member);}
    }
    this.groups=groups;this.byId=byId;this.water=water;this.dirtyPatchKeys.clear();
    for(const group of groups.values())if(group.currentCount!==group.members.length)group.boundsDirty=true;
    this.lastStats={groups:groups.size,readyGroups:0,invalidatedGroups:groups.size,skippedGroups:0,skippedCells:0};
  }

  /** Call for each newly computed/replaced resident. Unknown/out-of-selection
   * IDs are harmless and return false. A changed root invalidates the bound;
   * the group cannot skip again until all members resolve and it is rebuilt. */
  updateResident(id:string,root:ForestPatchRoot|null|undefined){
    const group=this.byId.get(id),member=group?.byId.get(id);if(!group||!member)return false;
    if(root&&(!Number.isFinite(root.groundHeightM)||!Number.isFinite(root.water)))throw new Error('Invalid resolved forest root');
    const next=root&&root.water===this.water?{groundHeightM:root.groundHeightM,water:root.water}:null;
    const previous=member.root;
    const changed=!!previous!==!!next||!!previous&&!!next&&
      (previous.groundHeightM!==next.groundHeightM||previous.water!==next.water);
    if(!changed)return true;
    if(previous&&previous.water===this.water)group.currentCount--;
    if(previous&&next&&(previous.groundHeightM!==next.groundHeightM||previous.water!==next.water))group.boundsDirty=true;
    member.root=next;
    if(next){group.currentCount++;if(!group.boundsDirty)this.extendBounds(group,member);}
    this.dirtyPatchKeys.add(group.key);
    return true;
  }

  /** A water or habitat/hydrology revision invalidates every root bound. The
   * caller repopulates records through updateResident as its bounded work runs. */
  invalidate(){
    for(const group of this.groups.values()){
      group.currentCount=0;group.boundsDirty=true;
      for(const member of group.members)member.root=null;
    }
  }

  setWater(water:number){
    if(!Number.isFinite(water))throw new Error('Broadphase water must be finite');
    if(water===this.water)return;
    this.water=water;this.invalidate();
  }

  /** Pending local root changes. Callers acknowledge only after applying the
   * corresponding incremental skip states; selection/water resets are global. */
  dirtyGroups(){return [...this.dirtyPatchKeys];}
  acknowledgeDirtyGroups(keys:readonly string[]){for(const key of keys)this.dirtyPatchKeys.delete(key);}

  /** Re-evaluate only the supplied patch keys and report their new skip state.
   * A missing/incomplete group is explicitly non-skippable. */
  skipStatesFor(keys:readonly string[],eyeMeters:readonly [number,number,number],water:number,
    currentPatch:(patchKey:string)=>boolean,minimumDistanceM=FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_DISTANCE_M){
    if(!eyeMeters||eyeMeters.length!==3||!eyeMeters.every(Number.isFinite)||!Number.isFinite(water)||
      !Number.isFinite(minimumDistanceM)||minimumDistanceM<0||typeof currentPatch!=='function')throw new Error('Invalid broadphase query');
    const states=new Map<string,boolean>();
    this.lastVisitedGroups=keys.length;this.totalVisitedGroups+=keys.length;this.incrementalScans++;
    if(water!==this.water){for(const key of keys)states.set(key,false);return states;}
    const [x,y,z]=eyeMeters;
    for(const key of keys){
      const group=this.groups.get(key);
      if(!group||group.currentCount!==group.members.length){states.set(key,false);continue;}
      if(group.boundsDirty)this.rebuildBounds(group);
      const bounds=group.bounds;
      if(!bounds||!currentPatch(key)){states.set(key,false);continue;}
      const near=Math.hypot(x-bounds.center[0],y-bounds.center[1],z-bounds.center[2])-bounds.radiusM;
      states.set(key,near>=minimumDistanceM+FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_MARGIN_M);
    }
    return states;
  }

  /** Returns canonical patch keys that are safe to omit from the existing
   * ordered per-cell loop. The callback must report a complete current-family,
   * current-water census patch. A conservative AABB-derived sphere encloses
   * every resolved resident root in the selected group. */
  skipPatchKeys(eyeMeters:readonly [number,number,number],water:number,
    currentPatch:(patchKey:string)=>boolean,minimumDistanceM=FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_DISTANCE_M){
    if(!eyeMeters||eyeMeters.length!==3||!eyeMeters.every(Number.isFinite)||!Number.isFinite(water)||
      !Number.isFinite(minimumDistanceM)||minimumDistanceM<0||typeof currentPatch!=='function')throw new Error('Invalid broadphase query');
    const skipped=new Set<string>();let readyGroups=0,invalidatedGroups=0,skippedCells=0;
    this.lastVisitedGroups=water===this.water?this.groups.size:0;this.totalVisitedGroups+=this.lastVisitedGroups;this.fullScans++;
    if(water!==this.water){this.lastStats={groups:this.groups.size,readyGroups:0,invalidatedGroups:this.groups.size,skippedGroups:0,skippedCells:0};return skipped;}
    for(const group of this.groups.values()){
      if(group.currentCount!==group.members.length){invalidatedGroups++;continue;}
      if(group.boundsDirty)this.rebuildBounds(group);
      const bounds=group.bounds;if(!bounds){invalidatedGroups++;continue;}readyGroups++;
      if(!currentPatch(group.key))continue;
      const [x,y,z]=eyeMeters,near=Math.hypot(x-bounds.center[0],y-bounds.center[1],z-bounds.center[2])-bounds.radiusM;
      if(near>=minimumDistanceM+FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_MARGIN_M){skipped.add(group.key);skippedCells+=group.members.length;}
    }
    this.lastStats={groups:this.groups.size,readyGroups,invalidatedGroups,skippedGroups:skipped.size,skippedCells};
    return skipped;
  }

  patchKeyForCell(id:string){return this.byId.get(id)?.key;}
  diagnostics(){return {groups:this.groups.size,water:this.water,lastFullScan:{...this.lastStats},lastVisitedGroups:this.lastVisitedGroups,
    totalVisitedGroups:this.totalVisitedGroups,fullScans:this.fullScans,incrementalScans:this.incrementalScans};}

  private rootPosition(member:Member):[number,number,number]{
    if(!member.root)throw new Error('Cannot bound an unresolved forest root');
    const radial=this.radiusM+member.root.groundHeightM,d=member.direction;
    return [d[0]*radial,d[1]*radial,d[2]*radial];
  }
  private extendBounds(group:Group,member:Member){
    const p=this.rootPosition(member),old=group.bounds;
    if(!old){group.bounds={min:[...p],max:[...p],center:[...p],radiusM:0};return;}
    for(let axis=0;axis<3;axis++){old.min[axis]=Math.min(old.min[axis],p[axis]);old.max[axis]=Math.max(old.max[axis],p[axis]);}
    this.finishBounds(old);
  }
  private rebuildBounds(group:Group){
    group.bounds=null;
    for(const member of group.members)if(member.root)this.extendBounds(group,member);
    group.boundsDirty=false;
  }
  private finishBounds(bounds:ForestPatchBounds){
    for(let axis=0;axis<3;axis++)bounds.center[axis]=(bounds.min[axis]+bounds.max[axis])*.5;
    bounds.radiusM=Math.hypot(bounds.max[0]-bounds.min[0],bounds.max[1]-bounds.min[1],bounds.max[2]-bounds.min[2])*.5;
  }
}

import {quantizeForestCoverage} from './forest-coverage.ts';
import {type Body,type V3} from './catalog.ts';
import {terrainSampler} from './terrain.ts';
import {biomeClassifier} from './surface-biomes.ts';
import {forestPopulationCells,FAR_CROWN_PROBABILITY,type PopulationCell} from './planet-population.ts';
import {forestHabitat,TREE_HABITAT_VERSION} from './forest-habitat.ts';
import type {ForestSelectionCache} from './forest-selection-cache.ts';
import {unpackForestSelection} from './forest-selection-transfer.ts';
import {PLANET_DETAIL as B} from './planet-detail.ts';
import {InstanceUpdateRanges as DirtyRange} from './instance-update-ranges.ts';
import type {Species} from './biosphere-model.ts';
import type {TreeAssetFamily,TreeAssetOptions} from './tree-asset.ts';

import {ForestTreeFamilyLibrary,type ForestTreeFamilyInput,type ForestTreeFamilyEntry} from './forest-tree-family-library.ts';
import {ForestPatchBroadphase} from './forest-patch-broadphase.ts';
import {ForestOrderedWorkIndex} from './forest-ordered-work.ts';
import {forestPresentationFamily,packForestPresentation,FOREST_PRESENTATION_VERSION} from './forest-presentation.ts';
import type {ForestFamilyPresentation} from './forest-impostor.ts';
import type {ForestTreeKind} from './forest-tree-family-library.ts';

type Resident={cell:PopulationCell;height:number;present:boolean;water:number;patchKey?:string};
type TreeLoader=(a:ArrayBuffer,b:ArrayBuffer,options:TreeAssetOptions)=>Promise<TreeAssetFamily>;
type AssetSlot={resident:Resident;originVersion:number;catalogueVersion:number};
const loadTreeFamily:TreeLoader=async(a,b,options)=>(await import('./tree-asset.ts')).loadTreeAssetFamily(a,b,options);

type BatchUpdates={residents:Resident[];originVersions:number[];slots:Map<string,number>;wanted:Resident[];wantedCoverage:number[];wantedIds:Set<string>;matrix:DirtyRange;color:DirtyRange;coverage:DirtyRange;seed:DirtyRange};
const batchUpdates=():BatchUpdates=>({residents:[],originVersions:[],slots:new Map(),wanted:[],wantedCoverage:[],wantedIds:new Set(),matrix:new DirtyRange(),color:new DirtyRange(),coverage:new DirtyRange(),seed:new DirtyRange()});
const crownBoundRadius=Math.hypot(...LEGACY_TREE_BOUND.extent)+Math.hypot(LEGACY_TREE_BOUND.center[0],LEGACY_TREE_BOUND.center[2]);

/** Bounded placement queue + retained habitat cache. Flight and walking call
 * the same update. Work begins above visible range; no landing flag exists. */
export class PlanetForest {
  readonly group=new THREE.Group();
  private meshes:THREE.InstancedMesh[];
  private canopy:THREE.InstancedMesh;
  private cache=new Map<string,Resident>();
  private wanted:PopulationCell[]=[];
  private queue:PopulationCell[]=[];
  private center=new THREE.Vector3(Infinity,Infinity,Infinity);
  private coverage:THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute[]=[];
  private canopyCoverage!:THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute;
  private retainedNear=new Set<string>();
  private retainedMiddle=new Set<string>();
  private fullCanopy:PlanetCanopy|null=null;
  private patchBroadphase:ForestPatchBroadphase;
  private workIndex:ForestOrderedWorkIndex;
  private broadphaseSelectionReady=false;
  private workStateVersion=0;
  private workBuiltStateVersion=-1;
  // Diagnostic-only revisions distinguish why the retained far-work list is rebuilt.
  private workSelectionRevision=0;
  private workHabitatRevision=0;
  private workGlobalHabitatRevision=0;
  private workBuiltSelectionRevision=-1;
  private workBuiltHabitatRevision=-1;
  private workBuiltGlobalHabitatRevision=-1;
  private workBuiltCanopyEpoch=-2;
  private workRefreshReasons={initial:0,selection:0,habitat:0,canopy:0,movement:0,selectionMismatch:0,incrementalPatch:0};
  private workCanopyRevision=-2;
  private workReferenceEye=new THREE.Vector3();
  private workReferenceEyeReady=false;
  private workListRefreshes=0;
  private immersiveMaterial=false;
  private origin=new THREE.Vector3();
  private originReady=false;
  private originVersion=0;
  private updates:BatchUpdates[]=[batchUpdates(),batchUpdates(),batchUpdates()];
  private sample:ReturnType<typeof terrainSampler>;
  private classify:ReturnType<typeof biomeClassifier>;
  private habitat:ReturnType<typeof forestHabitat>;
  private water=NaN;
  private sampledRivers:Body['canonicalRivers'];private sampledHydrology:Body['hydrology'];
  private matrix=new THREE.Matrix4();
  private position=new THREE.Vector3();
  private scale=new THREE.Vector3();
  private quaternion=new THREE.Quaternion();
  private yaw=new THREE.Quaternion();
  private direction=new THREE.Vector3();
  private color=new THREE.Color();
  private bounds=new THREE.Sphere();
  private disposed=false;
  private catalogueError='';
  private assets:ForestTreeFamilyLibrary<Resident,AssetSlot>;
  private assetCounts=[0,0];
  private catalogue=forestTreeCatalogue([]);
  private catalogueInput:readonly Species[]|undefined;
  private catalogueVersion=0;
  private canopyPresentationKey='';
  private presentedFamilies=new Map<ForestTreeKind,ForestFamilyPresentation>();
  private bindings=new WeakMap<Resident,ForestTreeBinding|null>();
  private assetDraw=false;
  private worldDate=0n;
  private worker:Worker|null=null;
  private selecting=false;
  private selectionRequest=0;
  private selectionDirection:V3|null=null;
  private workerRestarts=0;
  private workerFailures=0;
  private selectionApplyMs=0;
  private selectionTransfer:{bytes:number;cells:number;packMs:number;decodeMs:number}|null=null;
  private selectionCacheStats:ReturnType<ForestSelectionCache['diagnostics']>|null=null;
  private applySelection(direction:V3,cells:PopulationCell[],selectionMs:number){
    const started=performance.now();
    this.center.set(...direction);this.wanted=cells;
    this.setWorkSelection(cells);
    const incoming=new Set(cells.map(c=>c.id));for(const id of this.retainedNear)if(!incoming.has(id))this.retainedNear.delete(id);
    for(const id of this.retainedMiddle)if(!incoming.has(id))this.retainedMiddle.delete(id);
    this.queue=this.wanted.filter(cell=>this.cache.get(cell.id)?.water!==this.water);
    if(this.cache.size+this.queue.length>B.populationCacheLimit){
      const keep=new Set(this.wanted.map(c=>c.id));
      for(const id of this.cache.keys())if(!keep.has(id))this.cache.delete(id);
    }
    this.selectionMs=selectionMs;this.selecting=false;this.selectionDirection=null;
    this.selectionApplyMs=performance.now()-started;
  }
  visibleTrees=0;nearTrees=0;placementMs=0;selectionMs=0;
  private matrixWrites=0;
  private colorWrites=0;
  private coverageWrites=0;
  private scheduledUploadBytes=0;
  private updateMs=0;
  private farOwnedSkipped=0;
  private farWorkDiagnostics:Record<string,unknown>={selectionCells:0,groups:0,skippedGroups:0,skippedCells:0,activeCells:0,rebuilds:0};
  private originRebased=false;
  constructor(private body:Body,private treeLoader:TreeLoader=loadTreeFamily){
    this.patchBroadphase=new ForestPatchBroadphase(body.radiusM,forestCellPatchKey);
    this.workIndex=new ForestOrderedWorkIndex(forestCellPatchKey);
    this.assets=new ForestTreeFamilyLibrary(this.group,(a,b,options)=>this.treeLoader(a,b,options),resident=>resident.cell.id,[B.forestNearCapacity,B.forestCapacity]);
    this.sample=terrainSampler(body);this.classify=biomeClassifier(body);
    this.habitat=forestHabitat(this.sample,this.classify,body.radiusM);
    this.meshes=[B.forestNearCapacity,B.forestCapacity].map((capacity,i)=>{
      const mesh=new THREE.InstancedMesh(treeGeometry(i===0?1:0),
        forestMaterial(true),capacity);
      const coverage=new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(capacity),1);coverage.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);mesh.geometry.setAttribute('instanceCoverage',coverage);this.coverage.push(coverage);
      mesh.geometry.setAttribute('instanceForestSeed',new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Uint16Array(capacity),1).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
      mesh.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      mesh.instanceColor=new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(capacity*3).fill(1),3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      mesh.frustumCulled=false;mesh.count=0;
      mesh.castShadow=true;mesh.receiveShadow=true;mesh.customDepthMaterial=forestDepthMaterial();
      this.group.add(mesh);return mesh;
    });
    this.canopy=new THREE.InstancedMesh(treeGeometry(0),forestMaterial(true),B.canopyCapacity);
    this.canopyCoverage=new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(B.canopyCapacity),1);this.canopyCoverage.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);this.canopy.geometry.setAttribute('instanceCoverage',this.canopyCoverage);
    this.canopy.geometry.setAttribute('instanceForestSeed',new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Uint16Array(B.canopyCapacity),1).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
    this.canopy.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);this.canopy.frustumCulled=false;this.canopy.count=0;
    this.canopy.instanceColor=new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(B.canopyCapacity*3).fill(1),3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
    // Initial bufferData uploads the complete allocation. Drop its consumed
    // ranges as well as later partial uploads, so a first sparse edit does not
    // resend initialization ranges. Hidden batches retain pending work.
    for(const mesh of [...this.meshes,this.canopy])for(const attribute of [
      mesh.instanceMatrix,mesh.instanceColor!,
      mesh.geometry.getAttribute('instanceCoverage') as THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute,
      mesh.geometry.getAttribute('instanceForestSeed') as THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute,
    ])attribute.onUpload(()=>attribute.clearUpdateRanges());
    this.group.add(this.canopy);this.group.name='Continuous planetary forest';
    if(typeof Worker!=='undefined'){this.fullCanopy=new PlanetCanopy(body);this.group.add(this.fullCanopy.group);this.startWorker();}
  }
  private setWorkSelection(cells:readonly PopulationCell[]){
    this.workIndex.setSelection(cells);
    this.workStateVersion++;
    this.workSelectionRevision++;
    this.broadphaseSelectionReady=Number.isFinite(this.water);
    if(!this.broadphaseSelectionReady)return;
    this.patchBroadphase.setSelection(cells,this.water,cell=>{
      const resident=this.cache.get(cell.id);
      return resident&&resident.water===this.water?{groundHeightM:resident.height,water:resident.water}:null;
    });
  }
  /** Explicit local integration seam. Loading never changes canonical cells.
   * The previous renderer remains active until both new batches are ready. */
  async loadTreeAssets(a:ArrayBuffer,b:ArrayBuffer,options:TreeAssetOptions={}){
    return this.loadTreeFamilies([{kind:'branching',lod0:a,lod1:b}],options);
  }
  loadTreeFamilies(inputs:readonly ForestTreeFamilyInput[],options:TreeAssetOptions={}){
    return this.assets.replace(inputs,options);
  }
  clearTreeAssets(){
    this.assets.clear();
    this.assetCounts=[0,0];
  }
  private detailedSamples(){
    const samples=[];
    for(const entry of this.assets.entries.values())for(let batch=0;batch<2;batch++){
      if(!this.group.visible||!entry.batches[batch].group.visible)continue;
      let count=0;
      // Diagnostics are read by the live HUD. Bound both scans and output,
      // rather than allocating/filtering every resident on every read.
      const slots=entry.pools[batch].states;
      for(let index=0;index<Math.min(slots.length,64)&&count<8;index++){
        const slot=slots[index],heightM=slot.resident.cell.heightM;
        if(heightM<10||heightM>22)continue;
        const binding=this.binding(slot.resident);if(!binding)continue;
        samples.push({batch,family:entry.kind,id:slot.resident.cell.id,speciesId:binding.recipe.speciesId,
          recipe:binding.recipe.sourceFingerprint,heightM,...binding.appearance});count++;
      }
    }
    return samples;
  }
  private setCatalogue(species:readonly Species[]|undefined){
    if(species===this.catalogueInput)return;
    this.catalogueInput=species;
    let next:ReturnType<typeof forestTreeCatalogue>;
    try{next=forestTreeCatalogue(species??[]);this.catalogueError='';}
    catch(error){
      // Reject malformed presentation data without interrupting the canonical
      // forest or retrying the same immutable dated snapshot every frame.
      this.catalogueError=String(error);next=forestTreeCatalogue([]);
    }
    if(next.signature===this.catalogue.signature)return;
    this.catalogue=next;this.catalogueVersion++;this.bindings=new WeakMap();
  }
  private binding(resident:Resident){
    if(!this.bindings.has(resident))this.bindings.set(resident,this.catalogue.resolve(resident.cell.id));
    return this.bindings.get(resident)??null;
  }
  private syncCanopyPresentation(){
    const key=`${FOREST_PRESENTATION_VERSION}/${this.catalogueVersion}/${this.assets.revision}/${Number(this.assetDraw)}`;
    if(key===this.canopyPresentationKey)return;
    const families=new Map<ForestTreeKind,ForestFamilyPresentation>();
    if(this.assetDraw)for(const [kind,entry] of this.assets.entries){
      const presentation=entry.family.canopyPresentation;
      if(presentation?.enabled)families.set(kind,presentation);
    }
    const catalogue=this.catalogue;
    this.fullCanopy?.setPresentation?.(key,(id,heightM)=>{
      if(!families.size)return 0;
      const binding=catalogue.resolve(id),kind=forestPresentationFamily(binding,heightM,families);
      return kind&&binding?packForestPresentation(kind,binding.appearance.barkBrightness,binding.appearance.foliageBrightness):0;
    },families);
    this.presentedFamilies=families;
    this.canopyPresentationKey=key;
  }
  private expectedCanopyPresentation(resident:Resident){
    if(!this.assetDraw)return 0;
    const binding=this.binding(resident),kind=forestPresentationFamily(binding,resident.cell.heightM,this.presentedFamilies);
    return kind&&binding?packForestPresentation(kind,binding.appearance.barkBrightness,binding.appearance.foliageBrightness):0;
  }
  private startWorker(){
    try{
      const worker=new Worker(new URL('./planet-population-worker.ts',import.meta.url),{type:'module'});this.worker=worker;
      worker.onmessage=({data})=>{
        if(this.worker!==worker||!this.selecting)return;
        if(!data||!Number.isSafeInteger(data.requestId)){this.failWorker(worker);return;}
        if(data.requestId===this.selectionRequest){
          const started=performance.now();let cells:PopulationCell[];
          try{
            if(!this.selectionDirection||!Number.isFinite(data.selectionMs)||data.selectionMs<0||
              !Number.isFinite(data.packMs)||data.packMs<0)throw new Error('Invalid forest response metadata');
            cells=unpackForestSelection(data.packed);
          }catch{this.failWorker(worker);return;}
          this.selectionTransfer={bytes:data.packed.buffer.byteLength,cells:cells.length,packMs:data.packMs,decodeMs:performance.now()-started};
          // The main thread owns the requested centre; response metadata
          // cannot move it, and duplicate completed replies are ignored.
          // Application errors must surface as main-thread bugs, not use up
          // a worker restart or be silently converted into a fallback.
          this.selectionCacheStats=data.cache??null;this.applySelection(this.selectionDirection!,cells,data.selectionMs);
        }
      };
      worker.onerror=event=>{event.preventDefault();this.failWorker(worker);};
      worker.onmessageerror=()=>this.failWorker(worker);
    }catch{this.worker=null;this.selecting=false;this.workerFailures++;}
  }
  private failWorker(worker:Worker){
    if(this.worker!==worker)return;
    worker.terminate();this.worker=null;this.selectionCacheStats=null;this.selectionTransfer=null;this.selectionDirection=null;this.selecting=false;this.workerFailures++;this.selectionRequest++;
    if(this.workerRestarts<1){this.workerRestarts++;this.startWorker();}
  }
  /** Slots belong to resident IDs, not their current visibility/sort ordinal. */
  private writeInstance(batch:number,index:number,resident:Resident,coverage:number){
    const mesh=batch===2?this.canopy:this.meshes[batch],updates=this.updates[batch];
    const changed=updates.residents[index]!==resident;
    if(changed||updates.originVersions[index]!==this.originVersion){
      const {cell,height}=resident,r=this.body.radiusM;
      this.direction.set(...cell.direction);
      this.position.copy(this.direction).multiplyScalar(1+height/r).sub(this.origin);
      treeQuaternion(this.direction,cell.yaw,this.quaternion,this.yaw);
      this.scale.setScalar(cell.heightM/r);
      this.matrix.compose(this.position,this.quaternion,this.scale);mesh.setMatrixAt(index,this.matrix);
      updates.matrix.include(index,16);updates.originVersions[index]=this.originVersion;this.matrixWrites++;
    }
    if(changed){
      const roll=resident.cell.roll;
      this.color.setRGB(.8+roll*.2,.85+roll*.15,.75+roll*.25);mesh.setColorAt(index,this.color);
      updates.color.include(index,3);updates.residents[index]=resident;this.colorWrites++;
      const seed=mesh.geometry.getAttribute('instanceForestSeed') as THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute;seed.setX(index,Math.min(255,Math.floor(roll*256)));updates.seed.include(index,1);
    }
    const detailed=batch<2&&!!this.detailEntry(resident);
    const attribute=batch===2?this.canopyCoverage:this.coverage[batch],value=Math.fround(detailed?0:coverage);
    if(attribute.getX(index)!==value){attribute.setX(index,value);updates.coverage.include(index,1);this.coverageWrites++;}
  }
  private detailEntry(resident:Resident){
    if(!this.assetDraw)return undefined;
    const kind=forestPresentationFamily(this.binding(resident),resident.cell.heightM,this.presentedFamilies);
    return kind?this.assets.entries.get(kind):undefined;
  }
  private writeDetailed(entry:ForestTreeFamilyEntry<Resident,AssetSlot>,lod:number,index:number,resident:Resident,previous:AssetSlot|undefined):AssetSlot{
    const asset=entry.batches[lod],binding=this.binding(resident)!,cell=resident.cell;
    let state=previous;
    if(!previous||previous.resident!==resident||previous.originVersion!==this.originVersion||previous.catalogueVersion!==this.catalogueVersion){
      this.direction.set(...cell.direction);this.position.copy(this.direction).multiplyScalar(1+resident.height/this.body.radiusM).sub(this.origin);
      treeQuaternion(this.direction,cell.yaw,this.quaternion,this.yaw);
      this.scale.setScalar(1/this.body.radiusM);this.matrix.compose(this.position,this.quaternion,this.scale);
      asset.setInstance(index,this.matrix,cell.heightM,binding.appearance.windPhase01*2*Math.PI);
      const bark=binding.appearance.barkBrightness,leaf=binding.appearance.foliageBrightness;
      asset.setAppearance(index,[bark,bark,bark],[leaf,leaf,leaf]);
      state={resident,originVersion:this.originVersion,catalogueVersion:this.catalogueVersion};
    }
    asset.setCoverage(index,entry.coverage[lod].get(cell.id)!,Math.min(255,Math.floor(cell.roll*256)));
    return state!;
  }
  private wantInstance(batch:number,resident:Resident,coverage:number){
    coverage=quantizeForestCoverage(coverage)/256;
    const updates=this.updates[batch];updates.wanted.push(resident);updates.wantedCoverage.push(coverage);updates.wantedIds.add(resident.cell.id);
    this.fullCanopy?.own(resident.cell.id,coverage);
  }
  private reconcileBatch(batch:number){
    const updates=this.updates[batch],mesh=batch===2?this.canopy:this.meshes[batch];
    const coverage=batch===2?this.canopyCoverage:this.coverage[batch];
    if(batch<2){
      for(const entry of this.assets.entries.values()){entry.wanted[batch].length=0;entry.coverage[batch].clear();}
      for(let i=0;i<updates.wanted.length;i++){
        const resident=updates.wanted[i],entry=this.detailEntry(resident);if(!entry)continue;
        entry.wanted[batch].push(resident);entry.coverage[batch].set(resident.cell.id,updates.wantedCoverage[i]);
      }
      for(const entry of this.assets.entries.values()){
        const count=entry.pools[batch].reconcile(entry.wanted[batch],(index,resident,previous)=>this.writeDetailed(entry,batch,index,resident,previous));
        entry.batches[batch].setCount(count);entry.batches[batch].group.visible=count>0;
        this.assetCounts[batch]+=count;
      }
    }
    // A departure moves only the final resident into the hole. Reverse traversal
    // ensures that moved residents have already been checked for retention.
    for(let index=updates.residents.length-1;index>=0;index--){
      const resident=updates.residents[index];if(updates.wantedIds.has(resident.cell.id))continue;
      updates.slots.delete(resident.cell.id);
      const last=updates.residents.length-1;
      if(index!==last){
        const moved=updates.residents[last];this.writeInstance(batch,index,moved,coverage.getX(last));
        updates.slots.set(moved.cell.id,index);
      }
      updates.residents.pop();updates.originVersions.pop();
    }
    for(let i=0;i<updates.wanted.length;i++){
      const resident=updates.wanted[i];let index=updates.slots.get(resident.cell.id);
      if(index===undefined){index=updates.residents.length;updates.slots.set(resident.cell.id,index);}
      this.writeInstance(batch,index,resident,updates.wantedCoverage[i]);
    }
    mesh.count=updates.residents.length;
    if(batch<2&&this.assetCounts[batch]===mesh.count)mesh.visible=false;
    this.scheduledUploadBytes+=updates.matrix.flush(mesh.instanceMatrix);
    if(mesh.instanceColor)this.scheduledUploadBytes+=updates.color.flush(mesh.instanceColor);
    this.scheduledUploadBytes+=updates.coverage.flush(coverage);
    this.scheduledUploadBytes+=updates.seed.flush(mesh.geometry.getAttribute('instanceForestSeed') as THREE.BufferAttribute);
  }
  update(eye:THREE.Vector3,groundDirection:V3,altitudeM:number,enabled:boolean,water:number,immersive:boolean,frusta:THREE.Frustum[]=[],worldNs=0n,treeSpecies?:readonly Species[],shadowFrustum:THREE.Frustum|null=null){
    if(!Number.isFinite(water))throw new Error('Forest water level must be finite');
    if(this.assets.entries.size)this.setCatalogue(treeSpecies);
    this.worldDate=worldNs;this.assets.setDate(worldNs);
    this.assetDraw=this.assets.entries.size>0&&!immersive;this.assetCounts=[0,0];
    this.syncCanopyPresentation();
    for(const entry of this.assets.entries.values())for(const batch of entry.batches)batch.group.visible=false;
    const updateStarted=performance.now();
    this.group.visible=enabled&&altitudeM<B.forestStreamAltitudeM;
    this.visibleTrees=0;this.nearTrees=0;this.placementMs=0;this.selectionMs=0;
    this.matrixWrites=0;this.colorWrites=0;this.coverageWrites=0;this.scheduledUploadBytes=0;this.originRebased=false;this.updateMs=0;this.farOwnedSkipped=0;
    if(!this.group.visible){if(this.fullCanopy)this.fullCanopy.group.visible=false;return;}
    const draw=altitudeM<B.forestPrepareAltitudeM;
    for(const mesh of this.meshes)mesh.visible=draw;this.canopy.visible=draw;
    if(!draw){
      this.fullCanopy?.prepare(eye,groundDirection,altitudeM,water,enabled,immersive,this.origin);
      this.fullCanopy?.finish();this.updateMs=performance.now()-updateStarted;return;
    }
    if(immersive!==this.immersiveMaterial){
      this.immersiveMaterial=immersive;
      for(const mesh of [...this.meshes,this.canopy]){const material=mesh.material as THREE.MeshLambertMaterial;material.alphaHash=immersive;material.needsUpdate=true;}
    }
    const hydrologyChanged=this.sampledRivers!==this.body.canonicalRivers||this.sampledHydrology!==this.body.hydrology;
    if(water!==this.water||hydrologyChanged){
      this.sampledRivers=this.body.canonicalRivers;this.sampledHydrology=this.body.hydrology;
      if(hydrologyChanged)for(const resident of this.cache.values())resident.water=NaN;
      this.patchBroadphase.setWater(water);if(hydrologyChanged)this.patchBroadphase.invalidate();
      this.workStateVersion++;
      this.workHabitatRevision++;
      this.workGlobalHabitatRevision++;
      this.water=water;this.sample=terrainSampler(this.body,undefined,undefined,undefined,water);
      this.classify=biomeClassifier(this.body,water);
      this.habitat=forestHabitat(this.sample,this.classify,this.body.radiusM);
      // Selection depends only on identity/location, not water. Keep the single
      // in-flight job and resident trees while bounded habitat refresh replaces
      // them. Never queue another expensive selection on each playback bucket.
      this.queue=this.wanted.filter(cell=>this.cache.get(cell.id)?.water!==water);
    }
    const r=this.body.radiusM,ground=this.direction.set(...groundDirection);
    if(ground.distanceTo(this.center)*r>40&&!this.selecting){
      if(this.worker){
        const worker=this.worker;this.selecting=true;this.selectionDirection=[...groundDirection];
        try{worker.postMessage({requestId:++this.selectionRequest,seed:this.body.seed,direction:groundDirection,radiusM:r});}
        catch{this.failWorker(worker);}
      }else{
      const started=performance.now();
      const cells=forestPopulationCells(this.body.seed,groundDirection,r,B.forestFootprintM+100,B.canopyFootprintM,B.forestCellM);
      this.applySelection(groundDirection,cells,performance.now()-started);
      }
    }
    const started=performance.now();
    for(let count=0;this.queue.length&&count<B.placementSamplesPerFrame&&performance.now()-started<B.placementBudgetMs;count++){
      const cell=this.queue.shift()!,habitat=this.habitat(cell);
      this.cache.set(cell.id,{cell,height:habitat.groundHeightM,present:habitat.present,water});
      this.patchBroadphase.updateResident(cell.id,{groundHeightM:habitat.groundHeightM,water});
      this.workStateVersion++;
      this.workHabitatRevision++;
    }
    this.placementMs=performance.now()-started;
    // Retain the anchor across ordinary movement so static tree matrices need
    // no rewrite. Rebase before Float32 conversion to preserve local precision.
    if(!this.originReady||this.origin.distanceTo(eye)*r>=B.forestOriginRebaseM){
      this.origin.copy(eye);this.group.position.copy(this.origin);this.originReady=true;
      this.originVersion++;this.originRebased=true;
    }
    this.fullCanopy?.prepare(eye,groundDirection,altitudeM,water,enabled,immersive,this.origin);
    const selectionMismatch=!this.broadphaseSelectionReady||this.workIndex.diagnostics().selectionCells!==this.wanted.length;
    if(selectionMismatch){

    scheduledUploadBytes:this.scheduledUploadBytes,originRebased:this.originRebased,originVersion:this.originVersion,originRebaseM:B.forestOriginRebaseM,
    radiusM:B.forestRadiusM,heightRangeM:[10,22],cellSizeM:B.forestCellM,
    allocatedBytes:[...this.meshes,this.canopy].reduce((s,m)=>s+m.instanceMatrix.array.byteLength+(m.instanceColor?.array.byteLength??0)+
      Object.values(m.geometry.attributes).reduce((n,a)=>n+a.array.byteLength,0),0)*2};}
  dispose(){if(this.disposed)return;this.disposed=true;this.assets.dispose();this.worker?.terminate();this.worker=null;this.fullCanopy?.dispose();this.fullCanopy=null;for(const mesh of [...this.meshes,this.canopy]){mesh.geometry.dispose();(mesh.material as THREE.Material).dispose();mesh.customDepthMaterial?.dispose();}this.cache.clear();this.group.removeFromParent();}

  private applySelectionSlice(started:number){

import type {ForestTreeBinding} from './forest-tree-catalogue.ts';
import type {ForestTreeKind} from './forest-tree-family-library.ts';

export const FOREST_PRESENTATION_VERSION='forest-presentation-1';
const LOW=.8,RANGE=.4;
/** Shared eligibility for detailed and family-aware distant presentation.
 * The caller supplies the binding from the one already dated catalogue. */
export function forestPresentationFamily(binding:ForestTreeBinding|null,heightM:number,
  available:{has:(kind:ForestTreeKind)=>boolean}):ForestTreeKind|null{
  if(!binding||!Number.isFinite(heightM)||heightM<10||heightM>22)return null;
  const kind=binding.recipe.family;
  return available.has(kind)?kind:null;
}

/** Fits family and two bounded brightness values into the unused float channel
 * of the existing far instance buffer. Every encoded integer is exact in f32.
 * Code zero retains the legacy shape/roll tint. No identity or date is encoded. */
export function packForestPresentation(kind:ForestTreeKind,bark:number,foliage:number){
  if(!['branching','spire'].includes(kind)||![bark,foliage].every(x=>Number.isFinite(x)&&x>=LOW&&x<=LOW+RANGE))
    throw new Error('Invalid forest family presentation');
  const quantize=(x:number)=>Math.round((x-LOW)*255/RANGE);
  return (kind==='branching'?1:2)+4*quantize(foliage)+1024*quantize(bark);
}
export function unpackForestPresentation(code:number){
  if(!Number.isSafeInteger(code)||code<0||code>262142||code!==0&&![1,2].includes(code%4))
    throw new Error('Invalid packed forest presentation');
  if(code===0)return {kind:null,bark:1,foliage:1};
  return {kind:(code%4===1?'branching':'spire') as ForestTreeKind,
    foliage:LOW+(Math.floor(code/4)%256)*RANGE/255,bark:LOW+Math.floor(code/1024)*RANGE/255};
}

export const forestPresentationGLSL=`
float forestFamilyCode(float packed){return mod(packed,4.0);}
vec2 forestFamilyBrightness(float packed){
  return vec2(.8)+vec2(floor(packed/1024.0),mod(floor(packed/4.0),256.0))*(.4/255.0);
}
`;

import type {Body} from './catalog.ts';

export const FOREST_CENSUS_VERSION='forest-census-3';
/** Present members only: offset, direction xyz, roll, nominal height, yaw,
 * ground height and representative flag. An absent record in a COMPLETE patch
 * proves absence; a pending/missing patch must never be treated as empty. */
export const FOREST_CENSUS_STRIDE=9;
const sha256=async(text:string)=>Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',new TextEncoder().encode(text))),value=>value.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
export type ForestCensusPatch={
  context:string;address:ForestPatchAddress;testedCells:number;candidates:number;
  present:number;representatives:number;records:Float64Array;
  waterInterval?:ForestWaterInterval;
};
type Entry={patch:ForestCensusPatch;bytes:number};
type Active={patch:ForestPatch;index:number;records:number[];representatives:number};

/** Type-tagged canonical content. Unsupported values must never silently hash
 * as an empty object or null. Typed arrays retain both their type and values.
 * This is cache provenance, not the deterministic population seed. */
function canonical(value:unknown,ancestors=new Set<object>()):unknown{
  if(value===null)return null;
  if(value===undefined)return ['undefined'];
  if(typeof value==='number'){if(!Number.isFinite(value))throw new Error('Non-finite census context');return value;}
  if(typeof value==='string'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value!=='object')throw new Error('Unsupported census context value');
  if(ancestors.has(value))throw new Error('Cyclic census context');
  ancestors.add(value);
  try{
    if(ArrayBuffer.isView(value)){
      if(value instanceof DataView)throw new Error('Unsupported census context DataView');
      return ['typed',value.constructor.name,Array.from(value as unknown as ArrayLike<unknown>,v=>canonical(v,ancestors))];
    }
    if(Array.isArray(value))return ['array',Array.from(value,v=>canonical(v,ancestors))];
    if(Object.getPrototypeOf(value)!==Object.prototype&&Object.getPrototypeOf(value)!==null)throw new Error('Unsupported census context object');
    return ['object',Object.keys(value).sort().map(key=>{
      const descriptor=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value,key)!;
      if(!('value' in descriptor))throw new Error('Unsupported census context accessor');
      return [key,canonical(descriptor.value,ancestors)];
    })];
  }finally{ancestors.delete(value);}
}

/** This context owns a cloned body, including the actual hydrology geometry.
 * The SHA covers content, not merely region IDs. No date is simulated here:
 * dated world eligibility still gates the parent renderer, as it does near trees.
 * Object-key order cannot affect the stamp; array order is retained. */
export async function createForestCensus(body:Body,waterLevelM:number,maxBytes=48*1024*1024,maxPatches=16000,previous?:ForestCensus|null){
  if(!Number.isFinite(body.radiusM)||body.radiusM<=0||!Number.isFinite(waterLevelM)||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(maxBytes)||maxBytes<0||!Number.isSafeInteger(maxPatches)||maxPatches<0)throw new Error('Invalid forest census context');
  const description=JSON.stringify([FOREST_CENSUS_VERSION,POPULATION_VERSION,TREE_HABITAT_VERSION,
    TERRAIN_VERSION,REGIONAL_LANDFORM_VERSION,LANDFORM_VERSION,BIOME_VERSION,RIVER_SURFACE_VERSION,FOREST_CELL_M,
    FOREST_PATCH_VERSION,FOREST_PATCH_SIDE,canonical(body)]);
  const snapshot=structuredClone(body);
  const family=await sha256(description);
  return censusForSnapshot(snapshot,family,waterLevelM,maxBytes,maxPatches,previous);
}
async function censusForSnapshot(snapshot:Body,family:string,waterLevelM:number,maxBytes:number,maxPatches:number,previous?:ForestCensus|null){
  if(!Number.isFinite(waterLevelM))throw new Error('Invalid forest water level');
  const context=await sha256(JSON.stringify([family,waterLevelM]));
  const validity=new ForestWaterValidity(waterLevelM);
  const sample=terrainSampler(snapshot,undefined,undefined,false,waterLevelM,validity.observe);
  const tracked:typeof sample=d=>{const result=sample(d);if(snapshot.water)validity.observe(result.groundHeightM);return result;};
  // Track every actual query, including probes whose slope rejects the tree.
  // Recording probe ground heights too is conservative (only centres classify).
  const habitat=forestHabitat(tracked,biomeClassifier(snapshot,waterLevelM),snapshot.radiusM,true);
  const census=new ForestCensus(context,snapshot.seed,Math.ceil(2*snapshot.radiusM/FOREST_CELL_M),habitat,maxBytes,maxPatches,{family,validity,snapshot});
  if(previous)census.inherit(previous,waterLevelM);return census;
}

/** Single bounded in-progress patch plus a byte/entry bounded completed LRU.
 * The caller decides job priority and yields between step() calls. It cannot
 * receive a partial patch. Every result owns its own transferable buffer. */
export class ForestCensus {
  private entries=new Map<string,Entry>();
  private active:Active|null=null;
  private bytes=0;private hits=0;private misses=0;private evictions=0;private sampled=0;
  readonly context:string;readonly n:number;readonly family:string|null;
  private validity:ForestWaterValidity|null;private reusedPatches=0;
  private snapshot:Body|null;
  private seed:string;private habitat:ReturnType<typeof forestHabitat>;private maxBytes:number;private maxPatches:number;
  constructor(context:string,seed:string,n:number,
    habitat:ReturnType<typeof forestHabitat>,maxBytes:number,maxPatches:number,options?:{family:string;validity:ForestWaterValidity;snapshot?:Body}){
    if(!Number.isSafeInteger(n)||n<1||!Number.isSafeInteger(maxBytes)||maxBytes<0||
      !Number.isSafeInteger(maxPatches)||maxPatches<0)throw new Error('Invalid forest census limits');
    this.context=context;this.seed=seed;this.n=n;this.habitat=habitat;this.maxBytes=maxBytes;this.maxPatches=maxPatches;
    this.family=options?.family??null;this.validity=options?.validity??null;this.snapshot=options?.snapshot??null;
  }
  /** Uses only the worker-owned immutable snapshot; never accepts a new body. */
  atWater(water:number){
    if(!this.snapshot||!this.family)throw new Error('Census has no reusable body snapshot');
    return censusForSnapshot(this.snapshot,this.family,water,this.maxBytes,this.maxPatches,this);
  }
  private key(address:ForestPatchAddress){return `${address.face}/${address.n}/${address.x}/${address.y}`;}
  private copy(patch:ForestCensusPatch):ForestCensusPatch{return {...patch,address:{...patch.address},records:patch.records.slice(),
    ...(patch.waterInterval?{waterInterval:[...patch.waterInterval] as ForestWaterInterval}:{})};}
  inherit(previous:ForestCensus,water:number){
    if(!this.family||previous.family!==this.family||previous.n!==this.n)return;
    for(const {patch} of previous.entries.values())if(forestWaterIntervalContains(patch.waterInterval,water)){
      // Cache records are immutable and returned buffers are always copies.
      // Neither the previous context nor any outstanding reply is mutated.
      if(this.store({...patch,context:this.context}))this.reusedPatches++;
    }
  }
  start(address:ForestPatchAddress):ForestCensusPatch|null {
    if(this.active)throw new Error('Forest census already has an active patch');
    if(address.n!==this.n)throw new Error('Forest census grid mismatch');
    validateForestPatchAddress(address);
    const key=this.key(address),cached=this.entries.get(key);
    if(cached){this.hits++;this.entries.delete(key);this.entries.set(key,cached);return this.copy(cached.patch);}
    const patch=forestPatch(this.seed,address);
    this.validity?.reset();
    this.misses++;this.active={patch,index:0,records:[],representatives:0};return null;
  }
  step(maxCandidates=8):ForestCensusPatch|null {
    if(!Number.isInteger(maxCandidates)||maxCandidates<1||maxCandidates>64)throw new Error('Invalid forest census slice');
    try{return this.advance(maxCandidates);}catch(error){this.active=null;throw error;}
  }
  private advance(maxCandidates:number):ForestCensusPatch|null {
    const active=this.active;if(!active)return null;
    const {patch}=active,{x,y}=patch.address,end=Math.min(patch.cells.length,active.index+maxCandidates);
    for(;active.index<end;active.index++){
      const cell=patch.cells[active.index],sample=this.habitat(cell);this.sampled++;
      if(!Number.isFinite(sample.groundHeightM))throw new Error('Non-finite forest census height');
      if(!sample.present)continue;
      const parts=cell.id.split('/'),offset=(Number(parts[3])-y*FOREST_PATCH_SIDE)*FOREST_PATCH_SIDE+Number(parts[2])-x*FOREST_PATCH_SIDE;
      if(Number(parts[0])!==patch.address.face||Number(parts[1])!==patch.address.n||
        !Number.isInteger(offset)||offset<0||offset>=FOREST_PATCH_SIDE**2)throw new Error('Invalid forest census cell identity');
      const representative=Number(forestRepresentative(this.seed,cell.id));active.representatives+=representative;
      active.records.push(offset,...cell.direction,cell.roll,cell.heightM,cell.yaw,sample.groundHeightM,representative);
    }
    if(active.index<patch.cells.length)return null;
    const result:ForestCensusPatch={context:this.context,address:{...patch.address},testedCells:patch.testedCells,
      candidates:patch.cells.length,present:active.records.length/FOREST_CENSUS_STRIDE,
      representatives:active.representatives,records:new Float64Array(active.records),
      ...(this.validity?{waterInterval:this.validity.interval()}:{})};
    this.active=null;
    this.store(result);return this.copy(result);
  }
  private store(result:ForestCensusPatch){
    const key=this.key(result.address),bytes=result.records.byteLength+288+key.length*2;
    if(bytes<=this.maxBytes&&this.maxPatches>0){
      const previous=this.entries.get(key);if(previous){this.bytes-=previous.bytes;this.entries.delete(key);}
      while(this.entries.size&&(this.bytes+bytes>this.maxBytes||this.entries.size>=this.maxPatches)){
        const oldest=this.entries.keys().next().value!;this.bytes-=this.entries.get(oldest)!.bytes;this.entries.delete(oldest);this.evictions++;
      }
      this.entries.set(key,{patch:result,bytes});this.bytes+=bytes;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  cancelActive(){this.active=null;}
  diagnostics(){return {context:this.context,family:this.family,reusedPatches:this.reusedPatches,patches:this.entries.size,estimatedBytes:this.bytes,maxBytes:this.maxBytes,
    maxPatches:this.maxPatches,hits:this.hits,misses:this.misses,evictionsTotal:this.evictions,sampledCandidates:this.sampled,
    activeCandidates:this.active?this.active.patch.cells.length-this.active.index:0};}
}


SOURCE src/forest-census-worker.ts bytes=6602 sha256=d2461c4daa2019330ce7146a86294aed23e7d95460ee3846c599efd1710f5779
import {createForestCensus,type ForestCensus,type ForestCensusPatch} from './forest-census.ts';
import {validateForestPatchAddress,type ForestPatchAddress} from './forest-patch.ts';
import {forestPatchAddresses} from './forest-patch-selection.ts';

const MAX_BATCH_PATCHES=64,RESULT_BATCH=8,SLICE_MS=4,SLICE_CANDIDATES=8;
let generation=0,requestId=0,census:ForestCensus|null=null;
let retainedCensus:ForestCensus|null=null;
let radiusM=0,selectionRequest=0;
// A local MessageChannel yields to incoming worker messages without the nested
// setTimeout minimum-delay clamp throttling thousands of short census slices.
const wakeup=new MessageChannel();let scheduled=false;
wakeup.port1.onmessage=()=>{scheduled=false;tick();};
let job:{addresses:ForestPatchAddress[];index:number;active:boolean;results:ForestCensusPatch[];started:number;slices:number;maxSliceMs:number}|null=null;
function cancel(){job=null;census?.cancelActive();}
function fail(error:unknown,phase='request',details:Record<string,unknown>={}){
  cancel();self.postMessage({type:'error',phase,generation,requestId,context:census?.context??null,error:String(error),...details});
}
function flush(running:NonNullable<typeof job>,source:ForestCensus,done:boolean){
  const patches=running.results;running.results=[];
  self.postMessage({type:'patches',generation,requestId,context:source.context,patches,done,
    elapsedMs:performance.now()-running.started,slices:running.slices,maxSliceMs:running.maxSliceMs,cache:source.diagnostics()},
    patches.map(patch=>patch.records.buffer));
}
function schedule(){if(!scheduled){scheduled=true;wakeup.port2.postMessage(null);}}
function tick(){
  const running=job,source=census;if(!running||!source)return;
  const started=performance.now();
  try{
    for(let steps=0;steps<64&&running.index<running.addresses.length;steps++){
      let result:ForestCensusPatch|null=null;
      if(!running.active){result=source.start(running.addresses[running.index]);running.active=!result;}
      if(!result)result=source.step(SLICE_CANDIDATES);
      if(result){running.results.push(result);running.index++;running.active=false;}
      if(running.results.length>=RESULT_BATCH||performance.now()-started>=SLICE_MS)break;
    }
    running.slices++;running.maxSliceMs=Math.max(running.maxSliceMs,performance.now()-started);
    const done=running.index===running.addresses.length;
    if(running.results.length>=RESULT_BATCH||done){
      flush(running,source,done);
    }
    if(done)job=null;else schedule();
  }catch(error){
    // Complete patches are still authoritative. Terminate this batch and name
    // its failed patch, so a host can retain successes and isolate the failure.
    if(running.results.length)flush(running,source,false);
    fail(error,'request',{address:running.addresses[running.index]??null,index:running.index});
  }
}
self.onmessage=async({data})=>{
  // Malformed envelopes never cancel or impersonate an unrelated valid job.
  try{
    if(!data||!Number.isSafeInteger(data.generation)||data.generation<1)throw new Error('Invalid census generation');
    if(!['configure','patches','cancel','select'].includes(data.type))throw new Error('Invalid census message type');
    if(data.type!=='configure'&&(!Number.isSafeInteger(data.requestId)||data.requestId<1))throw new Error('Invalid census request');
  }catch(error){
    self.postMessage({type:'error',phase:'protocol',generation,requestId:null,error:String(error)});return;
  }
  try{
    if(data.type==='configure'){
      if(data.generation<=generation)return;
      const previous=census??retainedCensus,previousRadius=radiusM;
      cancel();generation=data.generation;requestId=0;selectionRequest=0;census=null;retainedCensus=previous;
      const ownGeneration=generation;let source:ForestCensus;
      try{
        if(data.body)source=await createForestCensus(data.body,data.waterLevelM,undefined,undefined,previous);
        else if(previous&&data.family===previous.family)source=await previous.atWater(data.waterLevelM);
        else throw new Error('Water configure requires a matching retained body family');
      }
      catch(error){if(ownGeneration===generation)fail(error,'configure');return;}
      if(ownGeneration!==generation)return;
      census=source;retainedCensus=source;radiusM=data.body?.radiusM??previousRadius;self.postMessage({type:'ready',generation,context:source.context,family:source.family,n:source.n,cache:source.diagnostics()});return;
    }
    if(data.generation!==generation){
      if(data.generation>generation&&(data.type==='patches'||data.type==='select'))self.postMessage({type:'error',phase:data.type==='select'?'selection':'request',generation:data.generation,
        requestId:data.requestId,context:null,error:'Forest census is not configured for this generation'});
      return;
    }
    if(data.type==='select'){
      if(data.requestId<=selectionRequest)return;selectionRequest=data.requestId;
      try{
        if(!census)throw new Error('Forest census is not configured');
        const started=performance.now(),addresses=forestPatchAddresses(data.center,radiusM,data.rangeM);
        const packed=new Float64Array(addresses.length*4);
        addresses.forEach((a,i)=>packed.set([a.face,a.n,a.x,a.y],i*4));
        self.postMessage({type:'selection',generation,requestId:selectionRequest,context:census.context,packed,selectionMs:performance.now()-started},[packed.buffer]);
      }catch(error){self.postMessage({type:'error',phase:'selection',generation,requestId:selectionRequest,context:census?.context??null,error:String(error)});}
      return;
    }
    if(data.type==='cancel'){
      if(data.requestId!==requestId||!job)return;
      cancel();self.postMessage({type:'cancelled',generation,requestId,context:census?.context??null});return;
    }
    if(data.requestId<=requestId)return;
    // A newer valid envelope implicitly supersedes the previous request,
    // including when its batch content is subsequently rejected.
    cancel();
    requestId=data.requestId;
    if(!census)throw new Error('Forest census is not configured');
    if(!Array.isArray(data.addresses)||data.addresses.length>MAX_BATCH_PATCHES)throw new Error('Forest census batch limit exceeded');
    const addresses:ForestPatchAddress[]=data.addresses.map((address:ForestPatchAddress)=>{
      validateForestPatchAddress(address);if(address.n!==census!.n)throw new Error('Forest census grid mismatch');return {...address};
    });
    job={addresses,index:0,active:false,results:[],started:performance.now(),slices:0,maxSliceMs:0};schedule();
  }catch(error){fail(error);}
};

import * as THREE from 'three';
import {treeCaptureViews,treeViewBlend} from './tree-impostor-views.ts';
import {packTreeCaptureLayer} from './tree-impostor-filter.ts';

export type TreeCaptureFiltering='legacy-unassociated'|'coverage-weighted';
export type TreeCaptureLoadOptions={filtering?:TreeCaptureFiltering};

/** Development representation for one exact captured height/variant. No forest
 * residency, instancing, shadow casting or game handoff is installed here. */
export async function loadTreeCapture(manifest:any,read:(path:string)=>Promise<ArrayBuffer>,options:TreeCaptureLoadOptions={}){
  const filtering=options.filtering??'legacy-unassociated';
  if(!['legacy-unassociated','coverage-weighted'].includes(filtering))throw new Error('Unsupported tree capture filtering mode');
  if(!manifest||manifest.schemaVersion!==2||![3,9,17].includes(manifest.options?.grid)||manifest.rowOrder!=='bottom-to-top'||manifest.colour!=='linear-unassociated-rgb-area-coverage'||manifest.data!=='object-normal-xyz-normalized-frontmost-depth'||manifest.classification!=='leaf-fraction'||!Array.isArray(manifest.artifacts))throw new Error('Unsupported tree capture contract');
  const {grid,size,heightM}=manifest.options??{},views=treeCaptureViews(grid);
  if(manifest.operation!=='atlas-tree-impostor-capture-2'||manifest.version!=='tree-material-capture-2'||![32,64,128].includes(size)||!Number.isFinite(heightM)||heightM<10||heightM>22||!Number.isFinite(manifest.radius)||manifest.radius<=0||manifest.radius>100||!Array.isArray(manifest.center)||manifest.center.length!==3||manifest.center.some((x:number)=>!Number.isFinite(x)||Math.abs(x)>100))throw new Error('Invalid tree capture manifest');
  if(JSON.stringify(manifest.views)!==JSON.stringify(views))throw new Error('Capture view bases do not match the declared contract');
  const records=new Map<string,any>(manifest.artifacts.map((r:any)=>[r.path,r]));
  if(records.size!==manifest.artifacts.length)throw new Error('Duplicate capture attachment');
  const colours:{data:Uint8Array;width:number;height:number}[]=[],classes:typeof colours=[],data:{data:Uint16Array;width:number;height:number}[]=[];
  async function readAttachment(viewIndex:number,mip:number,width:number,channel:'colour'|'data'|'classification'){
    const channels=channel==='classification'?1:4,channelCount=width*width*channels;
    const path=`view-${String(viewIndex).padStart(3,'0')}/mip-${mip}.${channel}.f32`,record=records.get(path);
    if(!record||record.bytes!==channelCount*4)throw new Error('Capture attachment length is invalid');
    const payload=await read(path);
    if(payload.byteLength!==channelCount*4)throw new Error('Truncated capture attachment');
    const hash=Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',payload)),v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
    if(hash!==record.sha256)throw new Error('Capture attachment hash mismatch');
    return new Float32Array(payload);
  }
  let bytes=0;
  for(let mip=0,width=size;width>=1;mip++,width/=2){
    const count=width*width*4,colour=new Uint8Array(count*views.length),values=new Uint16Array(colour.length),classification=new Uint8Array(colour.length);
    for(const view of views){
      const [colourFloats,dataFloats,classFloats]=await Promise.all([
        readAttachment(view.index,mip,width,'colour'),readAttachment(view.index,mip,width,'data'),readAttachment(view.index,mip,width,'classification'),
      ]);
      const layerOffset=view.index*count;
      if(filtering==='coverage-weighted'){
        const packed=packTreeCaptureLayer(colourFloats,dataFloats,classFloats);
        colour.set(packed.colour,layerOffset);values.set(packed.data,layerOffset);classification.set(packed.classification,layerOffset);
        continue;
      }
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
        const value=colourFloats[i],offset=layerOffset+i;
        if(!Number.isFinite(value)||value<0||value>1.001)throw new Error('Capture channel is outside its range');
        const encoded=i%4===3?value:value<=.0031308?12.92*value:1.055*value**(1/2.4)-.055;
        colour[offset]=Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(1,encoded))*255);
      }
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
        const value=dataFloats[i],offset=layerOffset+i;
        if(!Number.isFinite(value)||value < -1.001||value>1.001)throw new Error('Capture channel is outside its range');
        values[offset]=THREE.DataUtils.toHalfFloat(value);
      }
      for(let i=0;i<width*width;i++){
        const value=classFloats[i],offset=layerOffset+i*4;
        if(!Number.isFinite(value)||value<0||value>1.001)throw new Error('Capture channel is outside its range');
        classification[offset]=Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(1,value))*255);
      }
    }
    colours.push({data:colour,width,height:width});data.push({data:values,width,height:width});classes.push({data:classification,width,height:width});bytes+=colour.byteLength+values.byteLength+classification.byteLength;
  }
  // The installed Three DataArrayTexture upload path only uploads level zero.
  // Its CompressedArrayTexture path supports explicit RGBA mip data as well:
  // RGBAFormat selects texSubImage3D, without block compression.
  // @types excludes the installed renderer's explicit uncompressed RGBA path.
  const rgba=THREE.RGBAFormat as unknown as THREE.CompressedPixelFormat;
  const colourTexture=new THREE.CompressedArrayTexture(colours,size,size,views.length,rgba);
  colourTexture.colorSpace=THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  const dataTexture=new THREE.CompressedArrayTexture(data,size,size,views.length,rgba,THREE.HalfFloatType);
  dataTexture.colorSpace=THREE.NoColorSpace;
  // RGBA class storage uses the installed explicit-mip array path. Three spare
  // channels are a measured prototype cost; this is not yet an R8 upload path.
  const classTexture=new THREE.CompressedArrayTexture(classes,size,size,views.length,rgba);
  for(const texture of [colourTexture,dataTexture,classTexture]){
    if(Number(texture.format)!==THREE.RGBAFormat)throw new Error('Installed array texture default no longer supports RGBA mip uploads');
    texture.minFilter=THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter;texture.magFilter=THREE.LinearFilter;
    texture.generateMipmaps=false;texture.flipY=false;texture.needsUpdate=true;
  }
  return {manifest,views,colourTexture,dataTexture,classTexture,bytes,filtering,
    dispose(){colourTexture.dispose();dataTexture.dispose();classTexture.dispose();}};
}
export type TreeCapture=Awaited<ReturnType<typeof loadTreeCapture>>;

const sampleGLSL=`
precision highp sampler2DArray;
uniform sampler2DArray captureColour,captureData,captureClass;
uniform vec3 captureCenter,captureEye,captureDirection;
uniform float captureRadius,captureOrtho,captureMip,captureMask,captureParallax,captureHash,captureCoverageWeighted,captureStableReconstruction,captureRelightViews;
uniform vec3 captureWeights;
uniform ivec3 captureLayers;
uniform mat3 captureBasis0,captureBasis1,captureBasis2,captureNormalMatrix;
uniform mat4 captureModelView,captureProjection;
uniform vec2 captureRoughness,captureSpecular;
varying vec3 captureRayPoint;
vec2 captureUV(vec3 p,mat3 basis){vec3 d=p-captureCenter;return vec2(dot(d,basis[0]),dot(d,basis[1]))/(2.*captureRadius)+.5;}
void captureSample(int layer,mat3 basis,vec3 ro,vec3 rd,out vec4 colour,out vec3 normal,out float t,out float leaf){
  colour=vec4(0);normal=vec3(0,1,0);t=1e20;leaf=0.;
  float denominator=dot(rd,basis[2]);if(abs(denominator)<.01)return;
  float plane=dot(captureCenter-ro,basis[2]);t=plane/denominator;
  vec2 uv=captureUV(ro+rd*t,basis);
  for(int step=0;step<2;step++){
    vec4 data=textureLod(captureData,vec3(clamp(uv,0.,1.),float(layer)),captureMip);
    if(captureCoverageWeighted>.5){float alpha=textureLod(captureColour,vec3(clamp(uv,0.,1.),float(layer)),captureMip).a;data/=max(alpha,1e-6);}
    t=(plane+data.w*captureRadius*captureParallax)/denominator;uv=captureUV(ro+rd*t,basis);
  }
  if(any(lessThan(uv,vec2(0)))||any(greaterThan(uv,vec2(1)))||t<0.)return;
  colour=textureLod(captureColour,vec3(uv,float(layer)),captureMip);
  vec4 data=textureLod(captureData,vec3(uv,float(layer)),captureMip);
  float alpha=max(colour.a,1e-6);vec4 classification=textureLod(captureClass,vec3(uv,float(layer)),captureMip);
  if(captureCoverageWeighted>.5){colour.rgb/=alpha;data/=alpha;classification.r/=alpha;}
  normal=dot(data.xyz,data.xyz)>1e-8?normalize(data.xyz):basis[2];leaf=classification.r;
  // Captured leaf normals face their capture camera. Reorient a two-sided
  // leaf toward this ray before combining views from different directions.
  if(captureStableReconstruction>.5&&leaf>.5&&dot(normal,rd)>0.)normal=-normal;
  t=(plane+data.w*captureRadius)/denominator;
}
float captureNoise(vec2 p){return fract(52.9829189*fract(dot(floor(p),vec2(.06711056,.00583715))));}
`;

export class TreeCapturedImpostor {
  readonly mesh:THREE.Mesh<THREE.PlaneGeometry,THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial>;
  readonly uniforms;
  readonly capture:TreeCapture;
  constructor(capture:TreeCapture,sourceMaterials:readonly THREE.MeshStandardMaterial[]){
    this.capture=capture;
    if(sourceMaterials.length!==2||sourceMaterials.some(m=>m.metalness!==0)||sourceMaterials[0].name!=='MAT_AtlasBark'||sourceMaterials[1].name!=='MAT_AtlasFoliage')throw new Error('Impostor requires its bark/foliage source materials');
    const specular=sourceMaterials.map(m=>(m as THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial).specularIntensity??1);
    const u=this.uniforms={
      captureColour:{value:capture.colourTexture},captureData:{value:capture.dataTexture},captureClass:{value:capture.classTexture},
      captureCenter:{value:new THREE.Vector3(...capture.manifest.center as [number,number,number])},captureRadius:{value:capture.manifest.radius},
      captureEye:{value:new THREE.Vector3()},captureDirection:{value:new THREE.Vector3()},captureOrtho:{value:0},captureMip:{value:0},captureMask:{value:0},
      captureParallax:{value:1},captureHash:{value:0},captureCoverageWeighted:{value:Number(capture.filtering==='coverage-weighted')},captureStableReconstruction:{value:1},captureRelightViews:{value:0},
      captureWeights:{value:new THREE.Vector3()},captureLayers:{value:new Int32Array(3)},
      captureBasis0:{value:new THREE.Matrix3()},captureBasis1:{value:new THREE.Matrix3()},captureBasis2:{value:new THREE.Matrix3()},
      captureBillboardRight:{value:new THREE.Vector3()},captureBillboardUp:{value:new THREE.Vector3()},captureBillboardScale:{value:1},
      captureNormalMatrix:{value:new THREE.Matrix3()},captureModelView:{value:new THREE.Matrix4()},captureProjection:{value:new THREE.Matrix4()},
      captureRoughness:{value:new THREE.Vector2(sourceMaterials[0].roughness,sourceMaterials[1].roughness)},captureSpecular:{value:new THREE.Vector2(specular[0],specular[1])},
    };
    const material=new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({roughness:1,metalness:0,alphaHash:true});material.fog=false;
    material.onBeforeCompile=shader=>{
      Object.assign(shader.uniforms,u);
      shader.vertexShader=`uniform vec3 captureCenter,captureBillboardRight,captureBillboardUp;
uniform float captureRadius,captureBillboardScale;varying vec3 captureRayPoint;\n`+shader.vertexShader;
      shader.vertexShader=shader.vertexShader.replace('#include <begin_vertex>',`vec3 transformed=captureCenter+captureRadius*captureBillboardScale*(position.x*captureBillboardRight+position.y*captureBillboardUp);captureRayPoint=transformed;`);
      shader.fragmentShader=sampleGLSL+shader.fragmentShader;
      shader.fragmentShader=shader.fragmentShader.replace('#include <logdepthbuf_fragment>',`
vec3 ro=captureEye,rd=normalize(captureRayPoint-captureEye);
if(captureOrtho>.5){rd=captureDirection;ro=captureRayPoint+rd*dot(captureEye-captureRayPoint,rd);}
vec4 c0,c1,c2;vec3 n0,n1,n2;float t0,t1,t2,l0,l1,l2;
captureSample(captureLayers.x,captureBasis0,ro,rd,c0,n0,t0,l0);
captureSample(captureLayers.y,captureBasis1,ro,rd,c1,n1,t1,l1);
captureSample(captureLayers.z,captureBasis2,ro,rd,c2,n2,t2,l2);
vec3 weights=captureWeights*vec3(c0.a,c1.a,c2.a);float captureCoverage=dot(weights,vec3(1));
if(captureCoverage<=.00001)discard;
weights/=captureCoverage;
float hitDistance=min(weights.x>.001?t0:1e20,min(weights.y>.001?t1:1e20,weights.z>.001?t2:1e20));
// A tiny newly visible contribution must not select the depth of the entire
// pixel. Use the same continuous coverage weights as colour and normals.
if(captureStableReconstruction>.5)hitDistance=dot(weights,vec3(t0,t1,t2));

      shader.fragmentShader=shader.fragmentShader.replace('#include <roughnessmap_fragment>',`float roughnessFactor=mix(captureRoughness.x,captureRoughness.y,captureLeaf);`);
      shader.fragmentShader=shader.fragmentShader.replace('#include <lights_physical_fragment>',`#include <lights_physical_fragment>
float capturedSpecular=mix(captureSpecular.x,captureSpecular.y,captureLeaf);material.specularColor=vec3(.04)*capturedSpecular;material.specularF90=capturedSpecular;`);
      // Keep the original single-pass lighting as the default. The optional
      // comparison evaluates Three's same PBR chunks at each reconstructed
      // view surface before blending their reflected-light terms.
      const physicalChunk=THREE.ShaderChunk.lights_physical_fragment,
        lightBegin=THREE.ShaderChunk.lights_fragment_begin,
        lightMaps=THREE.ShaderChunk.lights_fragment_maps,
        lightEnd=THREE.ShaderChunk.lights_fragment_end;

        const suffix=String(index),hit=`captureViewHit${suffix}`,sample=`${suffix}`,begin=lightBegin.replaceAll('vViewPosition',`(-${hit}.xyz)`);
        return `{
vec4 diffuseColor=vec4(c${sample}.rgb,1.);
vec3 normal=normalize(captureNormalMatrix*n${sample}),nonPerturbedNormal=normal;
float roughnessFactor=mix(captureRoughness.x,captureRoughness.y,l${sample});
ReflectedLight reflectedLight=ReflectedLight(vec3(0),vec3(0),vec3(0),vec3(0));
${physicalChunk}

${begin}
${lightMaps}
${lightEnd}
capturePerViewDirectDiffuse${suffix}=reflectedLight.directDiffuse;capturePerViewIndirectDiffuse${suffix}=reflectedLight.indirectDiffuse;

}`;

reflectedLight.indirectDiffuse=capturePerViewIndirectDiffuse0*weights.x+capturePerViewIndirectDiffuse1*weights.y+capturePerViewIndirectDiffuse2*weights.z;

}`;
      const lightIncludes=/#include <lights_fragment_begin>\s*#include <lights_fragment_maps>\s*#include <lights_fragment_end>/g;
      const includeMatches=shader.fragmentShader.match(lightIncludes);
      if(!includeMatches||includeMatches.length!==1)throw new Error('Installed physical shader lighting include sequence has changed');
      shader.fragmentShader=shader.fragmentShader.replace(lightIncludes,lightingBlock);
      shader.fragmentShader=shader.fragmentShader.replace('#include <opaque_fragment>',`#include <opaque_fragment>

      // Diagnostic passes write after display transforms. Depth is metres
      // along camera forward, matching the source mesh's vViewPosition.z.

else if(captureMask>2.5)gl_FragColor.a=captureCoverage;`);
    };
    material.customProgramCacheKey=()=> `atlas-captured-tree-lab-3-${capture.filtering}`;
    this.mesh=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2,2),material);this.mesh.frustumCulled=false;
    this.mesh.name='Authored tree capture reconstruction (development)';
    const inverse=new THREE.Matrix4(),localCamera=new THREE.Matrix4(),viewport=new THREE.Vector4();
    this.mesh.onBeforeRender=(renderer,_scene,camera)=>{
      const gl=renderer.getContext() as WebGL2RenderingContext;if(capture.views.length>gl.getParameter(gl.MAX_ARRAY_TEXTURE_LAYERS))throw new Error('Capture exceeds texture array layer limit');
      inverse.copy(this.mesh.matrixWorld).invert();localCamera.multiplyMatrices(inverse,camera.matrixWorld);
      u.captureEye.value.setFromMatrixPosition(localCamera);u.captureDirection.value.set(0,0,-1).transformDirection(localCamera);

      const toCamera=u.captureOrtho.value?u.captureDirection.value.clone().negate():u.captureEye.value.clone().sub(u.captureCenter.value).normalize();

      const matrices=[u.captureBasis0,u.captureBasis1,u.captureBasis2];
