        matrices[i].value.setFromMatrix4(new THREE.Matrix4().makeBasis(new THREE.Vector3(...basis.right),new THREE.Vector3(...basis.up),new THREE.Vector3(...basis.towardCamera)));
      });
      u.captureBillboardRight.value.set(1,0,0).transformDirection(localCamera);u.captureBillboardUp.value.set(0,1,0).transformDirection(localCamera);
      const distance=u.captureEye.value.distanceTo(u.captureCenter.value),radius=u.captureRadius.value;
      // Tangent cone of the bounding sphere, projected onto its centre plane.
      // The old d/(d-r) expression needlessly enlarged the shaded quad.
      u.captureBillboardScale.value=u.captureOrtho.value?1:u.captureStableReconstruction.value>.5
        ?distance/Math.sqrt(Math.max(.0001,distance*distance-radius*radius))
        :distance/Math.max(.01,distance-radius);
      u.captureModelView.value.multiplyMatrices(camera.matrixWorldInverse,this.mesh.matrixWorld);
      u.captureNormalMatrix.value.getNormalMatrix(u.captureModelView.value);u.captureProjection.value.copy(camera.projectionMatrix);
      renderer.getCurrentViewport(viewport);
      const pixels=radius*Math.abs(camera.projectionMatrix.elements[5])*viewport.w/(u.captureOrtho.value?1:distance);
      u.captureMip.value=Math.max(0,Math.min(Math.log2(capture.manifest.options.size),Math.log2(capture.manifest.options.size/Math.max(1,pixels))));
    };
  }
  dispose(){this.mesh.geometry.dispose();this.mesh.material.dispose();this.mesh.removeFromParent();}
}


SOURCE src/tree-impostor-capture.ts bytes=12759 sha256=1247587da3cf71894425ed98257ec2e2acc1fe6668b3332b334988e732d256be
import * as THREE from 'three';
import {loadTreeAssetFamily,treeVertexMeters} from './tree-asset.ts';
import {treeCaptureViews,TREE_VIEW_VERSION} from './tree-impostor-views.ts';
import type {TreeFoliageMipSidecar} from './tree-foliage-sidecar.ts';

export const TREE_CAPTURE_VERSION='tree-material-capture-2';
export type CaptureLayer={size:number;colour:Float32Array;data:Float32Array;classification:Float32Array};

/** Coverage is area, not a second alpha-test threshold. RGB is linear and
 * unassociated. Normals are averaged as vectors. Depth uses the nearest covered
 * subsample to avoid averaging disconnected surfaces into empty space. */
export function reduceCapture(layer:CaptureLayer,factor:number):CaptureLayer {
  const {size,colour,data,classification}=layer;
  if(!Number.isInteger(size)||size<1||colour.length!==size*size*4||data.length!==colour.length||classification?.length!==size*size||!Number.isInteger(factor)||factor<1||size%factor)throw new Error('Invalid capture reduction');
  const next=size/factor,c=new Float32Array(next*next*4),d=new Float32Array(c.length),classes=new Float32Array(next*next);
  for(let y=0;y<next;y++)for(let x=0;x<next;x++){
    const p=(y*next+x)*4;let weight=0,nx=0,ny=0,nz=0,depth=-Infinity,leaf=0,best=-1;
    for(let dy=0;dy<factor;dy++)for(let dx=0;dx<factor;dx++){
      const q=((y*factor+dy)*size+x*factor+dx)*4,a=colour[q+3];
      if(a<=0)continue;
      weight+=a;for(let j=0;j<3;j++)c[p+j]+=colour[q+j]*a;
      nx+=data[q]*a;ny+=data[q+1]*a;nz+=data[q+2]*a;
      leaf+=classification[q/4]*a;if(data[q+3]>depth){depth=data[q+3];best=q;}
    }
    if(weight===0)continue;
    for(let j=0;j<3;j++)c[p+j]/=weight;c[p+3]=weight/(factor*factor);
    const length=Math.hypot(nx,ny,nz),normal=length>1e-7?[nx/length,ny/length,nz/length]:[data[best],data[best+1],data[best+2]];
    d[p]=normal[0];d[p+1]=normal[1];d[p+2]=normal[2];d[p+3]=depth;classes[p/4]=leaf/weight;
  }
  return {size:next,colour:c,data:d,classification:classes};
}

/** Nearest valid material padding stays inside one view and never creates
 * coverage. Mips are reduced before padding so empty pixels remain empty. */
export function padCapture(layer:CaptureLayer,steps=4):CaptureLayer {
  if(!Number.isInteger(layer.size)||layer.size<1||!(layer.colour instanceof Float32Array)||!(layer.data instanceof Float32Array)||!(layer.classification instanceof Float32Array)||layer.colour.length!==layer.size*layer.size*4||layer.data.length!==layer.colour.length||layer.classification.length!==layer.size*layer.size||!Number.isInteger(steps)||steps<0||steps>16)throw new Error('Invalid capture padding');
  const {size}=layer,c=layer.colour.slice(),d=layer.data.slice(),classification=layer.classification.slice();
  let valid=Uint8Array.from({length:size*size},(_,i)=>c[i*4+3]>0?1:0);
  for(let step=0;step<steps;step++){
    const next=valid.slice();
    for(let y=0;y<size;y++)for(let x=0;x<size;x++){
      const pixel=y*size+x;if(valid[pixel])continue;
      const neighbors=[x>0?pixel-1:-1,x+1<size?pixel+1:-1,y>0?pixel-size:-1,y+1<size?pixel+size:-1];
      const from=neighbors.find(i=>i>=0&&valid[i]);if(from===undefined)continue;
      for(let j=0;j<3;j++)c[pixel*4+j]=c[from*4+j];
      for(let j=0;j<4;j++)d[pixel*4+j]=d[from*4+j];classification[pixel]=classification[from];next[pixel]=1;
    }
    valid=next;
  }
  return {size,colour:c,data:d,classification};
}

export async function captureTree(lod0:ArrayBuffer,lod1:ArrayBuffer,
  options:{grid:number;size:number;supersample:number;heightM:number;lod?:0|1},
  receive:(view:number,mip:number,layer:CaptureLayer)=>Promise<void>,foliageMipSidecar?:TreeFoliageMipSidecar){
  const {grid,size,supersample,heightM}=options,lod=options.lod??0,views=treeCaptureViews(grid);
  if(![32,64,128].includes(size)||![1,2,4,8].includes(supersample)||![0,1].includes(lod)||!Number.isFinite(heightM)||heightM<10||heightM>22)throw new Error('Unsupported tree capture settings');
  const renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({antialias:false,powerPreference:'high-performance',alpha:true});
  renderer.setSize(size*supersample,size*supersample);renderer.setPixelRatio(1);

  renderer.setClearColor(0,0);renderer.shadowMap.enabled=false;
  const gl=renderer.getContext() as WebGL2RenderingContext;
  let family:Awaited<ReturnType<typeof loadTreeAssetFamily>>|undefined;
  let batch:ReturnType<NonNullable<typeof family>['createBatch']>|undefined;
  let target:THREE.WebGLRenderTarget|undefined;
  try{
    if(!gl.getExtension('EXT_color_buffer_float'))throw new Error('Tree capture requires EXT_color_buffer_float');
    if(size*supersample>gl.getParameter(gl.MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE))throw new Error('Capture target exceeds device texture limit');
    family=await loadTreeAssetFamily(lod0,lod1,{foliageMipSidecar});family.setDate(0n);
    batch=family.createBatch(lod,1);batch.setInstance(0,new THREE.Matrix4(),heightM,0);
    const scene=new THREE.Scene();scene.add(batch.group);
    const points:THREE.Vector3[]=[],bounds=new THREE.Box3();let windMargin=0;
    for(const mesh of family.lods[lod]){
      const attrs=mesh.geometry.attributes;
      for(let i=0;i<attrs.position.count;i++){
        const p=new THREE.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(attrs.position,i),pivot=new THREE.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(attrs._leaf_pivot,i);
        const leaf=attrs._branch_level.getX(i)>3.5;
        if(leaf)windMargin=Math.max(windMargin,2*p.distanceTo(pivot)*Math.sin(.12*attrs._wind.getX(i)/2));
        const point=treeVertexMeters(p,pivot,leaf,heightM,attrs._wind.getX(i),0);points.push(point);bounds.expandByPoint(point);
      }
    }
    const center=bounds.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
    let radius=0;for(const point of points)radius=Math.max(radius,point.distanceTo(center));radius+=windMargin+.02;
    if(Math.abs(bounds.min.y)>.02)throw new Error('Captured tree root must remain at ground contact');
    const distance=radius+1,camera=new THREE.OrthographicCamera(-radius,radius,radius,-radius,.5,2*radius+1.5);
    const uniforms={pass:{value:0},viewToObject:{value:new THREE.Matrix3()},distance:{value:distance},radius:{value:radius}};
    for(const mesh of family.lods[lod]){
      const material=mesh.material,previous=material.onBeforeCompile,previousKey=material.customProgramCacheKey();
      material.toneMapped=false;material.fog=false;material.dithering=false;
      material.onBeforeCompile=(shader,host)=>{
        previous.call(material,shader,host);
        Object.assign(shader.uniforms,{atlasCapturePass:uniforms.pass,atlasCaptureViewToObject:uniforms.viewToObject,atlasCaptureDistance:uniforms.distance,atlasCaptureRadius:uniforms.radius});
        shader.fragmentShader=`uniform int atlasCapturePass;
uniform mat3 atlasCaptureViewToObject;
uniform float atlasCaptureDistance,atlasCaptureRadius;
`+shader.fragmentShader;
        // Retain runtime texture decoding, MASK, normal maps and deformation;
        // replace the terminal lighting/output path with linear material data.
        if(!shader.fragmentShader.includes('#include <opaque_fragment>'))throw new Error('Capture shader terminal hook is missing');
        shader.fragmentShader=shader.fragmentShader.replace('#include <opaque_fragment>',`
if(atlasCapturePass==0)gl_FragColor=vec4(diffuseColor.rgb,${material.name==='MAT_AtlasFoliage'?'2.':'1.'});
else if(atlasCapturePass==1)gl_FragColor=vec4(normalize(atlasCaptureViewToObject*normal),
 (atlasCaptureDistance-vViewPosition.z)/atlasCaptureRadius);
else gl_FragColor=vec4(.125,.25,.5,1.);`);
        for(const chunk of ['tonemapping','colorspace','fog','premultiplied_alpha','dithering'])shader.fragmentShader=shader.fragmentShader.replace(`#include <${chunk}_fragment>`,'');
      };
      material.customProgramCacheKey=()=>`${previousKey}/${TREE_CAPTURE_VERSION}/${material.name}`;material.needsUpdate=true;
    }
    if(!batch.meshes.every((mesh,i)=>mesh.material===family!.lods[0][i].material)||!family.lods[0].some(mesh=>mesh.material.name==='MAT_AtlasFoliage'))throw new Error('Capture batch does not use the patched tree materials');
    const captureSize=size*supersample;
    target=new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(captureSize,captureSize,{type:THREE.FloatType,format:THREE.RGBAFormat,minFilter:THREE.NearestFilter,magFilter:THREE.NearestFilter,depthBuffer:true,stencilBuffer:false});
    target.texture.colorSpace=THREE.NoColorSpace;renderer.setRenderTarget(target);
    const colour=new Float32Array(captureSize*captureSize*4),data=new Float32Array(colour.length),classification=new Float32Array(captureSize*captureSize);
    let coveredSamples=0,coverageSum=0;const frameCoverage:number[]=[];
    for(const view of views){

      camera.position.copy(center).addScaledVector(toward,distance);
      camera.quaternion.setFromRotationMatrix(new THREE.Matrix4().makeBasis(right,up,toward));camera.updateMatrixWorld(true);
      uniforms.viewToObject.value.setFromMatrix4(camera.matrixWorld);
      if(view.index===0){

        }
        if(!sentinel)throw new Error('Capture shader sentinel found no covered samples');
      }
      for(const [pass,buffer] of [[0,colour],[1,data]] as const){
        uniforms.pass.value=pass;renderer.clear();renderer.render(scene,camera);renderer.readRenderTargetPixels(target,0,0,captureSize,captureSize,buffer);
        const error=gl.getError();if(error!==gl.NO_ERROR)throw new Error(`Capture GL error ${error} at view ${view.index}, pass ${pass}`);
      }
      for(let p=0;p<colour.length;p+=4){
        if(!Number.isFinite(colour[p]+colour[p+1]+colour[p+2]+colour[p+3]+data[p]+data[p+1]+data[p+2]+data[p+3]))throw new Error('Nonfinite capture texel');
        classification[p/4]=0;
        if(colour[p+3]>0){
          if(![1,2].includes(colour[p+3])||Math.abs(Math.hypot(data[p],data[p+1],data[p+2])-1)>.001||Math.abs(data[p+3])>1.0001||[colour[p],colour[p+1],colour[p+2]].some(x=>x<0||x>1.00001))throw new Error(`Capture channel outside declared range at view ${view.index}`);
          classification[p/4]=colour[p+3]-1;colour[p+3]=1;
          coveredSamples++;
        }
      }

      const area=layer.colour.reduce((sum,value,index)=>sum+(index%4===3?value:0),0);coverageSum+=area;frameCoverage.push(area);

    }
    if(coveredSamples===0)throw new Error('Capture produced no tree coverage');
    const ext=gl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');

      bounds:{min:bounds.min.toArray(),max:bounds.max.toArray(),windMargin},root:[0,0,0],
      rowOrder:'bottom-to-top',colour:'linear-unassociated-rgb-area-coverage',data:'object-normal-xyz-normalized-frontmost-depth',classification:'leaf-fraction',
      depth:'dot(surface-center,towardCamera)/radius; valid only where colour coverage > 0',
      coveredSamples,coverageSum,frameCoverage,bakedState:{date:'0',instancePhase:0,skeletonHeightM:heightM,neutralTint:true,wind:'sin(0)=0 under atlas-tree-physical-1'},adapter:{renderer:ext?gl.getParameter(ext.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL):gl.getParameter(gl.RENDERER),maxArrayLayers:gl.getParameter(gl.MAX_ARRAY_TEXTURE_LAYERS)},
      limitations:['Capture only: runtime impostor, parallax, handoff and game integration are pending','Mips preserve mean area coverage; they require coverage-aware runtime rendering, not a fixed MASK threshold','Depth is the frontmost covered subsample; normal/classification and colour average coverage and can mix disconnected surfaces','RGBA32F capture files are review data, not compressed shipping textures']};
  }finally{
    batch?.dispose();family?.dispose();target?.dispose();renderer.dispose();renderer.forceContextLoss();
  }
}

import {DataUtils} from 'three';

/** GPU-only packing experiment. Review captures remain linear/unassociated.
 * Weight all interpolated attributes by the SAME quantized coverage so texture
 * filtering cannot pull a covered surface toward transparent padding values.
 * The shader must divide every sampled attribute by sampled colour alpha. */
export function packTreeCaptureLayer(colour:Float32Array,data:Float32Array,classification:Float32Array){
  if(!(colour instanceof Float32Array)||!(data instanceof Float32Array)||!(classification instanceof Float32Array)||colour.length===0||colour.length%4||data.length!==colour.length||classification.length!==colour.length/4)throw new Error('Invalid capture layer shape');
  const rgba=new Uint8Array(colour.length),surface=new Uint16Array(data.length),leaf=new Uint8Array(colour.length);
  for(let p=0;p<colour.length;p+=4){
    for(let c=0;c<4;c++)if(!Number.isFinite(colour[p+c])||colour[p+c]<0||colour[p+c]>1.00001||!Number.isFinite(data[p+c])||Math.abs(data[p+c])>1.001)throw new Error('Invalid capture material value');
    const fraction=classification[p/4];if(!Number.isFinite(fraction)||fraction<0||fraction>1.00001)throw new Error('Invalid leaf fraction');
    const byte=Math.round(Math.min(1,colour[p+3])*255),coverage=byte/255;rgba[p+3]=byte;
    if(byte===0)continue;
    for(let c=0;c<3;c++){
      const linear=Math.min(1,colour[p+c])*coverage;
      rgba[p+c]=Math.round(255*(linear<=.0031308?12.92*linear:1.055*linear**(1/2.4)-.055));
    }
    for(let c=0;c<4;c++)surface[p+c]=DataUtils.toHalfFloat(data[p+c]*coverage);
    leaf[p]=Math.round(Math.min(1,fraction)*byte);
  }
  return {colour:rgba,data:surface,classification:leaf};
}


SOURCE src/tree-impostor-views.ts bytes=3531 sha256=caba31dfa7e2a2924a56b6dc5701b662ade1c20c95a40c58542ba13635d39520
/** Pure capture/view contract for the authored-tree distance experiment.
 * Coordinates are object-space, right-handed, Y-up. Directions point from the
 * capture centre towards the camera. This module does not render impostors. */
export type TreeViewVector=readonly [number,number,number];
export const TREE_VIEW_VERSION='tree-full-octahedral-1';
export const TREE_VIEW_GRID=9;
const sign=(x:number)=>x<0?-1:1;
function unit(v:TreeViewVector):TreeViewVector {
  if(v.length!==3||v.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)))throw new Error('View direction must contain three finite components');
  // Avoid overflow/underflow for otherwise valid nonzero directions.
  const scale=Math.max(...v.map(Math.abs));
  if(scale===0)throw new Error('View direction must be nonzero');
  const a=v.map(x=>x/scale),length=Math.hypot(...a);
  return [a[0]/length,a[1]/length,a[2]/length];
}
export function encodeTreeView(direction:TreeViewVector):readonly [number,number] {
  const [x,y,z]=unit(direction),sum=Math.abs(x)+Math.abs(y)+Math.abs(z);
  const a=x/sum,b=z/sum;
  return y>=0?[(a+1)/2,(b+1)/2]:[((1-Math.abs(b))*sign(a)+1)/2,((1-Math.abs(a))*sign(b)+1)/2];
}
export function decodeTreeView(u:number,v:number):TreeViewVector {
  if(!Number.isFinite(u)||!Number.isFinite(v)||u<0||u>1||v<0||v>1)throw new Error('Octahedral coordinates must lie in [0,1]');
  let x=2*u-1,z=2*v-1;
  const y=1-Math.abs(x)-Math.abs(z),fold=Math.max(0,-y);
  x-=sign(x)*fold;z-=sign(z)*fold;
  return unit([x,y,z]);
}
function checkGrid(grid:number){
  // An odd vertex count places equator folds and axes on cell edges/diagonals.
  if(!Number.isInteger(grid)||grid<3||grid>33||grid%2!==1)throw new Error('Tree view grid must be odd and between 3 and 33');
}
export function treeViewBlend(direction:TreeViewVector,grid=TREE_VIEW_GRID){
  checkGrid(grid);
  const [u,v]=encodeTreeView(direction),n=grid-1;
  const gx=u*n,gy=v*n,x=Math.min(n-1,Math.floor(gx)),y=Math.min(n-1,Math.floor(gy));
  const a=gx-x,b=gy-y;
  const at=(dx:number,dy:number)=>({index:(y+dy)*grid+x+dx,x:x+dx,y:y+dy});
  // Choose diagonals that follow the octahedron's equator folds. A single
  // diagonal orientation throughout the square crosses faces in two quadrants.
  const sameQuadrant=(x<n/2)===(y<n/2);
  const vertices=sameQuadrant
    ? a+b<=1?[at(0,0),at(1,0),at(0,1)]:[at(1,1),at(0,1),at(1,0)]
    : a>=b?[at(0,0),at(1,0),at(1,1)]:[at(0,0),at(1,1),at(0,1)];
  // Barycentric weights are in the unfolded octahedron plane. Reconstructing
  // the original ray requires L1-normalized capture vectors, not unit vectors.
  const weights=sameQuadrant
    ? a+b<=1?[1-a-b,a,b]:[a+b-1,1-a,1-b]
    : a>=b?[1-a,a-b,b]:[1-b,a,b-a];
  return {uv:[u,v] as const,vertices,weights};
}
function cross(a:TreeViewVector,b:TreeViewVector):TreeViewVector {
  return [a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1],a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2],a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0]];
}
export function treeCaptureBasis(direction:TreeViewVector){
  const towardCamera=unit(direction);
  const reference:TreeViewVector=Math.abs(towardCamera[1])>.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0];
  const right=unit(cross(reference,towardCamera)),up=unit(cross(towardCamera,right));
  // Each view stores its own basis; bases must not be interpolated at runtime.
  return {towardCamera,right,up};
}
export function treeCaptureViews(grid=TREE_VIEW_GRID){
  checkGrid(grid);
  return Array.from({length:grid*grid},(_,index)=>{
    const x=index%grid,y=Math.floor(index/grid),uv=[x/(grid-1),y/(grid-1)] as const;
    return {index,x,y,uv,...treeCaptureBasis(decodeTreeView(...uv))};
  });
}

/** Experimental author-6 adapter. Assets are supplied explicitly; this does not
 * replace the canonical forest or invent a second species catalogue. */
export const TREE_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_M=16;
export const TREE_WIND_PERIOD_NS=7_000_000_000n;
export const treeWindPhase=(date:bigint)=>phaseFromPeriod(date,TREE_WIND_PERIOD_NS);

type GLBDocument={json:any;binary:Uint8Array};
function readGLB(buffer:ArrayBuffer):GLBDocument {
  const view=new DataView(buffer);
  if(buffer.byteLength<28||view.getUint32(0,true)!==0x46546c67||view.getUint32(4,true)!==2||view.getUint32(8,true)!==buffer.byteLength)throw new Error('Expected a complete glTF 2 GLB');
  const length=view.getUint32(12,true),start=20+length;
  if(view.getUint32(16,true)!==0x4e4f534a||start+8>buffer.byteLength||view.getUint32(start+4,true)!==0x004e4942||start+8+view.getUint32(start,true)!==buffer.byteLength)throw new Error('Expected JSON and embedded binary chunks');
  const json=JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(new Uint8Array(buffer,20,length)));
  if(json.buffers?.length!==1||json.buffers[0].uri||json.extensionsRequired?.length)throw new Error('Only self-contained author-6 GLBs are supported');
  if(json.nodes?.length!==1||json.nodes[0].mesh!==0||['matrix','translation','rotation','scale'].some(k=>json.nodes[0][k])||json.meshes?.length!==1||json.meshes[0].primitives?.length!==2)throw new Error('Expected one untransformed two-material tree');
  if(json.materials?.length!==2||json.materials[0].name!=='MAT_AtlasBark'||json.materials[1].name!=='MAT_AtlasFoliage'||json.images?.length!==4)throw new Error('Expected author-6 bark and foliage');
  if((json.materials[0].alphaMode??'OPAQUE')!=='OPAQUE'||json.materials[1].alphaMode!=='MASK'||(json.materials[1].alphaCutoff??.5)<=0)throw new Error('Tree foliage requires alpha MASK with a positive cutoff');
  const formats:Record<string,string>={_WIND:'SCALAR',_BRANCH_LEVEL:'SCALAR',_LEAF_CENTER:'VEC3',_LEAF_PIVOT:'VEC3',POSITION:'VEC3',NORMAL:'VEC3',TEXCOORD_0:'VEC2',TANGENT:'VEC4'};
  for(const [index,p] of json.meshes[0].primitives.entries()){
    if(p.material!==index||p.mode!==undefined&&p.mode!==4||p.targets||Object.keys(p.attributes??{}).length!==8||!Object.keys(formats).every(k=>Number.isInteger(p.attributes?.[k])))throw new Error('Incomplete tree vertex contract');
    const count=json.accessors[p.attributes.POSITION]?.count;
    for(const [name,type] of Object.entries(formats)){
      const a=json.accessors[p.attributes[name]];
      if(!a||a.type!==type||a.componentType!==5126||a.normalized||a.sparse||!Number.isInteger(count)||count<1||a.count!==count)throw new Error(`Invalid tree accessor ${name}`);
    }
  }
  return {json,binary:new Uint8Array(buffer,start+8,view.getUint32(start,true))};
}
function imageBytes(doc:GLBDocument,index:number){
  const image=doc.json.images[index],view=doc.json.bufferViews[image.bufferView];
  if(image.uri||image.mimeType!=='image/png'||!view||view.buffer!==0||!Number.isInteger(view.byteLength)||view.byteLength<=0)throw new Error('Expected an embedded PNG');
  const start=view.byteOffset??0,end=start+view.byteLength;
  if(start<0||end>doc.binary.length)throw new Error('Image outside GLB binary');
  return doc.binary.subarray(start,end);
}
/** Check byte identity before sharing materials. LOD1 images are never decoded:
 * its parser receives texture-free material placeholders with stable indices. */
export function prepareTreeLODPair(lod0:ArrayBuffer,lod1:ArrayBuffer){
  const a=readGLB(lod0),b=readGLB(lod1);
  for(const key of ['materials','textures','samplers'])if(JSON.stringify(a.json[key])!==JSON.stringify(b.json[key]))throw new Error(`Tree LOD ${key} differ`);
  let embeddedTextureBytes=0;
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
    const x=imageBytes(a,i),y=imageBytes(b,i);embeddedTextureBytes+=x.length;
    if(x.length!==y.length||x.some((v,k)=>v!==y[k]))throw new Error(`Tree LOD image ${i} differs`);
  }
  const json=structuredClone(b.json);
  json.materials=json.materials.map((m:any)=>({name:m.name}));delete json.images;delete json.textures;delete json.samplers;
  // Keep the original binary offsets. Unused compressed PNG bytes remain in
  // this temporary parse input, but do not allocate image/GPU resources.
  const text=new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(json)),length=(text.length+3)&~3;
  const buffer=new ArrayBuffer(28+length+b.binary.length),view=new DataView(buffer),bytes=new Uint8Array(buffer);
  view.setUint32(0,0x46546c67,true);view.setUint32(4,2,true);view.setUint32(8,buffer.byteLength,true);
  view.setUint32(12,length,true);view.setUint32(16,0x4e4f534a,true);bytes.fill(32,20,20+length);bytes.set(text,20);
  view.setUint32(20+length,b.binary.length,true);view.setUint32(24+length,0x004e4942,true);bytes.set(b.binary,28+length);
  const foliageTexture=a.json.materials[1].pbrMetallicRoughness?.baseColorTexture?.index;
  const foliageImage=a.json.textures?.[foliageTexture]?.source;
  if(!Number.isInteger(foliageImage)||foliageImage<0||foliageImage>=4)throw new Error('Expected embedded foliage base-color image');
  return {lod1GeometryBuffer:buffer,embeddedTextureBytes,foliagePng:imageBytes(a,foliageImage)};
}

/** CPU reference for shader audits. Scale skeleton/attachment coordinates,
 * keeping every offset within a leaf card in metres, then rotate rigidly. */
export function treeVertexMeters(position:THREE.Vector3,pivot:THREE.Vector3,leaf:boolean,heightM:number,windWeight:number,phase:number,instancePhase=0){
  if(!Number.isFinite(heightM)||heightM<10||heightM>22)throw new Error('Prototype supports tree heights 10–22 m');
  if(!leaf)return position.clone().multiplyScalar(heightM/TREE_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_M);
  return position.clone().sub(pivot).applyAxisAngle(new THREE.Vector3(0,0,1),Math.sin(phase+instancePhase)*.12*windWeight).addScaledVector(pivot,heightM/TREE_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_M);
}

const declarations=`
attribute float _wind, _branch_level;
attribute vec3 _leaf_pivot;
attribute vec2 instanceTree;
uniform float atlasTreePhase;
mat3 atlasLeafRotation(){
  float angle=sin(atlasTreePhase+instanceTree.y)*.12*_wind;
  float c=cos(angle),s=sin(angle);
  return mat3(c,s,0.,-s,c,0.,0.,0.,1.);
}
`;
function deform(material:THREE.Material,phase:{value:number}){
  material.onBeforeCompile=shader=>{
    shader.uniforms.atlasTreePhase=phase;
    shader.vertexShader=declarations+shader.vertexShader;
    shader.vertexShader=shader.vertexShader.replace('#include <beginnormal_vertex>',`#include <beginnormal_vertex>
      if(_branch_level>3.5){objectNormal=atlasLeafRotation()*objectNormal;
        #ifdef USE_TANGENT
        objectTangent=atlasLeafRotation()*objectTangent;
        #endif
      }`);
    shader.vertexShader=shader.vertexShader.replace('#include <begin_vertex>',`#include <begin_vertex>
      transformed=_branch_level>3.5 ? _leaf_pivot*instanceTree.x+atlasLeafRotation()*(position-_leaf_pivot) : position*instanceTree.x;`);
  };
  material.customProgramCacheKey=()=> 'atlas-tree-physical-1';
}

          setInstance(index:number,matrix:THREE.Matrix4,heightM:number,phaseOffset=0){
            if(batchDisposed||!Number.isInteger(index)||index<0||index>=capacity||index>meshes[0].count||!Number.isFinite(heightM)||heightM<10||heightM>22||!Number.isFinite(phaseOffset)||matrix.elements.some(x=>!Number.isFinite(x)))throw new Error('Invalid tree instance or gap in initialized slots');

            if(!Number.isFinite(scale2)||scale2<=0||Math.abs(b.lengthSq()-scale2)>tolerance||Math.abs(c.lengthSq()-scale2)>tolerance||Math.abs(a.dot(b))>tolerance||Math.abs(a.dot(c))>tolerance||Math.abs(b.dot(c))>tolerance||matrix.determinant()<=0||e[3]!==0||e[7]!==0||e[11]!==0||e[15]!==1)throw new Error('Tree matrix requires rotation and positive uniform scene-unit scale');
            // Phase stays representable as Float32 even for a caller's very
            // large finite offset; the absolute universe date stays BigInt.
            const tau=2*Math.PI;phaseOffset=((phaseOffset%tau)+tau)%tau;
            const scale=Math.fround(heightM/TREE_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_M),offset=Math.fround(phaseOffset);
            const newSlot=index===meshes[0].count;
            if(newSlot&&forest&&(forest.getX(index)!==1||forest.getY(index)!==0)){
              forest.setXY(index,1,0);forestUpdates.include(index,2);forestUpdates.flush(forest);
            }
            if(tree.getX(index)!==scale||tree.getY(index)!==offset){
              tree.setXY(index,scale,offset);treeUpdates.include(index,2);treeUpdates.flush(tree);
            }
            for(const [part,mesh] of meshes.entries()){
              const color=mesh.instanceColor!;
              if(newSlot&&(color.getX(index)!==1||color.getY(index)!==1||color.getZ(index)!==1)){

              }

              }
              mesh.count=Math.max(mesh.count,index+1);
            }
          },
          /** Linear RGB multipliers over the authored bark and leaf textures.
           * Each part keeps its shared material; only this slot's color changes.
           * Fresh or re-grown slots are white. Call when a retained slot changes tree identity;
           * transform/rebase updates deliberately retain its current colors. */
          setAppearance(index:number,bark:readonly[number,number,number],foliage:readonly[number,number,number]){
            const colors=[bark,foliage];
            if(batchDisposed||!Number.isInteger(index)||index<0||index>=meshes[0].count||colors.some(color=>!Array.isArray(color)||color.length!==3||[0,1,2].some(i=>!Number.isFinite(color[i])||color[i]<0||color[i]>2)))throw new Error('Tree appearance requires an initialized slot and linear RGB multipliers in [0,2]');
            for(const [part,mesh] of meshes.entries()){
              const attribute=mesh.instanceColor!,color=colors[part],base=index*3;
              if(color.some((value,component)=>attribute.array[base+component]!==Math.fround(value))){
                attribute.setXYZ(index,...color);colorUpdates[part].include(index,3);colorUpdates[part].flush(attribute);
              }
            }
          },
          setCoverage(index:number,coverage:number,seed:number){
            if(batchDisposed||!forest||!Number.isInteger(index)||index<0||index>=meshes[0].count||!Number.isFinite(coverage)||coverage<0||coverage>1||!Number.isInteger(seed)||seed<0||seed>255)throw new Error('Invalid canonical forest coverage');
            const value=quantizeForestCoverage(coverage)/256;
            if(forest.getX(index)!==value||forest.getY(index)!==seed){forest.setXY(index,value,seed);forestUpdates.include(index,2);forestUpdates.flush(forest);}
          },
          dispose(){if(batchDisposed)return;batchDisposed=true;activeBatches--;for(const mesh of meshes){mesh.geometry.dispose();mesh.customDepthMaterial?.dispose();mesh.customDistanceMaterial?.dispose();mesh.dispose();}group.clear();group.removeFromParent();},
        };
      },
      dispose(){if(disposed)return;if(activeBatches)throw new Error('Dispose tree batches before their family');disposed=true;dispose();},
    };
  }catch(error){dispose();throw error;}
}

function probe(direction:readonly number[],representation:'mesh'|'capture',pixels=128,supersample=1,channel:'coverage'|'depth'|'colour'='coverage'){
  if(!capture||!impostor||!batch)throw new Error('Load a capture first');

  view.up.set(...(Math.abs(toward.y)>.999?[0,0,1]:[0,1,0]) as [number,number,number]);view.lookAt(center);view.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const resolution=pixels*supersample;
  const floating=channel!=='coverage';
  const target=new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(resolution,resolution,{depthBuffer:true,type:floating?THREE.FloatType:THREE.UnsignedByteType});target.samples=!floating&&supersample===1?4:0;
  const oldTarget=renderer.getRenderTarget(),oldMode=mode,background=scene.background,oldClear=new THREE.Color(),alpha=renderer.getClearAlpha();renderer.getClearColor(oldClear);
  const data=floating?new Float32Array(resolution*resolution*4):new Uint8Array(resolution*resolution*4);
  try{
    setMode(representation);mask.value=channel==='coverage'?1:channel==='depth'?2:3;impostor.uniforms.captureMask.value=mask.value;scene.background=null;renderer.setClearColor(0,0);
    renderer.setRenderTarget(target);renderer.clear();renderer.render(scene,view);renderer.readRenderTargetPixels(target,0,0,resolution,resolution,data);
    const gl=renderer.getContext(),error=gl.getError();if(error!==gl.NO_ERROR)throw new Error(`Probe GL error ${error}`);
    if(supersample===1)return Array.from(data);
    const reduced=new Float32Array(pixels*pixels*4);
    for(let y=0;y<resolution;y++)for(let x=0;x<resolution;x++)for(let c=0;c<4;c++){
      const p=(y*resolution+x)*4;
      reduced[(Math.floor(y/supersample)*pixels+Math.floor(x/supersample))*4+c]+=data[p+c]*(floating&&c<3?data[p+3]:1)/(supersample*supersample);
    }

    return Array.from(reduced);
  }finally{renderer.setRenderTarget(oldTarget);renderer.setClearColor(oldClear,alpha);scene.background=background;mask.value=0;impostor.uniforms.captureMask.value=0;setMode(oldMode);target.dispose();}
}

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {ForestOrderedWorkIndex} from '../src/forest-ordered-work.ts';

const cell=(x,y=0)=>({id:`0/128/${x}/${y}`});
const patch=id=>{const [face,n,x,y]=id.split('/').map(Number);return `${face}/${n}/${Math.floor(x/8)}/${Math.floor(y/8)}`;};

test('active indices retain the original canonical order as distant patches become owned',()=>{
  const cells=[cell(0),cell(8),cell(1),cell(9),cell(16),cell(2),cell(17),cell(10)];
  const index=new ForestOrderedWorkIndex(patch);index.setSelection(cells);
  assert.deepEqual(index.updateSkipped(new Set()),cells.map((_,i)=>i));
  const first=index.diagnostics().rebuilds;
  const active=index.updateSkipped(new Set([patch(cells[1].id),patch(cells[4].id)]));
  assert.deepEqual(active,[0,2,5]);
  assert.deepEqual(active.map(i=>cells[i].id),[cells[0].id,cells[2].id,cells[5].id]);
  assert.deepEqual(index.newlySkippedIds(),[cells[1].id,cells[3].id,cells[7].id,cells[4].id,cells[6].id]);
  index.updateSkipped(new Set([patch(cells[1].id),patch(cells[4].id)]));
  assert.equal(index.diagnostics().rebuilds,first+1,'unchanged census readiness reuses the ordered list');
  assert.deepEqual(index.newlySkippedIds(),[]);
});

test('a census fallback reintroduces whole patches without changing source order',()=>{
  const cells=[cell(0),cell(8),cell(1),cell(9),cell(2)],index=new ForestOrderedWorkIndex(patch);
  index.setSelection(cells);
  index.updateSkipped(new Set([patch(cells[1].id)]));
  assert.deepEqual(index.updateSkipped(new Set()),[0,1,2,3,4]);
  assert.deepEqual(index.newlySkippedIds(),[],'a formerly skipped patch becoming unavailable does not clear unrelated state');
  assert.deepEqual(index.updateSkipped(new Set()),[0,1,2,3,4]);
  assert.equal(index.diagnostics().skippedCells,0);
});

test('selection replacement resets retained work and stale skip keys',()=>{
  const index=new ForestOrderedWorkIndex(patch);index.setSelection([cell(0),cell(8)]);
  index.updateSkipped(new Set([patch(cell(8).id)]));
  index.setSelection([cell(16),cell(1),cell(17)]);
  assert.deepEqual(index.updateSkipped(new Set()),[0,1,2]);
  assert.deepEqual(index.newlySkippedIds(),[]);
  assert.equal(index.diagnostics().skippedGroups,0);
});

test('incremental patch transitions match full canonical rebuilds without rebuilding the whole list',()=>{
  const cells=Array.from({length:2048},(_,i)=>cell(i%128,Math.floor(i/128)));
  const incremental=new ForestOrderedWorkIndex(patch),oracle=new ForestOrderedWorkIndex(patch);
  incremental.setSelection(cells);oracle.setSelection(cells);
  let skipped=new Set();incremental.updateSkipped(skipped);oracle.updateSkipped(skipped);
  const fullRebuilds=incremental.diagnostics().rebuilds;
  const changed=[patch(cells[17].id),patch(cells[700].id),patch(cells[1200].id)];
  for(const key of changed){
    skipped=new Set(skipped);skipped.add(key);
    incremental.updateSkippedPatches(new Map([[key,true]]));oracle.updateSkipped(skipped);
    assert.deepEqual(incremental.currentIndices(),oracle.currentIndices());
    assert.deepEqual(incremental.newlySkippedIds(),cells.filter(c=>patch(c.id)===key).map(c=>c.id));
  }
  for(const key of [...changed].reverse()){
    skipped=new Set(skipped);skipped.delete(key);
    incremental.updateSkippedPatches(new Map([[key,false]]));oracle.updateSkipped(skipped);
    assert.deepEqual(incremental.currentIndices(),oracle.currentIndices());
    assert.deepEqual(incremental.newlySkippedIds(),[]);
  }
  assert.equal(incremental.diagnostics().rebuilds,fullRebuilds,'patch transitions avoid full canonical list scans');
  assert.equal(incremental.diagnostics().incrementalUpdates,changed.length*2);
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {ForestPatchBroadphase,FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_MARGIN_M} from '../src/forest-patch-broadphase.ts';
import {forestCellPatchKey} from '../src/planet-canopy.ts';

const keyFor=forestCellPatchKey;
const cell=(x,y=0,face=0,n=128)=>({id:`${face}/${n}/${x}/${y}`,direction:[1,0,0],roll:.2,heightM:16,yaw:0,distanceM:0});
const roots=(cells,groundHeightM=0,water=0)=>new Map(cells.map(c=>[c.id,{groundHeightM,water}]));
const broad=(cells,resolved,water=0,radius=6371000)=>{const index=new ForestPatchBroadphase(radius,keyFor);index.setSelection(cells,water,c=>resolved.get(c.id));return index;};
const oracle=(cells,resolved,water,eye,current,threshold=650)=>{
  const groups=new Map();for(const c of cells){const key=keyFor(c.id);let g=groups.get(key);if(!g)groups.set(key,g=[]);g.push(c);}
  const out=new Set();for(const [key,members] of groups){if(!current(key))continue;
    const points=[];for(const c of members){const root=resolved.get(c.id);if(!root||root.water!==water){points.length=0;break;}
      const r=6371000+root.groundHeightM;points.push([c.direction[0]*r,c.direction[1]*r,c.direction[2]*r]);}
    if(points.length!==members.length)continue;
    if(points.every(p=>Math.hypot(eye[0]-p[0],eye[1]-p[1],eye[2]-p[2])>=threshold+FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_MARGIN_M))out.add(key);
  }return out;
};
const asSet=x=>[...x].sort();

function fixture(){
  const cells=[];
  for(let y=0;y<16;y++)for(let x=0;x<16;x++){
    const angle=(x<8?0:.002)+(y<8?0:.002);cells.push({...cell(x,y),direction:[Math.cos(angle),Math.sin(angle),0]});
  }
  return cells;
}

test('group skip agrees with per-cell oracle across movement, patch validity and global cell order',()=>{
  const cells=fixture(),resolved=roots(cells),index=broad(cells,resolved),current=key=>key.endsWith('/0/0')||key.endsWith('/1/0');
  for(const eye of [[6371000+1000,0,0],[6371000+400,0,0],[6371000-900,0,0],[0,6371000+1200,0]]){
    const actual=index.skipPatchKeys(eye,0,current),expected=oracle(cells,resolved,0,eye,current);
    assert.deepEqual(asSet(actual),asSet(expected));
    const remaining=cells.filter(c=>!actual.has(keyFor(c.id)));
    assert.deepEqual(remaining.map(c=>c.id),cells.filter(c=>!expected.has(keyFor(c.id))).map(c=>c.id),
      'unskipped cells preserve canonical input iteration order');
  }
});

test('partial, stale-water, invalidated-hydrology and unavailable-census groups always fall back',()=>{
  const cells=fixture(),resolved=roots(cells),eye=[6371000+3000,0,0],all=()=>true;
  for(const kind of ['partial','water','hydrology','census']){
    const values=new Map(resolved),affected=keyFor(cells[0].id);
    if(kind==='partial')for(const c of cells)if(keyFor(c.id)===affected)values.delete(c.id);
    if(kind==='water')for(const c of cells)if(keyFor(c.id)===affected)values.set(c.id,{groundHeightM:0,water:5});
    const index=broad(cells,values,0);if(kind==='hydrology')index.invalidate();
    const current=kind==='census'?()=>false:all;
    const skipped=index.skipPatchKeys(eye,0,current);
    if(kind==='partial'||kind==='water')assert.equal(skipped.has(affected),false,`${kind} patch must retain full per-cell fallback`);
    else assert.equal(skipped.size,0,`${kind} invalidation keeps all patches on fallback`);
    assert.ok(index.diagnostics().lastFullScan.invalidatedGroups>0||kind==='census');
  }
});

test('water changes, habitat replacements, and later arrivals rebuild conservative bounds before skipping',()=>{
  const cells=fixture(),values=roots(cells),index=broad(cells,values),eye=[6371000+3000,0,0],all=()=>true;
  assert.ok(index.skipPatchKeys(eye,0,all).size>0);
  index.setWater(10);assert.equal(index.skipPatchKeys(eye,10,all).size,0);
  for(const c of cells)index.updateResident(c.id,{groundHeightM:0,water:10});
  assert.deepEqual(asSet(index.skipPatchKeys(eye,10,all)),asSet(oracle(cells,roots(cells,0,10),10,eye,all)));
  // Moving one root outward forces a conservative bound rebuild; it may expand
  // but can never produce an unsafe skip. Returning it restores exact oracle.
  index.updateResident(cells[0].id,{groundHeightM:2500,water:10});
  const moved=new Map(roots(cells,0,10));moved.set(cells[0].id,{groundHeightM:2500,water:10});
  assert.deepEqual(asSet(index.skipPatchKeys(eye,10,all)),asSet(oracle(cells,moved,10,eye,all)));
  index.invalidate();assert.equal(index.skipPatchKeys(eye,10,all).size,0);
  for(const c of cells)index.updateResident(c.id,{groundHeightM:0,water:10});
  assert.ok(index.skipPatchKeys(eye,10,all).size>0,'a fully refreshed group can be skipped again');
});

test('selection replacement drops old keys and a census arriving after a miss enables only fresh skips',()=>{
  const cells=fixture(),values=roots(cells),index=broad(cells,values),eye=[6371000+2000,0,0];
  assert.equal(index.skipPatchKeys(eye,0,()=>false).size,0);
  assert.ok(index.skipPatchKeys(eye,0,()=>true).size>0,'late current census makes an otherwise-ready group skippable');
  const replacement=cells.filter(c=>Number(c.id.split('/')[2])>=8);
  index.setSelection(replacement,0,c=>values.get(c.id));
  const current=index.skipPatchKeys(eye,0,()=>true);
  assert.ok(current.size>0);assert.ok([...current].every(key=>replacement.some(c=>keyFor(c.id)===key)));
  assert.equal(index.patchKeyForCell(cells[0].id),undefined);
});

test('invalid inputs fail closed and diagnostics report bounds readiness',()=>{
  assert.throws(()=>new ForestPatchBroadphase(NaN,keyFor));
  const cells=fixture(),values=roots(cells),index=broad(cells,values),eye=[6371000+2000,0,0];
  assert.throws(()=>index.skipPatchKeys([NaN,0,0],0,()=>true));
  assert.throws(()=>index.skipPatchKeys(eye,0,()=>true,-1));
  const keys=index.skipPatchKeys(eye,0,()=>true);assert.ok(keys.size>0);
  const stats=index.diagnostics();assert.equal(stats.lastFullScan.readyGroups,stats.groups);assert.equal(stats.lastFullScan.skippedGroups,keys.size);assert.ok(stats.lastFullScan.skippedCells>0);
});

test('distance boundary uses the conservative margin and never skips an inside group',()=>{
  const c=cell(0),index=broad([c],roots([c])),current=()=>true,radius=6371000;
  const inside=[radius+650+FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_MARGIN_M-.01,0,0];
  const outside=[radius+650+FOREST_PATCH_BROADPHASE_MARGIN_M+.01,0,0];
  assert.equal(index.skipPatchKeys(inside,0,current).size,0);
  assert.equal(index.skipPatchKeys(outside,0,current).size,1);
});

test('local resident edits re-evaluate only their patch and stale roots become non-skippable',()=>{
  const cells=fixture(),values=roots(cells),index=broad(cells,values),eye=[6371000+3000,0,0],all=()=>true;
  const before=index.skipPatchKeys(eye,0,all),affected=keyFor(cells[0].id);
  assert.ok(before.has(affected));
  index.updateResident(cells[0].id,{groundHeightM:3000,water:0});values.set(cells[0].id,{groundHeightM:3000,water:0});
  const dirty=index.dirtyGroups();assert.deepEqual(dirty,[affected]);
  const states=index.skipStatesFor(dirty,eye,0,all);
  assert.deepEqual([...states.keys()],[affected]);assert.equal(states.get(affected),false);
  const after=new Set(before);for(const [key,skip] of states)skip?after.add(key):after.delete(key);
  assert.deepEqual(asSet(after),asSet(oracle(cells,values,0,eye,all)));
  index.acknowledgeDirtyGroups(dirty);assert.deepEqual(index.dirtyGroups(),[]);

  index.updateResident(cells[0].id,null);
  const unresolved=index.skipStatesFor(index.dirtyGroups(),eye,0,all);
  assert.equal(unresolved.get(affected),false,'a missing root immediately restores per-cell fallback');
  index.setWater(5);
  assert.equal(index.skipStatesFor([affected],eye,5,all).get(affected),false,'stale-water roots never skip');
});

import {test,after} from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {createServer} from 'vite';

const vite=await createServer({configFile:false,optimizeDeps:{noDiscovery:true},server:{middlewareMode:true,hmr:false,ws:false},appType:'custom'});
const {PlanetCanopy}=await vite.ssrLoadModule('/src/planet-canopy.ts');
const {galaxies,refByAddress,system}=await vite.ssrLoadModule('/src/catalog.ts');

test('patch invalidation snapshots acknowledge only versions actually consumed',()=>{
  const canopy=new PlanetCanopy(body);
  try{
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers('0/128/0/0');
    const first=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    assert.deepEqual(first.keys.map(([key])=>key),['0/128/0/0']);
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers('0/128/0/0');
    canopy.acknowledgeCurrentPatchChanges(first);
    const newer=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    assert.equal(newer.keys.length,1,'a later edit of the same key survives the older acknowledgement');
    assert.ok(newer.keys[0][1]>first.keys[0][1]);
    canopy.acknowledgeCurrentPatchChanges(newer);
    assert.deepEqual(canopy.currentPatchChanges().keys,[]);
  }finally{canopy.dispose();}
});

test('global invalidation advances the epoch and supersedes pending local keys',()=>{
  const canopy=new PlanetCanopy(body);
  try{
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers('0/128/0/0');
    const old=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers();
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers('1/128/0/0');
    canopy.acknowledgeCurrentPatchChanges(old);
    const current=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    assert.ok(current.globalRevision>old.globalRevision);
    assert.deepEqual(current.keys.map(([key])=>key),['1/128/0/0']);
  }finally{canopy.dispose();}
});

test('an unconsumed local-key journal is bounded by escalating to a full-reset epoch',()=>{
  const canopy=new PlanetCanopy(body);
  try{
    const initial=canopy.currentPatchChanges().globalRevision;
    for(let i=0;i<8193;i++)canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers(`0/128/${i}/0`);
    const overflow=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    assert.ok(overflow.globalRevision>initial,'overflow requests a safe global refresh');
    assert.equal(overflow.keys.length,0,'the global reset replaces an unbounded per-key backlog');
    assert.equal(overflow.revision,8193);
  }finally{canopy.dispose();}
});


SOURCE tests/planet-canopy.test.mjs bytes=47123 sha256=0afc82d2b91616d373aafd2c8af19ab183e73486a89fc2446ab4f7c0b741c8d0
import {test} from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import {PlanetCanopy} from '../src/planet-canopy.ts';
import {LEGACY_TREE_BOUND} from '../src/tree-shape.ts';
import {galaxies,refByAddress,system} from '../src/catalog.ts';
import {PLANET_DETAIL} from '../src/planet-detail.ts';

const body=system(refByAddress(galaxies('LETHRA-001')[0],'G001/C2128,0,1/0')).planets[1];
const ground=[1,0,0],eye=new THREE.Vector3(1,0,0),nearOrigin=new THREE.Vector3();
const contextA='a'.repeat(64),contextB='b'.repeat(64);
const familyA='f'.repeat(64),familyB='c'.repeat(64);
import {packForestPresentation} from '../src/forest-presentation.ts';

class MockWorker{
  static instances=[];
  onmessage=null;onerror=null;onmessageerror=null;sent=[];terminated=false;
  constructor(url,options){this.url=url;this.options=options;MockWorker.instances.push(this);}
  postMessage(message){this.sent.push(message);}
  emit(data){this.onmessage?.({data});}
  error(){this.onerror?.({preventDefault(){}});}
  terminate(){this.terminated=true;}
}

function address(i=0,n=Math.ceil(2*body.radiusM/18)){
  return {face:0,n,x:1000+i,y:1000};
}
function recordBuffer(count){
  const out=new Float64Array(count*9);
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++)out.set([i,1,0,0,.25,16,.5,120,0],i*9);
  return out;
}
function patch(a,{present=1,candidates=64,testedCells=64,context=contextA,records=recordBuffer(present),waterInterval}={}){
  return {context,address:{...a},testedCells,candidates,present,representatives:0,records,...(waterInterval?{waterInterval}: {})};
}
function begin(capacity=256,canopyBody=body){
  const previousWorker=globalThis.Worker;MockWorker.instances=[];globalThis.Worker=MockWorker;
  const host=new PlanetCanopy(canopyBody,capacity);host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
  const worker=MockWorker.instances[0],configure=worker.sent.find(m=>m.type==='configure');
  assert.ok(worker&&configure,'prepare starts and configures a census worker');
  const generation=configure.generation,n=configure.body?Math.ceil(2*configure.body.radiusM/18):Math.ceil(2*body.radiusM/18);
  worker.emit({type:'ready',generation,context:contextA,family:familyA,n});
  host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
  const select=worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='select');assert.ok(select,'ready host requests a patch selection');
  return {host,worker,generation,n,select,context:contextA,restore(){host.dispose();globalThis.Worker=previousWorker;}};
}
function selectAndRequest(h,addresses){
  h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:h.generation,requestId:h.select.requestId,context:h.context,
    packed:Float64Array.from(addresses.flatMap(a=>[a.face,a.n,a.x,a.y])),selectionMs:1});
  h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
  const request=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='patches');assert.ok(request,'selection response starts a bounded patch request');
  return request;
}
function sendPatchResponse(h,request,patches,done=true){
  h.worker.emit({type:'patches',generation:h.generation,requestId:request.requestId,context:h.context,patches,done});
}

test('dated family retag preserves roots and requires matching appearance and canonical height for handoff',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`,id=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`;
  try{
    const oak=packForestPresentation('branching',.95,1.05),pine=packForestPresentation('spire',1.04,.96);
    let calls=0;h.host.setPresentation('catalogue-A',(resolved,height)=>{calls++;assert.equal(resolved,id);assert.equal(height,16);return oak;},new Map());
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a)]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);h.host.finish();
    const index=h.host.slots.get(id),root=h.host.renderer.positions.array.slice(),rotation=h.host.renderer.rotations.array.slice();
    assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,120,key,16,oak),true);
    assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,120,key,17,oak),false);
    assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,121,key,16,oak),false);
    assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,120,key,16,pine),false);
    const settledCalls=calls;h.host.canHandoff(id,120,key,16,oak);assert.equal(calls,settledCalls,'stationary handoff reuses the slot tag');
    h.host.setPresentation('catalogue-B',()=>pine,new Map());
    assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,120,key,16,oak),false,'on-demand retag precedes near pairing');
    assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,120,key,16,pine),true);
    assert.equal(h.host.renderer.parameters.getZ(index),pine);
    assert.deepEqual(h.host.renderer.positions.array,root);assert.deepEqual(h.host.renderer.rotations.array,rotation);
    h.host.setPresentation('basic',()=>0,new Map());assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,120,key,16,0),true);
    assert.equal(h.host.renderer.parameters.getZ(index),0);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('presentation replacement validates before adoption and rapid dates preserve the retag cursor',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n);
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a,{present:3})]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const oak=packForestPresentation('branching',1,1),pine=packForestPresentation('spire',1,1);
    h.host.setPresentation('A',()=>oak,new Map());
    const iterator=h.host.presentationRetag,first=iterator.next().value;h.host.tagPresentation(...first);
    h.host.setPresentation('B',()=>pine,new Map());
    assert.equal(h.host.presentationRetag,iterator,'changing date cannot restart from the first slot');
    const second=iterator.next().value;assert.notEqual(second[0],first[0]);h.host.tagPresentation(...second);
    const before=h.host.diagnostics().presentation;
    assert.throws(()=>h.host.setPresentation('invalid',()=>0,new Map([['spire',{enabled:true}]])));
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().presentation.key,before.key);
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().presentation.version,before.version);
    assert.equal(h.host.presentationRetag,iterator);
    for(let frame=0;frame<6&&h.host.presentationRetag;frame++)h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.presentationRetag,null,'a stable final snapshot completes a follow-up pass');
    for(const index of h.host.slots.values())assert.equal(h.host.renderer.parameters.getZ(index),pine);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('selection requests and queued census results stay bounded and installs are frame budgeted',()=>{
  const h=begin(4096),addresses=Array.from({length:70},(_,i)=>address(i,h.n));
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,addresses);
    assert.ok(request.addresses.length>0&&request.addresses.length<=64,'host requests no more than 64 whole patches per bounded slice');
    for(let batch=0;batch<Math.ceil(request.addresses.length/8);batch++){
      const patches=request.addresses.slice(batch*8,batch*8+8).map(a=>patch(a,{present:64}));
      sendPatchResponse(h,request,patches,batch===Math.ceil(request.addresses.length/8)-1);
      assert.ok(h.host.pending.length<=64);
    }
    assert.equal(h.host.pending.length,request.addresses.length,'incomplete/queued batches retain only requested complete patches');
    assert.equal(h.host.installed.size,0,'message receipt alone never installs a patch');
    let frames=0;
    while(h.host.pending.length&&frames++<20){
      h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
      assert.ok(h.host.installedThisFrame<=512,'installation respects the per-frame tree budget');
      assert.ok(h.host.pending.length<=64);
    }
    assert.equal(h.host.pending.length,0);assert.equal(h.host.slots.size,request.addresses.length*64);
    assert.equal(h.host.installed.size,request.addresses.length);
    const nextRequest=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='patches');
    assert.equal(nextRequest.addresses.length,addresses.length-request.addresses.length,
      'the remaining selection is requested only after the first bounded batch drains');
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('current-patch revision tracks ownership/readiness changes but not stationary queries',()=>{
  const h=begin(4096),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    const ready=h.host.currentPatchRevision();
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),false);
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatchRevision(),ready,'querying ownership is read-only');
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);
    sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a)]);h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const installed=h.host.currentPatchRevision();
    assert.ok(installed>ready,'installing a complete current patch invalidates cached decisions');
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatchRevision(),installed,'stationary prepare with unchanged ownership does not bump revision');
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,5,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.ok(h.host.currentPatchRevision()>installed,'a water reconfiguration invalidates current-patch decisions');
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),false);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('large selection decoding is sliced and atomically swaps the old patch window',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),oldKey=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:h.generation,requestId:h.select.requestId,context:h.context,
      packed:Float64Array.from([a.face,a.n,a.x,a.y]),selectionMs:1});
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.deepEqual(h.host.order,[oldKey]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const nextSelect=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='select');assert.notEqual(nextSelect.requestId,h.select.requestId);
    const addresses=Array.from({length:1300},(_,i)=>address(i+1,h.n));
    const packed=Float64Array.from(addresses.flatMap(a=>[a.face,a.n,a.x,a.y]));
    h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:h.generation,requestId:nextSelect.requestId,context:h.context,packed,selectionMs:3});
    h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().selectionApplyPending,true,'first bounded slice leaves application in progress');
    assert.deepEqual(h.host.order,[oldKey],'new window is not visible until its complete packed selection validates');
    h.host.selection.started=performance.now()-11000;
    h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.worker.terminated,false,'budgeted local decoding is not mistaken for a hung worker response');
    assert.equal(h.host.timeouts,0);
    let frames=0;
    while(h.host.diagnostics().selectionApplyPending&&frames++<10)h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.ok(frames<10);assert.equal(h.host.order.length,addresses.length);
    assert.equal(h.host.order[0],`${addresses[0].face}/${addresses[0].n}/${addresses[0].x}/${addresses[0].y}`);
    assert.equal(h.host.order.at(-1),`${addresses.at(-1).face}/${addresses.at(-1).n}/${addresses.at(-1).x}/${addresses.at(-1).y}`);
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().selectionApplyPending,false);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('old patch replies cannot install after the replacement address scan has completed',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),oldKey=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    const oldRequest=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);
    h.host.retries.clear();
    for(let i=0;i<900;i++)h.host.retries.set(`stale-${i}`,{attempts:1,after:0});
    h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const selection=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='select');
    const replacements=Array.from({length:1300},(_,i)=>address(i+1,h.n));
    h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:h.generation,requestId:selection.requestId,context:h.context,
      packed:Float64Array.from(replacements.flatMap(x=>[x.face,x.n,x.x,x.y])),selectionMs:1});
    let frames=0;
    while(h.host.selectionApply?.phase==='addresses'&&frames++<10)h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.ok(frames<10);assert.equal(h.host.selectionApply?.phase,'retries','large stale retry cleanup keeps the candidate set private across frames');
    h.worker.emit({type:'patches',generation:h.generation,requestId:oldRequest.requestId,context:h.context,
      patches:[patch(a)],done:true});
    h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.installed.has(oldKey),false,'the prior selection result is discarded once the new address scan completed');
    let applyFrames=0;while(h.host.selectionApply&&applyFrames++<10)h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.ok(applyFrames<10);
    assert.equal(h.host.order.includes(oldKey),false);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('malformed deferred selection terminates before replacing the current window',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),oldKey=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:h.generation,requestId:h.select.requestId,context:h.context,
      packed:Float64Array.from([a.face,a.n,a.x,a.y]),selectionMs:1});
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.deepEqual(h.host.order,[oldKey]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const selection=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='select');
    const invalidX=Math.ceil(h.n/8);
    h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:h.generation,requestId:selection.requestId,context:h.context,
      packed:Float64Array.from([0,h.n,invalidX,1000]),selectionMs:1});
    assert.deepEqual(h.host.order,[oldKey],'message callback queues decoding without changing the public window');
    h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.worker.terminated,true,'address validation happens in the budgeted apply phase');
    assert.deepEqual(h.host.order,[oldKey],'partial/malformed input cannot publish a replacement window');
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().selectionApplyPending,false);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('only structurally complete patches install; validated empty patches remain known',()=>{

  }finally{e.restore();}
});

});

test('near ownership, absence suppression, and reused-slot previous IDs resolve in one frame',()=>{
  const h=begin(1),a=address(0,h.n),b=address(1,h.n),keyA=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a)]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const idA=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`,slotA=h.host.slots.get(idA);
    assert.equal(slotA,0);assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(keyA),true);assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(idA,120),true);
    h.host.own(idA,.5);h.host.finish();assert.equal(h.host.renderer.ownership.array[slotA],128,'near and far ownership commit in the same frame');

    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    h.host.noteNear(idA,keyA,false,0);h.host.finish();
    assert.equal(h.host.renderer.ownership.array[slotA],256,'near absence suppresses stale far presence');

    const keyB=`${b.face}/${b.n}/${b.x}/${b.y}`,idB=`${b.face}/${b.n}/${b.x*8}/${b.y*8}`;
    h.host.desired.set(keyB,b);h.host.remove(keyA);h.host.pending.push(patch(b));
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const slotB=h.host.slots.get(idB);assert.equal(slotB,slotA,'released storage is reused');
    assert.equal(h.host.renderer.ownership.array[slotB],0,'reused slot clears stale ownership');
    h.host.own(idB,200/256);h.host.finish();
    assert.equal(h.host.renderer.ownership.array[slotB],200,'the previous frame ID cannot reset ownership of a reused slot');
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('caches current-patch ownership lookups only within one prepare transaction',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a)]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const installed=h.host.installed,originalGet=installed.get.bind(installed);let reads=0;
    installed.get=key=>{reads++;return originalGet(key);};
    const frameReads=reads;
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true);assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true);
    h.host.noteNear(`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`,key,true,0);
    assert.equal(reads,frameReads+1,'repeated direct and near ownership checks share one patch-map lookup');
    assert.deepEqual(h.host.diagnostics().patchLookupCache,{hits:2,misses:1,keys:1});

    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const nextFrameReads=reads;
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true);assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true);
    assert.equal(reads,nextFrameReads+1,'the next prepare transaction refreshes the patch answer');

    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,10,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const waterReads=reads;
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),false,'old-water patches cannot reuse the previous frame true value');
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),false);
    assert.equal(reads,waterReads+1);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('a census arrival replaces a cached miss at the next frame boundary',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`,id=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`;
  try{
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),false);
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),false);
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a)]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true,'the new prepare transaction sees the complete installed census patch');
    h.host.noteNear(id,key,false,0);h.host.finish();
    assert.equal(h.host.renderer.ownership.array[h.host.slots.get(id)],256,'near absence ownership still applies with cached patch validity');
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('water reconfiguration ignores stale generation replies and accepts the new context',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n);
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,10,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const config2=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='configure');assert.equal(config2.generation,h.generation+1);
    h.worker.emit({type:'patches',generation:h.generation,requestId:request.requestId,context:h.context,patches:[patch(a)],done:true});
    assert.equal(h.host.pending.length,0);assert.equal(h.host.installed.size,0);
    h.worker.emit({type:'ready',generation:config2.generation,context:contextB,n:h.n});
    h.host.prepare(eye,[0,1,0],100,10,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const selection2=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='select');assert.equal(selection2.generation,config2.generation);
    const previousOrder=h.host.order.slice();
    h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:h.generation,requestId:selection2.requestId,context:contextA,packed:new Float64Array(),selectionMs:0});
    assert.deepEqual(h.host.order,previousOrder,'stale selection data cannot replace current-water patches');
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().selectionPending,true);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('matching family retags an in-range patch only after ready without rewriting its slot',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`,id=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`;
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a,{waterInterval:[-1,10]})]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const slot=h.host.slots.get(id),position=Array.from(h.host.renderer.positions.array.slice(slot*4,slot*4+4));
    let writes=0;const write=h.host.renderer.write.bind(h.host.renderer);h.host.renderer.write=(...args)=>{writes++;return write(...args);};
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,5,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const configure=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='configure');
    assert.equal(h.host.installed.get(key).water,0,'configuration alone does not retag installed content');
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),false);
    assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,120),true,'canonical geometry remains pairable during a water reconfiguration');
    h.worker.emit({type:'ready',generation:configure.generation,context:contextB,family:familyA,n:h.n});
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,5,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true);
    assert.equal(h.host.installed.get(key).water,5);
    assert.equal(h.host.reusedPatches,1);
    assert.equal(h.host.slots.get(id),slot);assert.equal(writes,0,'interval reuse does not rewrite the unchanged slot');
    assert.deepEqual(Array.from(h.host.renderer.positions.array.slice(slot*4,slot*4+4)),position);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('outside-interval and different-family patches stay stale and are requested again',()=>{
  for(const family of [familyA,familyB]){
    const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
    try{
      const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a,{waterInterval:[-1,10]})]);
      h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
      h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,family===familyA?20:5,true,false,nearOrigin);
      const configure=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='configure');
      h.worker.emit({type:'ready',generation:configure.generation,context:contextB,family,n:h.n});
      h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,family===familyA?20:5,true,false,nearOrigin);
      assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),false);
      assert.equal(h.host.installed.get(key).water,0);
      assert.equal(h.host.reusedPatches,0);
      h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,family===familyA?20:5,true,false,nearOrigin);
      const replacement=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='patches');
      assert.ok(replacement?.addresses.some(a=>`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`===key),
        family===familyA?'outside the stored interval requests a fresh census':'a changed family hash requests a fresh census');
    }finally{h.restore();}
  }
});

test('near absence during a water shift still suppresses the paired far instance',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`,id=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`;
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a,{waterInterval:[-1,10]})]);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const slot=h.host.slots.get(id);assert.equal(h.host.renderer.ownership.array[slot],0);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,5,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),false,'old patch waits for the matching-family ready message');
    h.host.noteNear(id,key,false,5);h.host.finish();
    assert.equal(h.host.renderer.ownership.array[slot],256,'current near absence wins while canonical far geometry remains pairable');
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('water targets coalesce to one follow-up configure and water-only messages omit the body',()=>{
  const h=begin(64);
  try{
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,5,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const firstPending=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='configure');
    assert.equal(firstPending.waterLevelM,5);assert.equal(firstPending.body,undefined);
    assert.equal(firstPending.family,familyA);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,10,true,false,nearOrigin);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,20,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.worker.sent.filter(m=>m.type==='configure').length,2,'no parallel configure is sent while the first is unresolved');
    assert.equal(h.host.configuredWater,5);assert.equal(h.host.water,20,'the newest target remains queued locally');
    h.worker.emit({type:'ready',generation:firstPending.generation,context:contextB,family:familyA,n:h.n});
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,20,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const latest=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='configure');
    assert.equal(h.worker.sent.filter(m=>m.type==='configure').length,3,'latest target is configured only after readiness');
    assert.equal(latest.waterLevelM,20);assert.equal(latest.body,undefined);assert.equal(latest.family,familyA);
    assert.equal(h.host.configuredWater,20);assert.equal(h.host.ready,false);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('a lagging interval reply is validated against its configured water snapshot',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,5,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const configure=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='configure');
    h.worker.emit({type:'ready',generation:configure.generation,context:contextB,family:familyA,n:h.n});
    h.host.water=20;h.host.request={id:444,keys:new Set([key]),progressAt:performance.now()};
    h.host.receive({type:'patches',generation:configure.generation,requestId:444,context:contextB,
      patches:[patch(a,{context:contextB,waterInterval:[-1,10]})],done:true});
    assert.equal(h.host.pending.length,1,'the interval contains configured water 5 even though the target has advanced to 20');
    assert.equal(h.host.pending[0].waterInterval[1],10);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,20,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const next=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='configure');assert.equal(next.waterLevelM,20);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

  const h=begin(64,{...body});
  try{
    h.host.body.canonicalRivers=[];
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const configure=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='configure');
    assert.equal(configure.waterLevelM,0);assert.equal(configure.family,undefined);
    assert.equal(configure.body,h.host.body,'changed hydrology references are sent with the full canonical body');
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('close canonical crowns require same-frame ownership; holes and submerged trees do not count as forced closes',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a)]);h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const id=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`,slot=h.host.slots.get(id);assert.equal(slot,0);
    const root=new THREE.Vector3().fromArray(h.host.bodyPositions,slot*3),rotation=new THREE.Quaternion().fromArray(h.host.renderer.rotations.array,slot*4);
    const localBound=new THREE.Vector3(...LEGACY_TREE_BOUND.center).multiplyScalar(16).applyQuaternion(rotation);
    const closeEye=root.add(localBound).multiplyScalar(1/body.radiusM);

    h.host.prepare(closeEye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);h.host.observeClose(id,closeEye);h.host.finish();
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().farForcedClose,1,'an unowned close crown is counted');
    h.host.prepare(closeEye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);h.host.observeClose(id,closeEye);h.host.own(id,1);h.host.finish();
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().farForcedClose,0,'near ownership applied in this frame prevents a forced far close');
    h.host.prepare(closeEye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);h.host.observeClose(id,closeEye);h.host.noteNear(id,key,false,0);h.host.finish();
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().nearAbsentInCensus,1);assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().farForcedClose,0,'near absence suppresses the far copy');
    assert.ok(body.water>0,'fixture needs a water-bearing body for submerged filtering');
    h.host.prepare(closeEye,ground,100,200,true,false,nearOrigin);h.host.observeClose(id,closeEye);h.host.finish();
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().farForcedClose,0,'submerged roots are excluded from close checks');
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('worker failure retries once then disables the full canopy for legacy near rendering',()=>{
  const h=begin(64);
  try{
    h.worker.error();assert.equal(h.worker.terminated,true);assert.equal(h.host.workerRestarts,1);
    const retry=MockWorker.instances.at(-1);assert.notEqual(retry,h.worker);
    const restartConfig=retry.sent.find(m=>m.type==='configure');assert.equal(restartConfig.body,h.host.body,'worker restart reconstructs from the canonical full body');
    assert.equal(restartConfig.family,undefined);
    retry.error();
    assert.equal(retry.terminated,true);assert.equal(h.host.workerRestarts,1);assert.equal(h.host.failures,2);
    const count=MockWorker.instances.length;assert.equal(h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin),false);
    assert.equal(MockWorker.instances.length,count,'restart limit is bounded');assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().terminalFailure,true);
    assert.equal(h.host.group.visible,false,'the failed far canopy stays hidden');assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff('missing/0/0/0',12),false);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('hung configure, selection, and request each restart once then disable far ownership',()=>{
  const cases=['configure','selection','request'];
  for(const phase of cases){
    const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),b=address(1,h.n),keyA=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
    try{
      let handoffId='missing/0/0/0',height=12;
      if(phase==='configure'){
        h.host.ready=false;h.host.selection=null;h.host.configuredAt=performance.now()-10001;
      }else if(phase==='selection'){
        h.host.selection.started=performance.now()-10001;
      }else{
        const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a,b]);
        h.worker.emit({type:'patches',generation:h.generation,requestId:request.requestId,context:h.context,
          patches:[patch(a)],done:false});
        h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
        const id=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`;handoffId=id;
        height=h.host.heights[h.host.slots.get(id)];assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(keyA),true);
        h.host.request.progressAt=performance.now()-10001;
      }
      h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
      assert.equal(MockWorker.instances.length,2,`${phase}: first timeout restarts the worker once`);
      assert.equal(h.host.workerRestarts,1);assert.equal(h.host.terminalFailure,false);
      const restarted=MockWorker.instances.at(-1);assert.ok(restarted.sent.some(m=>m.type==='configure'));
      h.host.configuredAt=performance.now()-10001;
      h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
      assert.equal(MockWorker.instances.length,2,`${phase}: second timeout is terminal, without another restart`);
      assert.equal(h.host.timeouts,2);assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().terminalFailure,true);
      assert.equal(h.host.group.visible,false,'terminal failure disables old far instances');
      assert.equal(h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin),false,'caller keeps the legacy near path');
      assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(keyA),false);assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(handoffId,height),false,'terminal far state cannot take near ownership');
    }finally{h.restore();}
  }
});

test('valid partial patch progress refreshes the request watchdog timestamp',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),b=address(1,h.n);
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a,b]),old=performance.now()-10001;
    h.host.request.progressAt=old;
    h.worker.emit({type:'patches',generation:h.generation,requestId:request.requestId,context:h.context,patches:[patch(a)],done:false});
    assert.ok(h.host.request.progressAt>old);assert.ok(performance.now()-h.host.request.progressAt<1000);
    assert.equal(h.host.request.keys.size,1,'the response made valid partial progress');
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

  const dry=begin(64,{...body,water:0});
  try{

    for(let i=0;i<4;i++)assert.equal(dry.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin),true);
    assert.equal(dry.host.generation,generation);assert.equal(dry.worker.sent.filter(m=>m.type==='configure').length,configures);
