});

test('canopy prefetches while hidden between prepare and streaming altitude thresholds',()=>{
  const h=begin(64);
  const a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`,id=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`;
  try{
    const high=PLANET_DETAIL.forestPrepareAltitudeM+1000;
    assert.ok(high<PLANET_DETAIL.forestStreamAltitudeM);
    assert.equal(h.host.prepare(eye,ground,high,0,true,false,nearOrigin),false,'prefetch stays visually hidden');
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().streaming,true);assert.equal(h.host.group.visible,false);
    h.worker.emit({type:'ready',generation:h.generation,context:contextA,n:h.n});
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,high,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const selection=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='select');assert.ok(selection);
    h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:h.generation,requestId:selection.requestId,context:contextA,
      packed:Float64Array.from([a.face,a.n,a.x,a.y]),selectionMs:0});
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,high,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const request=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='patches');assert.ok(request);
    sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a)]);h.host.prepare(eye,ground,high,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.installed.has(key),true,'prefetch installs the complete patch while hidden');
    assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,120),false,'hidden prefetch cannot take visible near ownership');
    assert.equal(h.host.prepare(eye,ground,PLANET_DETAIL.forestPrepareAltitudeM-100,0,true,false,nearOrigin),true);
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true);assert.equal(h.host.canHandoff(id,120),true,'the prepared patch becomes available when revealed');
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('canopy does not start or keep streaming above its altitude band',()=>{
  const previousWorker=globalThis.Worker;MockWorker.instances=[];globalThis.Worker=MockWorker;
  const host=new PlanetCanopy(body,64);
  try{
    assert.equal(host.prepare(eye,ground,PLANET_DETAIL.forestStreamAltitudeM+1,0,true,false,nearOrigin),false);
    assert.equal(host.diagnostics().streaming,false);assert.equal(MockWorker.instances.length,0,'high altitude does not launch background census work');
    assert.equal(host.prepare(eye,ground,PLANET_DETAIL.forestStreamAltitudeM-1,0,true,false,nearOrigin),false);
    assert.equal(host.diagnostics().streaming,true);assert.equal(MockWorker.instances.length,1,'worker starts on entering the prefetch band');
  }finally{host.dispose();globalThis.Worker=previousWorker;}
});

test('patch failures stop retrying after three attempts and an infinite wake does not spin',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    let request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);
    h.worker.emit({type:'error',phase:'request',generation:h.generation,requestId:request.requestId,context:h.context,error:'injected failure 1'});
    assert.equal(h.host.retries.get(key).attempts,1);
    h.host.retries.set(key,{attempts:2,after:0});h.host.retryWakeAt=0;
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    request=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='patches');assert.notEqual(request.requestId,undefined);
    h.worker.emit({type:'error',phase:'request',generation:h.generation,requestId:request.requestId,context:h.context,error:'injected failure 3'});
    assert.equal(h.host.retries.get(key).attempts,3);assert.equal(h.host.retries.get(key).after,Infinity);
    const count=h.worker.sent.filter(m=>m.type==='patches').length;
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.worker.sent.filter(m=>m.type==='patches').length,count,'a permanently backed-off patch does not spin');
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('a pending patch job cannot install results from the previous selection after a long move',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n),b=address(1,h.n);
  try{
    const first=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);
    const turn=5000/body.radiusM,newGround=[Math.cos(turn),Math.sin(turn),0],newEye=new THREE.Vector3(...newGround);
    h.host.prepare(newEye,newGround,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const secondSelection=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='select');assert.ok(secondSelection.requestId>h.select.requestId);
    h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:h.generation,requestId:secondSelection.requestId,context:h.context,
      packed:Float64Array.from([b.face,b.n,b.x,b.y]),selectionMs:1});
    h.host.prepare(newEye,newGround,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.deepEqual([...h.host.desired.keys()],[`${b.face}/${b.n}/${b.x}/${b.y}`]);
    h.worker.emit({type:'patches',generation:h.generation,requestId:first.requestId,context:h.context,patches:[patch(a)],done:true});

    const latest=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='patches');
    assert.ok(latest.requestId>first.requestId);assert.deepEqual(latest.addresses,[b]);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('water refresh can replace a resident patch using its old slots',()=>{
  const h=begin(2),a=address(0,h.n),key=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x}/${a.y}`;
  try{
    const first=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,first,[patch(a)]);h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.slots.size,1);assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true);
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,10,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const configure=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='configure');assert.equal(configure.generation,h.generation+1);
    h.worker.emit({type:'ready',generation:configure.generation,context:contextB,n:h.n});
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,10,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const selection=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='select');
    h.worker.emit({type:'selection',generation:configure.generation,requestId:selection.requestId,context:contextB,
      packed:Float64Array.from([a.face,a.n,a.x,a.y]),selectionMs:0});
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,10,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const replacement=h.worker.sent.findLast(m=>m.type==='patches');
    assert.ok(replacement&&replacement.generation===configure.generation);
    h.worker.emit({type:'patches',generation:configure.generation,requestId:replacement.requestId,context:contextB,
      patches:[patch(a,{present:2,candidates:64,context:contextB})],done:true});
    h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,10,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.slots.size,2,'the old one-tree allocation plus one free slot can hold the two-tree replacement');
    assert.equal(h.host.currentPatch(key),true);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

test('origin rebasing retains positions until the replacement buffer is complete',()=>{
  const h=begin(64),a=address(0,h.n);
  try{
    const request=selectAndRequest(h,[a]);sendPatchResponse(h,request,[patch(a)]);h.host.prepare(eye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    const id=`${a.face}/${a.n}/${a.x*8}/${a.y*8}`,slot=h.host.slots.get(id);assert.equal(slot,0);
    const before=Array.from(h.host.renderer.positions.array.slice(0,4)),origin=h.host.origin.clone();
    h.host.renderer.mesh.geometry.instanceCount=3000;
    const distantEye=new THREE.Vector3(1,9000/body.radiusM,0);
    h.host.prepare(distantEye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().rebasePending,true);assert.deepEqual(Array.from(h.host.renderer.positions.array.slice(0,4)),before);
    assert.deepEqual(h.host.origin.toArray(),origin.toArray(),'origin stays put during sliced preparation');
    for(let slice=0;slice<128&&h.host.diagnostics().rebasePending;slice++){
      h.host.prepare(distantEye,ground,100,0,true,false,nearOrigin);
      if(h.host.diagnostics().rebasePending){
        assert.deepEqual(Array.from(h.host.renderer.positions.array.slice(0,4)),before);
        assert.deepEqual(h.host.origin.toArray(),origin.toArray(),'origin stays put until all replacement positions are ready');
      }
    }
    assert.equal(h.host.diagnostics().rebasePending,false);assert.notDeepEqual(h.host.origin.toArray(),origin.toArray());
    assert.ok(Math.abs(h.host.renderer.positions.getX(slot)-(h.host.bodyPositions[slot*3]-h.host.origin.x))<.01);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(h.host.renderer.positions.getY(slot)-(h.host.bodyPositions[slot*3+1]-h.host.origin.y))<.01);
  }finally{h.restore();}
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {padCapture,reduceCapture} from '../src/tree-impostor-capture.ts';

function layer(size,pixels){
  const colour=new Float32Array(size*size*4),data=new Float32Array(colour.length),classification=new Float32Array(size*size);
  for(const {x,y,rgb,coverage=1,normal=[0,1,0],depth=0,leaf=0} of pixels){
    const p=(y*size+x)*4;
    colour.set([...rgb,coverage],p);data.set([...normalize(normal),depth],p);classification[p/4]=leaf;
  }
  return {size,colour,data,classification};
}
const close=(a,b,tolerance=1e-6)=>assert.ok(Math.abs(a-b)<=tolerance,`${a} != ${b} within ${tolerance}`);
const sumCoverage=l=>l.colour.reduce((s,v,i)=>s+(i%4===3?v:0),0);

test('reduction computes area coverage, unassociated linear RGB, normal vectors, leaf fraction and frontmost covered depth',()=>{
  const source=layer(2,[
    {x:0,y:0,rgb:[.1,.3,.7],coverage:1,normal:[1,0,0],depth:-.4,leaf:0},
    {x:1,y:0,rgb:[.8,.4,.2],coverage:.5,normal:[0,0,1],depth:.7,leaf:1},
    {x:0,y:1,rgb:[.9,.1,.5],coverage:0,normal:[-1,0,0],depth:1,leaf:1},
    {x:1,y:1,rgb:[.2,.2,.2],coverage:.5,normal:[0,1,0],depth:-.2,leaf:0},
  ]);
  const reduced=reduceCapture(source,2),p=0;
  close(reduced.colour[p+3],.5);
  for(let c=0;c<3;c++)close(reduced.colour[p+c],([.1,.3,.7][c]+.5*[.8,.4,.2][c]+.5*[.2,.2,.2][c])/2);
  close(reduced.data[p+3],.7);close(reduced.classification[p/4],.25);
  const expected=normalize([1,0,0].map((v,c)=>v+.5*[0,0,1][c]+.5*[0,1,0][c]));
  for(let c=0;c<3;c++)close(reduced.data[p+c],expected[c],2e-6);
});

test('normal reduction averages Cartesian object-space vectors directly',()=>{
  const normals=[[1,0,0],[0,1,1]],source=layer(2,normals.map((normal,i)=>({x:i,y:0,rgb:[.5,.5,.5],normal}))),reduced=reduceCapture(source,2);
  const expected=normalize(normalize(normals[0]).map((v,i)=>v+normalize(normals[1])[i]));
  for(let i=0;i<3;i++)close(reduced.data[i],expected[i],2e-6);
  close(Math.hypot(...reduced.data.slice(0,3)),1,2e-6);
});

test('normals straddling an octahedral seam average toward the downward direction in Cartesian space',()=>{
  const epsilon=.001,source=layer(2,[
    {x:0,y:0,rgb:[.5,.5,.5],normal:[epsilon,-1,0]},
    {x:1,y:0,rgb:[.5,.5,.5],normal:[-epsilon,-1,0]},
  ]),reduced=reduceCapture(source,2);
  assert.ok(reduced.data[1]<-.99999,'seam-adjacent normals remain pointed down');
  close(reduced.data[0],0,1e-8);close(reduced.data[2],0,1e-8);
});

test('near-antipodal normal result uses the deepest covered source normal as deterministic fallback',()=>{
  const a=[1,0,0],b=[-1,0,0],source=layer(2,[
    {x:0,y:0,rgb:[1,0,0],normal:a,depth:-.3},
    {x:1,y:0,rgb:[0,1,0],normal:b,depth:.8},
  ]),reduced=reduceCapture(source,2);
  for(let c=0;c<3;c++)close(reduced.data[c],b[c]);
  close(reduced.data[3],.8);
});

test('frontmost depth ignores uncovered samples and empty output texels stay empty',()=>{
  const source=layer(2,[
    {x:0,y:0,rgb:[.2,.4,.6],normal:[0,1,0],depth:.25},
    {x:1,y:0,rgb:[1,1,1],coverage:0,normal:[1,0,0],depth:1},
  ]),reduced=reduceCapture(source,2);
  close(reduced.colour[3],.25);close(reduced.data[3],.25);
  const empty=reduceCapture(layer(2,[]),2);assert.deepEqual([...empty.colour],[0,0,0,0]);assert.deepEqual([...empty.data],[0,0,0,0]);assert.deepEqual([...empty.classification],[0]);
});

test('mean area coverage is conserved through a complete factor-two mip chain',()=>{
  let current=layer(8,[
    {x:0,y:0,rgb:[.1,.2,.3],coverage:.25},{x:1,y:0,rgb:[.2,.3,.4],coverage:.5},
    {x:5,y:2,rgb:[.8,.5,.2],coverage:1},{x:7,y:7,rgb:[.4,.3,.2],coverage:.75},
  ]);
  const total=sumCoverage(current)/(current.size*current.size);
  while(current.size>1){current=reduceCapture(current,2);close(sumCoverage(current)/(current.size*current.size),total,2e-7);}
});

test('padding copies nearest covered material/data without growing coverage, wrapping tiles or mutating input',()=>{
  const source=layer(5,[
    {x:0,y:2,rgb:[1,.25,.1],normal:[1,0,0],depth:-.2,leaf:0},
    {x:4,y:2,rgb:[.1,.25,1],normal:[0,0,1],depth:.4,leaf:1},
  ]),beforeColour=[...source.colour],beforeData=[...source.data],beforeClassification=[...source.classification],padded=padCapture(source,2);
  assert.notEqual(padded.colour,source.colour);assert.notEqual(padded.data,source.data);
  assert.deepEqual([...source.colour],beforeColour);assert.deepEqual([...source.data],beforeData);assert.deepEqual([...source.classification],beforeClassification);
  for(let i=0;i<source.colour.length;i+=4)close(padded.colour[i+3],source.colour[i+3]);
  const at=(x,y)=>y*5+x;
  for(const [actual,expected] of [[padded.colour.slice(at(1,2)*4,at(1,2)*4+3),[1,.25,.1]],[padded.colour.slice(at(3,2)*4,at(3,2)*4+3),[.1,.25,1]]])for(let c=0;c<3;c++)close(actual[c],expected[c]);
  const leftOnly=padCapture(layer(5,[{x:0,y:2,rgb:[.7,.6,.5],normal:[1,0,0]}]),2);
  assert.deepEqual([...leftOnly.colour.slice(at(4,2)*4,at(4,2)*4+4)],[0,0,0,0],'padding does not wrap from left edge to right edge');
  for(let c=0;c<4;c++)close(padded.data[at(1,2)*4+c],source.data[at(0,2)*4+c]);
  close(padded.classification[at(1,2)],source.classification[at(0,2)]);
});

test('invalid reduction and padding arguments are rejected',()=>{
  const valid=layer(4,[{x:1,y:1,rgb:[.2,.3,.4]}]);
  for(const factor of [0,-1,1.5,5,NaN])assert.throws(()=>reduceCapture(valid,factor),/Invalid capture reduction/);
  assert.throws(()=>reduceCapture({...valid,size:3},1),/Invalid capture reduction/);
  assert.throws(()=>reduceCapture({...valid,colour:new Float32Array(3)},1),/Invalid capture reduction/);
  for(const steps of [-1,1.5,NaN,Infinity,17])assert.throws(()=>padCapture(valid,steps),/Invalid capture padding/);
  assert.throws(()=>padCapture({...valid,data:new Float32Array(4)}),/Invalid capture padding/);
  assert.throws(()=>padCapture({...valid,classification:new Float32Array(4)}),/Invalid capture padding/);
  assert.throws(()=>padCapture({...valid,size:0}),/Invalid capture padding/);
});

function normalize(v){const n=Math.hypot(...v);return v.map(x=>x/n);}

    this.assets=new ForestTreeFamilyLibrary(this.group,(a,b,options)=>this.treeLoader(a,b,options),resident=>resident.cell.id,[B.forestNearCapacity,B.forestCapacity]);
    this.sample=terrainSampler(body);this.classify=biomeClassifier(body);
    this.habitat=forestHabitat(this.sample,this.classify,body.radiusM);
    this.meshes=[B.forestNearCapacity,B.forestCapacity].map((capacity,i)=>{
      const mesh=new THREE.InstancedMesh(treeGeometry(i===0?1:0),
        forestMaterial(true),capacity);
      const coverage=new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(capacity),1);coverage.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);mesh.geometry.setAttribute('instanceCoverage',coverage);this.coverage.push(coverage);
      mesh.geometry.setAttribute('instanceForestSeed',new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Uint16Array(capacity),1).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
      mesh.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      mesh.instanceColor=new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(capacity*3).fill(1),3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
      mesh.frustumCulled=false;mesh.count=0;
      mesh.castShadow=true;mesh.receiveShadow=true;mesh.customDepthMaterial=forestDepthMaterial();
      this.group.add(mesh);return mesh;
    });
    this.canopy=new THREE.InstancedMesh(treeGeometry(0),forestMaterial(true),B.canopyCapacity);
    this.canopyCoverage=new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(B.canopyCapacity),1);this.canopyCoverage.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);this.canopy.geometry.setAttribute('instanceCoverage',this.canopyCoverage);
    this.canopy.geometry.setAttribute('instanceForestSeed',new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Uint16Array(B.canopyCapacity),1).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage));
    this.canopy.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);this.canopy.frustumCulled=false;this.canopy.count=0;
    this.canopy.instanceColor=new THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute(new Float32Array(B.canopyCapacity*3).fill(1),3).setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
    // Initial bufferData uploads the complete allocation. Drop its consumed
    // ranges as well as later partial uploads, so a first sparse edit does not
    // resend initialization ranges. Hidden batches retain pending work.
    for(const mesh of [...this.meshes,this.canopy])for(const attribute of [
      mesh.instanceMatrix,mesh.instanceColor!,
      mesh.geometry.getAttribute('instanceCoverage') as THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute,
      mesh.geometry.getAttribute('instanceForestSeed') as THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute,
    ])attribute.onUpload(()=>attribute.clearUpdateRanges());
    this.group.add(this.canopy);this.group.name='Continuous planetary forest';
    if(typeof Worker!=='undefined'){this.fullCanopy=new PlanetCanopy(body);this.group.add(this.fullCanopy.group);this.startWorker();}
  }
  private setWorkSelection(cells:readonly PopulationCell[]){
    this.workIndex.setSelection(cells);
    this.workStateVersion++;
    this.workSelectionRevision++;
    this.broadphaseSelectionReady=Number.isFinite(this.water);
    if(!this.broadphaseSelectionReady)return;
    this.patchBroadphase.setSelection(cells,this.water,cell=>{
      const resident=this.cache.get(cell.id);
      return resident&&resident.water===this.water?{groundHeightM:resident.height,water:resident.water}:null;
    });
  }
  /** Explicit local integration seam. Loading never changes canonical cells.
   * The previous renderer remains active until both new batches are ready. */
  async loadTreeAssets(a:ArrayBuffer,b:ArrayBuffer,options:TreeAssetOptions={}){
    return this.loadTreeFamilies([{kind:'branching',lod0:a,lod1:b}],options);
  }
  loadTreeFamilies(inputs:readonly ForestTreeFamilyInput[],options:TreeAssetOptions={}){
    return this.assets.replace(inputs,options);
  }
  clearTreeAssets(){
    this.assets.clear();
    this.assetCounts=[0,0];
  }
  private detailedSamples(){
    const samples=[];
    for(const entry of this.assets.entries.values())for(let batch=0;batch<2;batch++){
      if(!this.group.visible||!entry.batches[batch].group.visible)continue;
      let count=0;
      // Diagnostics are read by the live HUD. Bound both scans and output,
      // rather than allocating/filtering every resident on every read.
      const slots=entry.pools[batch].states;
      for(let index=0;index<Math.min(slots.length,64)&&count<8;index++){
        const slot=slots[index],heightM=slot.resident.cell.heightM;
        if(heightM<10||heightM>22)continue;
        const binding=this.binding(slot.resident);if(!binding)continue;
        samples.push({batch,family:entry.kind,id:slot.resident.cell.id,speciesId:binding.recipe.speciesId,
          recipe:binding.recipe.sourceFingerprint,heightM,...binding.appearance});count++;
      }
    }
    return samples;
  }
  private setCatalogue(species:readonly Species[]|undefined){
    if(species===this.catalogueInput)return;
    this.catalogueInput=species;
    let next:ReturnType<typeof forestTreeCatalogue>;
    try{next=forestTreeCatalogue(species??[]);this.catalogueError='';}
    catch(error){
      // Reject malformed presentation data without interrupting the canonical
      // forest or retrying the same immutable dated snapshot every frame.
      this.catalogueError=String(error);next=forestTreeCatalogue([]);
    }
    if(next.signature===this.catalogue.signature)return;
    this.catalogue=next;this.catalogueVersion++;this.bindings=new WeakMap();
  }
  private binding(resident:Resident){
    if(!this.bindings.has(resident))this.bindings.set(resident,this.catalogue.resolve(resident.cell.id));
    return this.bindings.get(resident)??null;
  }
  private syncCanopyPresentation(){
    const key=`${FOREST_PRESENTATION_VERSION}/${this.catalogueVersion}/${this.assets.revision}/${Number(this.assetDraw)}`;
    if(key===this.canopyPresentationKey)return;
    const families=new Map<ForestTreeKind,ForestFamilyPresentation>();
    if(this.assetDraw)for(const [kind,entry] of this.assets.entries){
      const presentation=entry.family.canopyPresentation;
      if(presentation?.enabled)families.set(kind,presentation);
    }
    const catalogue=this.catalogue;
    this.fullCanopy?.setPresentation?.(key,(id,heightM)=>{
      if(!families.size)return 0;
      const binding=catalogue.resolve(id),kind=forestPresentationFamily(binding,heightM,families);
      return kind&&binding?packForestPresentation(kind,binding.appearance.barkBrightness,binding.appearance.foliageBrightness):0;
    },families);
    this.presentedFamilies=families;
    this.canopyPresentationKey=key;
  }
  private expectedCanopyPresentation(resident:Resident){
    if(!this.assetDraw)return 0;
    const binding=this.binding(resident),kind=forestPresentationFamily(binding,resident.cell.heightM,this.presentedFamilies);
    return kind&&binding?packForestPresentation(kind,binding.appearance.barkBrightness,binding.appearance.foliageBrightness):0;
  }
  private startWorker(){
    try{
      const worker=new Worker(new URL('./planet-population-worker.ts',import.meta.url),{type:'module'});this.worker=worker;
      worker.onmessage=({data})=>{
        if(this.worker!==worker||!this.selecting)return;
        if(!data||!Number.isSafeInteger(data.requestId)){this.failWorker(worker);return;}
        if(data.requestId===this.selectionRequest){
          const started=performance.now();let cells:PopulationCell[];
          try{
            if(!this.selectionDirection||!Number.isFinite(data.selectionMs)||data.selectionMs<0||
              !Number.isFinite(data.packMs)||data.packMs<0)throw new Error('Invalid forest response metadata');
            cells=unpackForestSelection(data.packed);
          }catch{this.failWorker(worker);return;}
          this.selectionTransfer={bytes:data.packed.buffer.byteLength,cells:cells.length,packMs:data.packMs,decodeMs:performance.now()-started};
          // The main thread owns the requested centre; response metadata
          // cannot move it, and duplicate completed replies are ignored.
          // Application errors must surface as main-thread bugs, not use up
          // a worker restart or be silently converted into a fallback.
          this.selectionCacheStats=data.cache??null;this.applySelection(this.selectionDirection!,cells,data.selectionMs);
        }
      };
      worker.onerror=event=>{event.preventDefault();this.failWorker(worker);};
      worker.onmessageerror=()=>this.failWorker(worker);
    }catch{this.worker=null;this.selecting=false;this.workerFailures++;}
  }
  private failWorker(worker:Worker){
    if(this.worker!==worker)return;
    worker.terminate();this.worker=null;this.selectionCacheStats=null;this.selectionTransfer=null;this.selectionDirection=null;this.selecting=false;this.workerFailures++;this.selectionRequest++;
    if(this.workerRestarts<1){this.workerRestarts++;this.startWorker();}
  }
  /** Slots belong to resident IDs, not their current visibility/sort ordinal. */
  private writeInstance(batch:number,index:number,resident:Resident,coverage:number){
    const mesh=batch===2?this.canopy:this.meshes[batch],updates=this.updates[batch];
    const changed=updates.residents[index]!==resident;
    if(changed||updates.originVersions[index]!==this.originVersion){
      const {cell,height}=resident,r=this.body.radiusM;
      this.direction.set(...cell.direction);
      this.position.copy(this.direction).multiplyScalar(1+height/r).sub(this.origin);
      treeQuaternion(this.direction,cell.yaw,this.quaternion,this.yaw);
      this.scale.setScalar(cell.heightM/r);
      this.matrix.compose(this.position,this.quaternion,this.scale);mesh.setMatrixAt(index,this.matrix);
      updates.matrix.include(index,16);updates.originVersions[index]=this.originVersion;this.matrixWrites++;
    }
    if(changed){
      const roll=resident.cell.roll;
      this.color.setRGB(.8+roll*.2,.85+roll*.15,.75+roll*.25);mesh.setColorAt(index,this.color);
      updates.color.include(index,3);updates.residents[index]=resident;this.colorWrites++;
      const seed=mesh.geometry.getAttribute('instanceForestSeed') as THREE.InstancedBufferAttribute;seed.setX(index,Math.min(255,Math.floor(roll*256)));updates.seed.include(index,1);
    }
    const detailed=batch<2&&!!this.detailEntry(resident);
    const attribute=batch===2?this.canopyCoverage:this.coverage[batch],value=Math.fround(detailed?0:coverage);
    if(attribute.getX(index)!==value){attribute.setX(index,value);updates.coverage.include(index,1);this.coverageWrites++;}
  }
  private detailEntry(resident:Resident){
    if(!this.assetDraw)return undefined;
    const kind=forestPresentationFamily(this.binding(resident),resident.cell.heightM,this.presentedFamilies);
    return kind?this.assets.entries.get(kind):undefined;
  }
  private writeDetailed(entry:ForestTreeFamilyEntry<Resident,AssetSlot>,lod:number,index:number,resident:Resident,previous:AssetSlot|undefined):AssetSlot{
    const asset=entry.batches[lod],binding=this.binding(resident)!,cell=resident.cell;
    let state=previous;
    if(!previous||previous.resident!==resident||previous.originVersion!==this.originVersion||previous.catalogueVersion!==this.catalogueVersion){
      this.direction.set(...cell.direction);this.position.copy(this.direction).multiplyScalar(1+resident.height/this.body.radiusM).sub(this.origin);
      treeQuaternion(this.direction,cell.yaw,this.quaternion,this.yaw);
      this.scale.setScalar(1/this.body.radiusM);this.matrix.compose(this.position,this.quaternion,this.scale);
      asset.setInstance(index,this.matrix,cell.heightM,binding.appearance.windPhase01*2*Math.PI);
      const bark=binding.appearance.barkBrightness,leaf=binding.appearance.foliageBrightness;
      asset.setAppearance(index,[bark,bark,bark],[leaf,leaf,leaf]);
      state={resident,originVersion:this.originVersion,catalogueVersion:this.catalogueVersion};
    }
    asset.setCoverage(index,entry.coverage[lod].get(cell.id)!,Math.min(255,Math.floor(cell.roll*256)));
    return state!;
  }
  private wantInstance(batch:number,resident:Resident,coverage:number){
    coverage=quantizeForestCoverage(coverage)/256;
    const updates=this.updates[batch];updates.wanted.push(resident);updates.wantedCoverage.push(coverage);updates.wantedIds.add(resident.cell.id);
    this.fullCanopy?.own(resident.cell.id,coverage);
  }
  private reconcileBatch(batch:number){
    const updates=this.updates[batch],mesh=batch===2?this.canopy:this.meshes[batch];
    const coverage=batch===2?this.canopyCoverage:this.coverage[batch];
    if(batch<2){
      for(const entry of this.assets.entries.values()){entry.wanted[batch].length=0;entry.coverage[batch].clear();}
      for(let i=0;i<updates.wanted.length;i++){
        const resident=updates.wanted[i],entry=this.detailEntry(resident);if(!entry)continue;
        entry.wanted[batch].push(resident);entry.coverage[batch].set(resident.cell.id,updates.wantedCoverage[i]);
      }
      for(const entry of this.assets.entries.values()){
        const count=entry.pools[batch].reconcile(entry.wanted[batch],(index,resident,previous)=>this.writeDetailed(entry,batch,index,resident,previous));
        entry.batches[batch].setCount(count);entry.batches[batch].group.visible=count>0;
        this.assetCounts[batch]+=count;
      }
    }
    // A departure moves only the final resident into the hole. Reverse traversal
    // ensures that moved residents have already been checked for retention.
    for(let index=updates.residents.length-1;index>=0;index--){
      const resident=updates.residents[index];if(updates.wantedIds.has(resident.cell.id))continue;
      updates.slots.delete(resident.cell.id);
      const last=updates.residents.length-1;
      if(index!==last){
        const moved=updates.residents[last];this.writeInstance(batch,index,moved,coverage.getX(last));
        updates.slots.set(moved.cell.id,index);
      }
      updates.residents.pop();updates.originVersions.pop();
    }
    for(let i=0;i<updates.wanted.length;i++){
      const resident=updates.wanted[i];let index=updates.slots.get(resident.cell.id);
      if(index===undefined){index=updates.residents.length;updates.slots.set(resident.cell.id,index);}
      this.writeInstance(batch,index,resident,updates.wantedCoverage[i]);
    }
    mesh.count=updates.residents.length;
    if(batch<2&&this.assetCounts[batch]===mesh.count)mesh.visible=false;
    this.scheduledUploadBytes+=updates.matrix.flush(mesh.instanceMatrix);
    if(mesh.instanceColor)this.scheduledUploadBytes+=updates.color.flush(mesh.instanceColor);
    this.scheduledUploadBytes+=updates.coverage.flush(coverage);
    this.scheduledUploadBytes+=updates.seed.flush(mesh.geometry.getAttribute('instanceForestSeed') as THREE.BufferAttribute);
  }
  update(eye:THREE.Vector3,groundDirection:V3,altitudeM:number,enabled:boolean,water:number,immersive:boolean,frusta:THREE.Frustum[]=[],worldNs=0n,treeSpecies?:readonly Species[],shadowFrustum:THREE.Frustum|null=null){
    if(!Number.isFinite(water))throw new Error('Forest water level must be finite');
    if(this.assets.entries.size)this.setCatalogue(treeSpecies);
    this.worldDate=worldNs;this.assets.setDate(worldNs);
    this.assetDraw=this.assets.entries.size>0&&!immersive;this.assetCounts=[0,0];
    this.syncCanopyPresentation();
    for(const entry of this.assets.entries.values())for(const batch of entry.batches)batch.group.visible=false;
    const updateStarted=performance.now();
    this.group.visible=enabled&&altitudeM<B.forestStreamAltitudeM;
    this.visibleTrees=0;this.nearTrees=0;this.placementMs=0;this.selectionMs=0;
    this.matrixWrites=0;this.colorWrites=0;this.coverageWrites=0;this.scheduledUploadBytes=0;this.originRebased=false;this.updateMs=0;this.farOwnedSkipped=0;
    if(!this.group.visible){if(this.fullCanopy)this.fullCanopy.group.visible=false;return;}
    const draw=altitudeM<B.forestPrepareAltitudeM;
    for(const mesh of this.meshes)mesh.visible=draw;this.canopy.visible=draw;
    if(!draw){
      this.fullCanopy?.prepare(eye,groundDirection,altitudeM,water,enabled,immersive,this.origin);
      this.fullCanopy?.finish();this.updateMs=performance.now()-updateStarted;return;
    }
    if(immersive!==this.immersiveMaterial){
      this.immersiveMaterial=immersive;
      for(const mesh of [...this.meshes,this.canopy]){const material=mesh.material as THREE.MeshLambertMaterial;material.alphaHash=immersive;material.needsUpdate=true;}
    }
    const hydrologyChanged=this.sampledRivers!==this.body.canonicalRivers||this.sampledHydrology!==this.body.hydrology;
    if(water!==this.water||hydrologyChanged){
      this.sampledRivers=this.body.canonicalRivers;this.sampledHydrology=this.body.hydrology;
      if(hydrologyChanged)for(const resident of this.cache.values())resident.water=NaN;
      this.patchBroadphase.setWater(water);if(hydrologyChanged)this.patchBroadphase.invalidate();
      this.workStateVersion++;
      this.workHabitatRevision++;
      this.workGlobalHabitatRevision++;
      this.water=water;this.sample=terrainSampler(this.body,undefined,undefined,undefined,water);
      this.classify=biomeClassifier(this.body,water);
      this.habitat=forestHabitat(this.sample,this.classify,this.body.radiusM);
      // Selection depends only on identity/location, not water. Keep the single
      // in-flight job and resident trees while bounded habitat refresh replaces
      // them. Never queue another expensive selection on each playback bucket.
      this.queue=this.wanted.filter(cell=>this.cache.get(cell.id)?.water!==water);
    }
    const r=this.body.radiusM,ground=this.direction.set(...groundDirection);
    if(ground.distanceTo(this.center)*r>40&&!this.selecting){
      if(this.worker){
        const worker=this.worker;this.selecting=true;this.selectionDirection=[...groundDirection];
        try{worker.postMessage({requestId:++this.selectionRequest,seed:this.body.seed,direction:groundDirection,radiusM:r});}
        catch{this.failWorker(worker);}
      }else{
      const started=performance.now();
      const cells=forestPopulationCells(this.body.seed,groundDirection,r,B.forestFootprintM+100,B.canopyFootprintM,B.forestCellM);
      this.applySelection(groundDirection,cells,performance.now()-started);
      }
    }
    const started=performance.now();
    for(let count=0;this.queue.length&&count<B.placementSamplesPerFrame&&performance.now()-started<B.placementBudgetMs;count++){
      const cell=this.queue.shift()!,habitat=this.habitat(cell);
      this.cache.set(cell.id,{cell,height:habitat.groundHeightM,present:habitat.present,water});
      this.patchBroadphase.updateResident(cell.id,{groundHeightM:habitat.groundHeightM,water});
      this.workStateVersion++;
      this.workHabitatRevision++;
    }
    this.placementMs=performance.now()-started;
    // Retain the anchor across ordinary movement so static tree matrices need
    // no rewrite. Rebase before Float32 conversion to preserve local precision.
    if(!this.originReady||this.origin.distanceTo(eye)*r>=B.forestOriginRebaseM){
      this.origin.copy(eye);this.group.position.copy(this.origin);this.originReady=true;
      this.originVersion++;this.originRebased=true;
    }
    this.fullCanopy?.prepare(eye,groundDirection,altitudeM,water,enabled,immersive,this.origin);
    const selectionMismatch=!this.broadphaseSelectionReady||this.workIndex.diagnostics().selectionCells!==this.wanted.length;
    if(selectionMismatch){
      this.patchBroadphase.setWater(water);this.setWorkSelection(this.wanted);
    }
    const eyeMeters=this.position.set(eye.x*r,eye.y*r,eye.z*r);
    const legacyCanopyRevision=this.fullCanopy?(this.fullCanopy.currentPatchRevision?.()??NaN):-1;
    const canopyChanges=this.fullCanopy?.currentPatchChanges?.()??{globalRevision:legacyCanopyRevision,revision:legacyCanopyRevision,keys:[] as [string,number][]};
    const moved=this.workReferenceEyeReady&&eyeMeters.distanceToSquared(this.workReferenceEye)>20*20;
    const initial=!this.workReferenceEyeReady;
    const selectionChanged=this.workSelectionRevision!==this.workBuiltSelectionRevision;
    const habitatChanged=this.workGlobalHabitatRevision!==this.workBuiltGlobalHabitatRevision;
    const canopyChanged=canopyChanges.globalRevision!==this.workBuiltCanopyEpoch;
    const fullRefresh=initial||selectionMismatch||selectionChanged||habitatChanged||canopyChanged||moved;
    let workIndices=this.workIndex.currentIndices();
    const canopyDirtyKeys=canopyChanges.keys.map(([key])=>key);
    const habitatDirtyKeys=this.patchBroadphase.dirtyGroups();
    if(fullRefresh){
      if(initial)this.workRefreshReasons.initial++;
      if(selectionChanged)this.workRefreshReasons.selection++;
      if(habitatChanged)this.workRefreshReasons.habitat++;
      if(canopyChanged)this.workRefreshReasons.canopy++;
      if(moved)this.workRefreshReasons.movement++;
      if(selectionMismatch)this.workRefreshReasons.selectionMismatch++;
      const skippedPatches=this.fullCanopy?.group.visible?this.patchBroadphase.skipPatchKeys(
        [eyeMeters.x,eyeMeters.y,eyeMeters.z],water,key=>this.fullCanopy?.currentPatch(key)??false,B.forestFootprintM+20):new Set<string>();
      workIndices=this.workIndex.updateSkipped(skippedPatches);
      for(const id of this.workIndex.newlySkippedIds()){this.retainedNear.delete(id);this.retainedMiddle.delete(id);}
      this.workReferenceEye.copy(eyeMeters);this.workReferenceEyeReady=true;
      this.workCanopyRevision=canopyChanges.revision;this.workBuiltCanopyEpoch=canopyChanges.globalRevision;
      this.workListRefreshes++;
      this.workBuiltSelectionRevision=this.workSelectionRevision;this.workBuiltHabitatRevision=this.workHabitatRevision;
      this.workBuiltGlobalHabitatRevision=this.workGlobalHabitatRevision;this.workBuiltStateVersion=this.workStateVersion;
      this.patchBroadphase.acknowledgeDirtyGroups(habitatDirtyKeys);
      this.fullCanopy?.acknowledgeCurrentPatchChanges?.(canopyChanges);
    }else{
      const dirtyKeys=[...new Set([...canopyDirtyKeys,...habitatDirtyKeys])];
      if(dirtyKeys.length){
        const states=this.patchBroadphase.skipStatesFor(dirtyKeys,
          [this.workReferenceEye.x,this.workReferenceEye.y,this.workReferenceEye.z],water,
          key=>this.fullCanopy?.currentPatch(key)??false,B.forestFootprintM+20);
        workIndices=this.workIndex.updateSkippedPatches(states);
        for(const id of this.workIndex.newlySkippedIds()){this.retainedNear.delete(id);this.retainedMiddle.delete(id);}
        this.workRefreshReasons.incrementalPatch++;
        this.workCanopyRevision=canopyChanges.revision;
        this.workBuiltStateVersion=this.workStateVersion;
        this.workBuiltHabitatRevision=this.workHabitatRevision;
        this.patchBroadphase.acknowledgeDirtyGroups(habitatDirtyKeys);
        this.fullCanopy?.acknowledgeCurrentPatchChanges?.(canopyChanges);
      }
    }
    const workStats=this.workIndex.diagnostics(),patchStats=this.patchBroadphase.diagnostics();
    this.farWorkDiagnostics={...workStats,patchGroups:patchStats.groups,patchLastFullScan:patchStats.lastFullScan,
      patchVisitedGroups:patchStats.lastVisitedGroups,patchVisitedGroupsTotal:patchStats.totalVisitedGroups,
      patchFullScans:patchStats.fullScans,patchIncrementalScans:patchStats.incrementalScans,
      skippedCells:workStats.skippedCells,activeCells:workStats.activeCells,refreshes:this.workListRefreshes,
      refreshReasons:this.workRefreshReasons,selectionRevision:this.workSelectionRevision,habitatRevision:this.workHabitatRevision,
      builtSelectionRevision:this.workBuiltSelectionRevision,builtHabitatRevision:this.workBuiltHabitatRevision,
      globalHabitatRevision:this.workGlobalHabitatRevision,builtGlobalHabitatRevision:this.workBuiltGlobalHabitatRevision,
      canopyRevision:this.fullCanopy?.currentPatchRevision?.()??-1,canopyEpoch:canopyChanges.globalRevision,workStateRevision:this.workStateVersion,builtWorkStateRevision:this.workBuiltStateVersion};
    this.farOwnedSkipped=workStats.skippedCells;
    const counts=[0,0],capacities=immersive?[64,1024]:[B.forestNearCapacity,B.forestCapacity];let canopyCount=0;
    for(const updates of this.updates){updates.wanted.length=0;updates.wantedCoverage.length=0;updates.wantedIds.clear();}
    const altitudeFade=1-THREE.MathUtils.smoothstep(altitudeM,B.forestPrepareAltitudeM-500,B.forestPrepareAltitudeM);
    for(const index of workIndices){
      const cell=this.wanted[index];if(!cell)continue;
      const dx=cell.direction[0]-groundDirection[0],dy=cell.direction[1]-groundDirection[1],dz=cell.direction[2]-groundDirection[2];
      if((dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz)*r*r<100*100)this.fullCanopy?.observeClose(cell.id,eye);
      const resident=this.cache.get(cell.id);if(!resident)continue;
      resident.patchKey??=forestCellPatchKey(cell.id);
      if(resident.water===water&&this.fullCanopy?.currentPatch(resident.patchKey)){
        const scale=1+resident.height/r,x=cell.direction[0]*scale-eye.x,y=cell.direction[1]*scale-eye.y,z=cell.direction[2]*scale-eye.z;
        if((x*x+y*y+z*z)*r*r>=650*650){
          // Complete current patches own this entire distant band. Keep stale
          // water and missing patches on the full fallback/absence path below.
          this.retainedNear.delete(cell.id);this.retainedMiddle.delete(cell.id);this.farOwnedSkipped++;continue;
        }
      }
      this.fullCanopy?.noteNear(cell.id,resident.patchKey,resident.present,resident.water);
      if(!resident.present)continue;
      // Hide newly submerged old residents immediately while other habitat
      // properties are refreshed. The current bucket sampler remains authority.
      if(resident.water!==water&&this.body.water>0&&resident.height<=water){this.fullCanopy?.own(cell.id,1);continue;}
      this.direction.set(...cell.direction);
      this.position.copy(this.direction).multiplyScalar(1+resident.height/r);
      this.bounds.center.copy(this.position).addScaledVector(this.direction,LEGACY_TREE_BOUND.center[1]*cell.heightM/r);this.bounds.radius=crownBoundRadius*cell.heightM/r;
      if(this.assetDraw){
        this.bounds.center.copy(this.position);
        this.bounds.radius=Math.max((Math.hypot(...LEGACY_TREE_BOUND.center)+crownBoundRadius)*cell.heightM,this.assets.maxRadiusM)/r;
      }
      if(frusta.length&&!frusta.some(f=>f.intersectsSphere(this.bounds))&&!shadowFrustum?.intersectsSphere(this.bounds))continue;
      const distance=this.position.distanceTo(eye)*r;
      const horizontal=Math.hypot(this.direction.x-groundDirection[0],this.direction.y-groundDirection[1],this.direction.z-groundDirection[2])*r;
      // Detailed and distant draws refer to one canonical tree. A retained
      // band prevents oscillation between equal-silhouette batches.
      const wasNear=this.retainedNear.has(cell.id),nearLimit=wasNear?B.forestNearM+12:B.forestNearM-12;
      let lod=distance<nearLimit?0:1;
      if(lod===0)this.retainedNear.add(cell.id);else this.retainedNear.delete(cell.id);
      if(distance>=B.forestRadiusM)continue;
      const edgeCoverage=altitudeFade*(1-THREE.MathUtils.smoothstep(distance,B.forestRadiusM-300,B.forestRadiusM));
      const paired=this.fullCanopy?.canHandoff(cell.id,resident.height,resident.patchKey,cell.heightM,this.expectedCanopyPresentation(resident))??false;
      if(paired){
        const middleLimit=this.retainedMiddle.has(cell.id)?600:550;
        if(distance>=middleLimit){this.retainedMiddle.delete(cell.id);continue;}
        this.retainedMiddle.add(cell.id);
      }
      if(cell.canopyRepresentative&&horizontal>B.forestFootprintM-100){
        if(canopyCount>=(immersive?2048:B.canopyCapacity))continue;
        this.wantInstance(2,resident,edgeCoverage);canopyCount++;continue;
      }
      if(counts[lod]>=capacities[lod]){if(lod===0)lod=1;else continue;}
      if(counts[lod]>=capacities[lod])continue;
      const fade=edgeCoverage*(paired||cell.canopyRepresentative?1:1-THREE.MathUtils.smoothstep(horizontal,B.forestFootprintM-130,B.forestFootprintM));
      if(fade<=0)continue;
      this.wantInstance(lod,resident,fade);counts[lod]++;
    }
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++)this.reconcileBatch(i);
    this.fullCanopy?.finish();
    this.nearTrees=counts[0];this.visibleTrees=counts[0]+counts[1]+canopyCount;
    this.updateMs=performance.now()-updateStarted;
  }
  diagnostics(){return {visible:this.visibleTrees,near:this.nearTrees,cached:this.cache.size,pending:this.queue.length,
    detailedTrees:{ready:this.assets.entries.size>0,loading:this.assets.loading,error:this.assets.error||this.catalogueError,visible:[...this.assetCounts],species:this.catalogue.count,dateNs:this.worldDate.toString(),catalogueVersion:this.catalogueVersion,
      heightPolicy:'canonical-population-proxy',geometryVariants:this.assets.entries.size,farRepresentation:'family-profile-with-legacy-fallback',presentationKey:this.canopyPresentationKey,instanceSlots:'compact-per-family',
      families:[...this.assets.entries.values()].map(entry=>({kind:entry.kind,
        submitted:entry.pools.map((pool,lod)=>this.group.visible&&entry.batches[lod].group.visible?pool.items.length:0),
        retainedSlots:entry.pools.map(pool=>pool.items.length),capacities:entry.pools.map(pool=>pool.capacity),foliageMipmaps:entry.family.foliageMipmaps??null})),
      foliageMipmaps:this.assets.entries.get('branching')?.family.foliageMipmaps??null,samples:this.detailedSamples(),
      handoffSamples:this.detailedSamples().map(sample=>({id:sample.id,far:this.fullCanopy?.presentationSample?.(sample.id)??null}))},
    candidateCells:this.wanted.length,canopyStands:0,farCanonicalTrees:this.group.visible?this.canopy.count:0,farSamplingFraction:FAR_CROWN_PROBABILITY,habitatVersion:TREE_HABITAT_VERSION,placementMs:this.placementMs,selectionMs:this.selectionMs,selectionPending:this.selecting,selectionWorker:!!this.worker,
    representation:'canonical-tree-field',densityFocus:'observer-ground',instanceUpdates:'stable-slots-retained-origin-dirty-ranges',
    selectionJobsInFlight:this.selecting?1:0,waterBucket:this.water,selectionApplyMs:this.selectionApplyMs,selectionCache:this.selectionCacheStats,selectionTransfer:this.selectionTransfer,fullCanopy:this.fullCanopy?.diagnostics()??null,
    workerRestarts:this.workerRestarts,workerFailures:this.workerFailures,selectionMode:this.worker?'worker':'synchronous-fallback',
    updateMs:this.updateMs,farOwnedSkipped:this.farOwnedSkipped,farWork:this.farWorkDiagnostics,matrixWrites:this.matrixWrites,colorWrites:this.colorWrites,coverageWrites:this.coverageWrites,
    scheduledUploadBytes:this.scheduledUploadBytes,originRebased:this.originRebased,originVersion:this.originVersion,originRebaseM:B.forestOriginRebaseM,
    radiusM:B.forestRadiusM,heightRangeM:[10,22],cellSizeM:B.forestCellM,
    allocatedBytes:[...this.meshes,this.canopy].reduce((s,m)=>s+m.instanceMatrix.array.byteLength+(m.instanceColor?.array.byteLength??0)+
      Object.values(m.geometry.attributes).reduce((n,a)=>n+a.array.byteLength,0),0)*2};}
  dispose(){if(this.disposed)return;this.disposed=true;this.assets.dispose();this.worker?.terminate();this.worker=null;this.fullCanopy?.dispose();this.fullCanopy=null;for(const mesh of [...this.meshes,this.canopy]){mesh.geometry.dispose();(mesh.material as THREE.Material).dispose();mesh.customDepthMaterial?.dispose();}this.cache.clear();this.group.removeFromParent();}
}


SUPPLEMENT tests/planet-canopy-invalidation.test.mjs bytes=2466 sha256=7681d459802753411775611854a42eb9c5304eb3e76e14f9e6bd2b9e41c18e02
import {test,after} from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {createServer} from 'vite';

const vite=await createServer({configFile:false,optimizeDeps:{noDiscovery:true},server:{middlewareMode:true,hmr:false,ws:false},appType:'custom'});
const {PlanetCanopy}=await vite.ssrLoadModule('/src/planet-canopy.ts');
const {galaxies,refByAddress,system}=await vite.ssrLoadModule('/src/catalog.ts');
const body=system(refByAddress(galaxies('LETHRA-001')[0],'G001/C2128,0,1/0')).planets[1];
after(()=>vite.close());

test('patch invalidation snapshots acknowledge only versions actually consumed',()=>{
  const canopy=new PlanetCanopy(body);
  try{
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers('0/128/0/0');
    const first=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    assert.deepEqual(first.keys.map(([key])=>key),['0/128/0/0']);
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers('0/128/0/0');
    canopy.acknowledgeCurrentPatchChanges(first);
    const newer=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    assert.equal(newer.keys.length,1,'a later edit of the same key survives the older acknowledgement');
    assert.ok(newer.keys[0][1]>first.keys[0][1]);
    canopy.acknowledgeCurrentPatchChanges(newer);
    assert.deepEqual(canopy.currentPatchChanges().keys,[]);
  }finally{canopy.dispose();}
});

test('global invalidation advances the epoch and supersedes pending local keys',()=>{
  const canopy=new PlanetCanopy(body);
  try{
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers('0/128/0/0');
    const old=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers();
    canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers('1/128/0/0');
    canopy.acknowledgeCurrentPatchChanges(old);
    const current=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    assert.ok(current.globalRevision>old.globalRevision);
    assert.deepEqual(current.keys.map(([key])=>key),['1/128/0/0']);
  }finally{canopy.dispose();}
});

test('an unconsumed local-key journal is bounded by escalating to a full-reset epoch',()=>{
  const canopy=new PlanetCanopy(body);
  try{
    const initial=canopy.currentPatchChanges().globalRevision;
    for(let i=0;i<8193;i++)canopy.invalidateCurrentPatchAnswers(`0/128/${i}/0`);
    const overflow=canopy.currentPatchChanges();
    assert.ok(overflow.globalRevision>initial,'overflow requests a safe global refresh');
    assert.equal(overflow.keys.length,0,'the global reset replaces an unbounded per-key backlog');
    assert.equal(overflow.revision,8193);
  }finally{canopy.dispose();}
});

DOC docs/captured-family-relighting-2026-09-28.md bytes=4122 sha256=8684645062e3aac6416b2626902aa34d808f470f6eacf8c18f9026ef63269b6b
# Per-view relighting for captured Oak and Pine

Date: 2026-09-28. Isolated RTX 5090 lab comparison; no game integration or
visual approval.

## Result

The opt-in `captureRelightViews` branch evaluates Three.js's installed physical
lighting chunks separately for each of the three blended capture views, then
blends the resulting direct and indirect diffuse/specular terms. The baseline
single-pass shading remains the default. The report used the runtime's
source-hash-bound foliage sidecars, actual Oak and Pine LOD1, and 16 m capture
families (81 views at 64 px; 7,077,456 bytes of explicit mip data per family).

At the intermediate `[1, 0.3, 1]` direction, the report's alpha-weighted mean
probe-RGB luminance error improved, but remained outside the 10% comparison
gate:

| Family | Existing single-pass | Per-view relighting | Median / p95 view-depth error |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Oak | −35.94% | −21.00% | 1.163 m / 3.216 m |
| Pine | −38.03% | −24.95% | 1.177 m / 2.720 m |

The FloatType probe render targets use Three.js's `LinearSRGBColorSpace` and
`NoToneMapping`. The alpha-weighted luminance values are therefore an
untone-mapped, linear-RGB image-space comparison in arbitrary renderer units,
not calibrated radiometric data. ACES Filmic and exposure 1.2 apply to the
on-screen screenshots only. The lighting toggle does not alter reconstructed
depth: the depth figures match the prior sidecar run. Coverage also remains
unchanged by the lighting option. At the 33 px
intermediate probe, the weighted capture was −6.08% area / 0.874 blurred IoU
for Oak and −6.24% / 0.848 for Pine.
The mesh reference's 528-to-1056 px covered-area change is below 0.7% for Oak
and 3.3–3.6% for Pine; this supports the area comparison but does not make the
rendered mesh a perfect radiometric or per-ray truth reference.

The source mesh's alpha-hash comparison is species-dependent. At 33 px it
brings Oak's area to roughly +2.4–2.8% of the reference, compared with MASK at
−27.2%. Pine instead overfills by roughly +76–78%, compared with MASK at
−49.6%. These are single-frame probes without temporal accumulation; HASH is
not a general fix for both families.

## Metric interpretation and visual limits

The depth probe compares 33 px capture view-depth against a 1056 px source-mesh
render reduced to 33 px. Each source pixel's depth is coverage-weighted across
its 32×32 supersamples; each capture depth is the continuous blend of the
selected view depths. Median and p95 absolute errors are computed only where
both outputs have coverage above 0.1. This is a useful matched image-space
comparison, not exact ray-traced surface truth.

The saved lab screenshots magnify the low-resolution capture. Oak still reads
soft and stippled; Pine is especially sparse and dark against its source mesh.
In these historical screenshots the visible dropdown still says “Legacy,”
because programmatic harness loads did not update the control. The JSON status
records the actual loaded filtering mode; the report and harness record the
`captureRelightViews` value. Use those records as the mode evidence.
The images and scores do not establish ordinary-distance appearance,
transition quality, temporal stability, forest cost, or production readiness.
The per-view branch also uses the proxy's shadow-coordinate varyings; it does
not reconstruct per-view shadow-map coordinates. No performance conclusion
was measured.

The paired [relighting report](../test-results/family-capture/sidecar-relight-v1/report.json)
and [prior sidecar baseline](../test-results/family-capture/sidecar-surface-v1/report.json)
record source hashes, probe rows, errors, and per-view enable state. Inspect the
[Oak source](../test-results/family-capture/sidecar-relight-v1/oak-coverage-weighted-mesh.png)
and [Oak capture](../test-results/family-capture/sidecar-relight-v1/oak-coverage-weighted-capture.png),
or the [Pine source](../test-results/family-capture/sidecar-relight-v1/pine-coverage-weighted-mesh.png)
and [Pine capture](../test-results/family-capture/sidecar-relight-v1/pine-coverage-weighted-capture.png).


DOC test-results/family-capture/README.md bytes=7162 sha256=d8a83bb4d976aa26b5c9c627008c0be561f6fe474511a9c22204ff944b762d68
# Actual family LOD1 capture comparison — 2026-09-28

These are isolated development measurements, not forest or visual acceptance.
Live remains `8e16776`. Source family geometry and original files are preserved.

## Inputs and capture

Root tool commit `24d81e3` accepts the existing author-7 and EZ-Tree Oak/Pine
source contracts, verifies briefs/provenance and both GLB hashes, and preserves
the original manifest plus upstream MIT notice. Runtime capture commit
`7d703ee` selects the actual simplified LOD1. The validated comparison brief is
`examples/asset-briefs/atlas-family-lod1-capture-comparison.json` in IWSDK root.

Each 16m family was captured at 81 full-sphere directions, 512px source reduced
to 64px plus independent mips. Each decoded GPU set uses 7,077,456 bytes.
`capture-audit.json` records the retained output locations, source manifest
hashes, adapter and channel checks. `audit.mjs` checks all 2,268 files per
family by length/hash, finite values, nonempty bark/leaf classifications and
base coverage sums. Both passed on RTX 5090 / ANGLE D3D11. Review files retain
NOASSERTION for original Atlas materials; MIT geometry attribution is retained.

## Runs

- `filtering-v1`: harness failed because the lab extension accidentally removed
  its existing `impostor` getter. The getter was restored. This was not a GPU
  product failure; no results were collected.
- `filtering-v2`: actual GPU A/B, no GL/page/console errors. Its 33px reference
  used only 132px source samples and was insufficiently converged.
- `filtering-v3`: 33px source reference uses 528px samples; intermediate Oak
  weighted capture gives blurred IoU 0.873 and coverage error -6.37%. Pine gives
  0.838 and -6.73%, but 264px versus 528px source coverage differs about 20%.
- `surface-v1`: metric depth and linear rendered colour measured with a 1056px
  mesh reference. Oak reference coverage changes <0.6% from 528px; Pine still
  changes 15–16%. Pine convergence is not established. This catches failures
  that silhouette-only comparisons missed.
- `surface-v2`: same inputs with coverage-weighted view depth, two-sided leaf
  orientation, and sphere tangent-cone bounds. No GL/page/console errors.
  Weighted-filter intermediate median depth error fell from 1.873m to 1.158m
  for Oak and 1.617m to 1.143m for Pine; p95 fell from 7.017m to 3.214m and
  3.828m to 2.444m respectively. Silhouettes are unaffected by this depth blend.

The primary result is **not accepted**: intermediate weighted capture luminance
is still 35.6% below the Oak mesh and 26.6% below Pine, outside the 10% gate.
Median depth still exceeds 1m. The normal orientation correction made little
difference in these directions. Coverage-weighted packing fixes transparent
attribute filtering, but it is not a general silhouette or lighting improvement.

All reports include runtime source hashes. The last run used the recorded
uncommitted renderer changes before their staged commit; the SHA label alone
does not identify those bytes. Frame pixel counts describe the entire capture
diameter, not the tree's projected height. The screenshots deliberately magnify
the 64px representation to expose artifacts; they do not represent accepted
close-range use. Root raster contact, temporal stability, full forest cost,
physical height variants and game handoff remain unverified.

## Corrected source sampling

The runs above intentionally load original GLB texture mips. They use the real
game geometry but **do not model the current game's offline foliage mips**.
That missing input was found during the comparison audit. Root `35ff152` and
Atlas `d2223c3` add explicit, source-hash-bound sidecars to both capture and
reference, with no silent fallback. `sidecar-audit` verifies the new retained
captures; `sidecar-surface-v1` repeats the hardware comparisons with the actual
runtime foliage inputs. All artifacts and GPU error checks pass.

At the intermediate 33px frame, weighted capture versus the 528px reference
gives blurred IoU 0.874 Oak / 0.848 Pine, area error -6.08% / -6.24%. The
528px→1056px source convergence error is <0.7% Oak and 3.3–3.6% Pine. Native
MASK geometry still loses 27% / 50% coverage respectively. This is relevant
to the visible transition and is not solved by a matching proxy alone.

The source correction improves Pine capture coverage and reference convergence,
but intermediate luminance is still -35.9% Oak / -38.0% Pine; median depth
errors are 1.163m / 1.177m. These captures also remain unaccepted. An opt-in
per-view PBR lighting experiment is being implemented separately; its existence
is not evidence of improvement.

`sidecar-relight-v1` subsequently tests committed `bd6d96a` with its opt-in
`captureRelightViews=1` path on RTX 5090; all four family/filter combinations
compile/render without GL/page/console errors. The installed Three PBR chunks
shade each view before coverage-weighted light blending. Intermediate weighted
luminance error improves from -35.94% to -21.00% Oak and -38.03% to -24.95%
Pine; it still fails the 10% gate. No full-game or GPU cost claim is made.

That run also tests native Three alpha hashing as a separate mesh setting,
using the current offline mip chains. Oak's 33px area error is +2.4–2.8%, with
blurred IoU 0.886–0.895 against the MASK supersampled reference. Pine instead
overfills by 76–78%, with blurred IoU about 0.555. This is not grounds for a
global foliage-mode switch. The family-specific behavior needs a coherent
source/capture coverage contract and actual-game temporal review.

`alpha-distribution.json` provides a CPU explanation for that distinction. The
current sidecars preserve a fixed MASK cutoff, not mean alpha. Pine's expected
hashed coverage / cutoff coverage grows from 1.071 at the original level to
1.901 at mip 7; Oak's mip-7 ratio is 1.051. Thus combining the current Pine
MASK correction with alpha hashing changes its intended density. Before any
family-mode switch, compare hashing with mean-coverage mips (or the original
chain) and retain a consistent source/capture sampling contract. This texture
statistic does not by itself establish the mesh's screen-space coverage.

## Reproduce

Keep rendering dev server on 5197, retained root captures present, and the GPU
free of other browser/generation jobs. Run `node test-results/family-capture/audit.mjs`
then set `ATLAS_CAPTURE_REPORT` to a **fresh** report directory and run
`node test-results/family-capture/compare.mjs`. The harness uses original MASK
source coverage and reports native mesh loss separately from supersampling.
Set `ATLAS_CAPTURE_SIDECAR=1` for the runtime-matching captures and reference.
The optional `ATLAS_CAPTURE_RELIGHT=1` requires the per-view lighting uniform;
the runner fails if that option is requested but unavailable. New runs also
report native Three alpha-hash coverage as a separate source comparison.

Next implementation must resolve intermediate-view lighting/depth and Pine
source sampling before game integration; the original solid canopy remains
visually rejected by the genuine Opus review. Do not retune a scalar brightness
to hide a directional reconstruction defect.


DOC test-results/forest-work-refresh-96997ec/README.md bytes=2202 sha256=0ae0877f39a4b46ffee9faa9e2767b0d3991cacd9364757684d263aa875c6d71
# Incremental forest-work profile, 96997ec

Built from clean commit `96997ec2114dc4ccf207fd5f53700dea8823afc7` (`dirty=false`). Browser renderer was an NVIDIA RTX 5090 through ANGLE/D3D11. The same normal navigation fixture passed: 573 basic trees, then 632 detailed trees on first attach; one controlled Oak HTTP 503 preserved the basic forest and retry succeeded; date return preserved 16/16 matched tree identities; leave/revisit reused the 20-request package cache; 16/16 near/far presentation samples matched. No unexpected browser errors; WebGL error 0.

## Refresh work observed

The 6 s stationary profile, after a 2 s prewarm, had 0 full far-work refreshes/rebuilds (start and end remained 128). It processed 79 incremental broadphase queries, visiting 879 patch groups total (about 11 per query), and 72 incremental ordered-index membership updates. Canopy revision still advanced by 879. There were no selection, habitat, movement, or full-epoch changes in the window.

For comparison, the d5c5322 profile had 135 full refreshes/rebuilds and a 1,297 increase in canopy revision, with all refreshes attributed to canopy revision. The d5 instrumentation did not count visited groups; 135 × 5,621 groups gives an inferred upper work count of about 759k group visits. In 96997ec the measured visit count is 879, with zero full scans. This is direct evidence that local canopy changes now use the dirty-patch path while global resets retain full scans.

## Timing limits

The 969 profile p50/p95 values were planetUpdate 18.9/33.9 ms, frame work 32.1/54.3 ms, and instrumented GPU pass sum 7.37/16.95 ms. The d5 profile had 30.2/46.0 ms, 45.0/66.4 ms, and 9.77/19.16 ms respectively. These timing distributions are descriptive, not a causal performance claim: the same route reached different population counts and camera positions (632 vs. 767 detailed trees on the first attach), and the profile samples are not perfectly workload-matched. The visited-group and full-scan counters are the attributable result.

Artifacts: `report.json`, `stationary-6s.cpuprofile`, `committed-build.log`, and numbered route screenshots. The d5 baseline remains preserved in `../forest-work-refresh-d5c5322/`.

Raw reports/images are preserved under C:\LLM\IWSDK\universe-atlas-rendering\test-results\opus-forest-work-capture-review; file hashes/source locations are in inputs.json. Provide prioritized concrete findings, cite source/test paths, and avoid unsupported visual or performance claims.
